任一新命令取得global lock后必须先恢复残留init再做其他transaction工作，且未到`journalCommitted`的原plan绝不resume。bootstrap-next是单列规则：base`install-init.json` absent而`.next`存在，按顺序证明尚未创建transaction directory；只要`.next`是fixed basename下regular/current uid/mode0600/nlink1/≤8KiB，就不论canonical、partial或malformed都identity-bound unlink+root fsync，不晋升、不解析为authority。base init存在时，verified `.next`也只删除重算当前stage；canonical时另校验same transaction/nonce与合法下一stage，partial/malformed不晋升。wrong leaf identity统一exit3、`JOURNAL_INVALID/status`、protectedMutation=none；trusted init存在时回显其transactionId，否则transactionId/state=null，保留证据。

init recovery的唯一后态如下；每一行完成后当前新命令才可继续：

| committed init stage | exact observed prefix | deterministic recovery |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `allocated` | target absent | identity-bound删init+root fsync |
| `allocated` | target是新建的exact empty current-uid mode0700 dir | 重验root identity后`unlinkat(...,AT_REMOVEDIR)`+root fsync，再删init+root fsync |
| `directoryCreated` | root identity匹配；base journal/journal.next absent；目录empty或只含verified regular current-uid mode0600 nlink1≤4KiB owner leaf（bytes可partial） | 若owner leaf存在则identity-bound unlink+transaction-dir fsync；关闭并重验dir后rmdir+root fsync；最后删init+root fsync。不得重写owner或journal |
| `ownerCommitted` | exact owner nonce/root/hash匹配；base journal absent；可有verified regular current-uid mode0600 nlink1≤1MiB `journal.next`（canonical/partial/malformed） | 先identity-bound删journal.next+transaction-dir fsync，再删owner+dir fsync，rmdir+root fsync，最后删init+root fsync。不得重算revision1，原plan终止 |
| `ownerCommitted` | exact owner且base journal已是revision1/collectingFacts、nonce/ownerSha匹配（crash在journal rename后、init推进前） | 先提交init=`journalCommitted`绑定revision1；再写revision2 `aborted`+`initRecoveryAbort`，提交published绑定revision2，删init+root fsync |
| `journalCommitted` | exact owner+journal；journal为0-protected `collectingFacts|awaitingOracle|complete` | 原子写下一revision `aborted`+`initRecoveryAbort`，提交published，删init+root fsync |
| `journalCommitted` | exact journal已`planned|aborted` | 保持journal，提交published绑定current revision，删init+root fsync |
| `published` | nonce/root/owner/journal hash+revision全部匹配 | 只删init+root fsync |

任一行出现unexpected entry、symlink、owner/root/hash/revision不符、base journal处于其他state，均exit3/JOURNAL_INVALID、保留全部证据并回显trusted transactionId；不映射`manualRecoveryRequired`，因为init协议不允许protected mutation前仍存活。fault tests逐stage证明cleanup unlink/dir fsync/rmdir/root fsync顺序，且可证prefix不留下原plan非terminal状态。

init恢复后、创建新plan前还必须root-fd按raw basename byte序读取最多`4096 + 1`个direct entry，不能在第4096项静默截断；除`install.lock/install-init.json/install-init.next`外只接受32-lower-hex transaction directory，逐项no-follow验证owner+journal。0...4096项才继续；一旦观察到第4097项，不解析/跳过后部、不创建新init/transaction，固定返回exit3、`command=plan,error={code:JOURNAL_INVALID,stage:plan,retryable:false},transactionId=null,state=null,protectedMutation=none`，已有bytes 0 mutation。expired planned可原子aborted，init已不在但journal为0-protected construction state的impossible orphan可写`initRecoveryAbort`后aborted；`aborted|rolledBack|committed`不阻断。若存在任何其他active state，当前plan不得创建新transaction：按raw ID byte序选择最小blocking transaction，返回该`transactionId`与其current state；planned/pending等schema-valid state用exit3/`INVALID_STATE`，tuple/identity不可证用exit7/manual。多项active时仍返回最小ID，逐次处理即可发现余项。这样published init unlink后到stdout之间crash留下的planned transaction会由下一plan稳定显露，而不会静默创建第二个；fault fixture覆盖entry count 4096/4097、init unlink、result canonicalization、stdout write前后。

transaction ID来自16个cryptographic random bytes。`<root>/<id>/` mode0700含owner marker和`journal.json` mode0600；journal≤1 MiB、events≤256。写入固定为同目录 `journal.next`，fsync file→`renameat`→fsync transaction dir；所有fd exactly-once close。schema `$defs.journal` required keys精确为 `schemaVersion,transactionId,creationNonce,ownerSha256,revision,state,mode,planFingerprint,planSnapshot,approval,continuation,checkpoints,filesystemTuple,stagingManifest,rollbackCleanupManifest,operationCursor,outerOracle,permissionProbe,events`、`additionalProperties=false`；schemaVersion const=`harvis.install-journal.v1`，creationNonce为init/owner同一32-lower-hex，ownerSha256为owner canonical bytes bare SHA；两项从revision1开始immutable且每次读取/写入都重算owner并逐byte对账。revision为从1开始且每次写exact+1的positive integer，planFingerprint为null或sha256Fingerprint。published init的nonce/ownerSha/journalSha/revision必须与owner/current journal完全一致后才可删除init；删除后journal是nonce/owner binding的持久authority。events action closed enum增加`initRecoveryAbort`，只允许上述0-protected init recovery写入。

`$defs.planSnapshot`是rollback所需的immutable脱敏baseline，required exact=`candidate,installed,pathDigests,plistFingerprints,tokenMetadataFingerprint,services,appProcess,approvedEffects,oldApiBaseline,oldTickBaseline,logBaselines,authorityInspectFingerprint,outerOracleCaptureId,outerOracleArtifactSha256,outerOracleFingerprint,createdAt,expiresAt`、additionalProperties=false；复用result同名defs。plistFingerprints exact=`{http,tick}`且值为sha256Fingerprint，token/authority/oracle为sha256Fingerprint；oldApiBaseline不使用generic fingerprint wrapper，其exact keys与ready/permissionDegraded oneOf完全由§9.3后文定义为`health,bar,task,permission`，schema在此只引用同一`$defs.oldApiBaseline`。oldTickBaseline required exact=`runtimeVersion,stateClass,lastTickAt`，version=nonempty≤64、stateClass=`ready|warning|permissionDegraded`、lastTickAt=null或rfc3339UTC。logBaselines为固定`httpOut,httpErr,tickOut,tickErr`四项有序array，每项required exact=`kind,pathDigest,parentIdentitySha256,leafIdentitySha256,baselineSize,prefixSha256`，digests bare、size0...128MiB；prefixSha256是baseline exact bytes SHA。outerOracleCaptureId=32-lower-hex、artifactSha256=bareSha256。planSnapshot不存candidate/source raw path，也不依赖rollback CLI再次提供candidate；restart后log append/Tick rollback oracle只信这些journal-bound facts。

checkpoints exact boolean keys为 `approvalConsumed,stagingManifestReady,rollbackCleanupManifestReady,appStageStarted,appStaged,helperStageStarted,helperStaged,appStopIntent,appStopped,httpBootoutIntent,httpStopped,tickBootoutIntent,tickStopped,appSwapIntent,appSwapped,appFinalized,helperSwapIntent,helperSwapped,helperFinalized,httpBootstrapIntent,httpStarted,apiVerified,permissionProbeVerified,tickBootstrapIntent,tickFirstRunVerified,candidateAppStarted,rollbackStarted,rollbackCompleted,helperCleanupStarted,helperCleanupDone,appCleanupStarted,appCleanupDone`，additionalProperties=false。filesystemTuple的app/helper各为 `{target,next,rollback,tupleSha256}`；前三项都是 exact `{state,identitySha256}`、additionalProperties=false，state只允许`old|new|partialNew|absent|other`，identitySha256在absent时必须null、其余为manifest/known-identity canonical bare SHA或other observation bare SHA；tupleSha256为三slot canonical payload bare SHA。stagingManifest与rollbackCleanupManifest都为 `{sha256:null|bareSha256,size:null|positive<=16MiB,entryCount:null|0...20000,totalBytes:null|0...512MiB}`；operationCursor=`{phase:null|copy|rollbackPartial|commitCleanup,component:null|helper|app,manifestKind:null|stagingNew|rollbackOld,nextEntry:0...20000,manifestSha256:null|bareSha256}`，各object additionalProperties=false。

`outerOracle`与`permissionProbe`都是journal required object，但不在这里另建简称schema：它们的唯一exact required roster、nullable conditional与oneOf分别由本节后文“outerOracle完整取代”及permissionProbe stop/resume truth table定义。schema文件只能引用那两个最终`$defs`，不得保留旧的五字段outerOracle或无handshake permissionProbe别名。

events exact required=`seq,at,stage,action,result,errorCode,rootCauseStage,rootCauseCode`、additionalProperties=false；seq从1连续，at=rfc3339UTC，stage沿用terminal result stage，action closed=`planCreated|initRecoveryAbort|expiredAbort|approvalConsumed|appStop|serviceBootout|copyStage|swapIntent|swap|finalize|serviceBootstrap|appStart|apiVerify|probeSubmit|probeQuiesce|probe|fdaPrompt|settingsOpen|oracleCapture|oracleCompare|oracleRebaseline|rollback|cleanupStage|stateTransition`，result=`intent|ok|failed|skipped`，errorCode=null或terminal result code enum。rootCauseStage=null或event-only enum`plan|prepare|app|services|swap|verify|fda|rollback|commit`，rootCauseCode=null或terminal result code加event-only`SWAP_FAILED|PERMISSION_PROBE_FAILED`；result=`intent|ok|skipped`时errorCode/rootCause二项必须全null，result=failed时rootCauseStage/rootCauseCode必须同时non-null，errorCode只有该event同时发布terminal error时才non-null且必须等于terminal result code。rollback归并事件用stage=rollback/errorCode=`ROLLED_BACK|ROLLBACK_FAILED|MANUAL_RECOVERY_REQUIRED`并保留原rootCause；不得把event-only code写进result。journal不存token/cookie/raw API body/raw certificate/raw candidate/source path；root/lock/journal/marker任一symlink/owner/mode/nlink/size/schema/sequence/conditional失配均fail closed。

transaction directory有两个immutable canonical manifest，均mode0600、≤16 MiB、≤20,000 entries：`staging-manifest.json`描述candidate/new App copy，`rollback-cleanup-manifest.json`在plan时从installed/old App生成并记录其exact identities/postorder，供Phase4删除rollback sibling；二者各自SHA/size写journal后fsync，不能用new manifest删除old-only path。`$defs.appTreeManifest` required exact=`schemaVersion,transactionId,purpose,component,entryCount,totalBytes,sourceTreeSha256,entries`、additionalProperties=false；schemaVersion const=`harvis.app-tree-manifest.v1`，purpose=`stagingNew|rollbackOld`，component const=`app`，entryCount与entries.count exact相等，totalBytes为全部regular file size和，sourceTreeSha256为§9.2 canonical App tree SHA。每个entry required exact=`path,kind,mode,size,contentSha256,xattrs,identitySha256,postorderIndex`、additionalProperties=false；root entry的path exact为空字符串且kind=directory，其他path为非空bundle-relative strict UTF-8、不得`.`/`..`/absolute/NUL且≤1024 bytes，全部按raw UTF-8 byte升序唯一；kind=`directory|file`，mode只含permission bits，xattrs为0或1个按name排序的exact `{name:"com.apple.provenance",valueSha256:64-lower-hex}`。directory的size/contentSha256必须null；file的size为0...128MiB、contentSha256=64-lower-hex、nlink由运行时强制1。stagingNew全部identitySha256=null；rollbackOld全部identitySha256为§9.2 regular/directory identity canonical 64-lower-hex。postorderIndex在0...entryCount-1全局唯一，子项必须小于其任一ancestor directory，root directory最后；parser独立重算entryCount/totalBytes/sourceTreeSha256/order/ancestry。canonical bytes沿用§9.1 ASCII JSON规则。helper是单regular file，不伪造App manifest：operationCursor component=helper时manifestKind=null且nextEntry只为0或1，以filesystemTuple slot identity控制。

copy/cleanup每最多128 entries只原子覆盖/fsync operationCursor，不追加per-batch event；events仅记录阶段边界，所以完整copy+cleanup仍≤256。cursor单调；crash resume时cursor之前entry须满足该phase完成态，当前batch entry允许“recorded exact identity仍present”或“syscall已完成而absent”，extra/symlink/identity漂移进入manual；没有对应manifest绝不开始copy/unlink。golden/malformed corpus必须覆盖duplicate/path traversal/invalid UTF-8/wrong sort/count/size/hash/xattr/identity/postorder/extra key与purpose条件。

planned transaction 到 approval.expiresAt仍未 consumed时，下一次 plan/apply/status在lock内先原子写 `aborted` + `expiredAbort`，不做 protected mutation；status返回aborted/0，旧apply返回exit4，随后新plan可创建。除 terminal `rolledBack|committed|aborted` 外，任一 transaction阻止新plan。

fingerprint算法不留实现选择：path digest=`SHA-256("path\0"+rawUTF8)`；regular file identity payload=`{dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,nlink,size,mtimeNs,ctimeNs,contentSha256}`；directory invariant identity payload=`{dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,xattrs}`，明确排除会因合法child unlink变化的nlink/size/mtime/ctime，xattrs是下述sorted name+valueSha256；token metadata省略contentSha256；PID payload=`{pid,startSec,startUsec,executableDev,executableIno,executableSha256}`。App tree walker root-anchored/no-follow，最大depth64、20,000 entries、单file128 MiB、total512 MiB、30秒monotonic deadline；只接受UTF-8 relative name、directory/regular file，regular file要求nlink1，directory不要求nlink1但用visited `(dev,ino)`拒绝循环/重复，拒绝symlink/special/ACL/nonzero file flags与除 `com.apple.provenance` 外xattr。record按raw UTF-8 relative path排序：directory=`{path,kind:"d",mode,xattrs}`，file=`{path,kind:"f",mode,size,sha256,xattrs}`，xattrs只存name+valueSha256。capture期间每个directory仍以完整stat的dev/ino/uid/gid/mode/nlink/size/mtime/ctime做pre/post race比较，整树第二次rewalk必须相等，否则`APP_TREE_CHANGED`；cleanup阶段只比较manifest directory invariant identity与visited dev/ino，允许且仅允许已journaled child unlink引起的可变metadata，root最后删除。

所有fingerprint payload都在同一JSON Schema `$defs`中以`additionalProperties=false`和下列exact required roster锁定，schema字段均const=`harvis.install-fingerprint.v1`：apply=`schema,purpose,transactionId,mode,signatureClass,candidate,installed,plistFingerprints,tokenMetadataFingerprint,services,appProcess,approvedEffects,createdAt,expiresAt`；rollbackManual=`schema,purpose,transactionId,state,journalRevision,filesystemTupleSha256,stagingManifestSha256,rollbackCleanupManifestSha256,createdAt,expiresAt`；fdaApprove=`schema,purpose,transactionId,failedProbeSha256,journalRevision,manualTCCChange,rollbackDoesNotRevertManualTCCChange,createdAt,expiresAt`；fdaDone=`schema,purpose,transactionId,failedProbeSha256,settingsOpenedAt,journalRevision,manualTCCChangeAcknowledged,rollbackDoesNotRevertManualTCCChange,createdAt,expiresAt`；acceptance=`schema,purpose,transactionId,state,candidateFingerprint,helperFingerprint,servicesFingerprint,appProcessFingerprint,outerOracleFingerprint,journalRevision,verifiedAt,createdAt,expiresAt`；commitResume=`schema,purpose,transactionId,state,journalRevision,operationCursor,rollbackCleanupManifestSha256,filesystemTupleSha256,createdAt,expiresAt`。purpose分别为exact enum值；all timestamps为RFC3339 UTC，apply/rollbackManual/acceptance/commitResume TTL exact +15min、fdaApprove +120s、fdaDone +600s，两个FDA boolean均true。

apply payload的source-key/Git只以outerOracleFingerprint绑定，不含raw source path。`fdaDone`是“用户已处理”ack而不虚构成功probe；`commitCleanupManual`只是不可消费的diagnostic classification，continuation fingerprint/expiresAt必须null，人工修复后仅status可签发新的commitResume。所有可消费fingerprint=`sha256:`+SHA-256(canonical payload)，single-use且revision/fact漂移exit4。`pendingUserAcceptance`中的每次`status`都必须在0 protected mutation下重验candidate/helper/plist/service/App与outer oracle；只允许transaction-owned journal/oracle artifact原子写。immutable App/helper/plist/receipt/path事实漂移必须拒绝并按可证明性rollback/manual；service/App PID允许在CU或人工重启后变化，但status须对当前PID重新执行exact label/executable identity与A→health→B稳定barrier，只有稳定且candidate identity正确才把新fingerprint写入journal。每次成功status原子递增revision、失效旧acceptance并签发新的+15min one-shot acceptance；PID不稳定、crash-loop或executable不符不签发。Phase4必须在最后一次CU后调用status、展示并批准fresh fingerprint；commit再次重验相同facts。acceptance验证与consume必须和state=`committing`、operationCursor=`commitCleanup/helper/null/0`初始化在同一个fsynced journal revision原子写；helper done后才原子切到`commitCleanup/app/rollbackOld/0`。crash只能是pending+unconsumed或committing+consumed，不得形成pending+consumed死态。

Approval consumed 后才 staging。next/rollback basename固定为 `.HarvisBar.app.next-<id>/.HarvisBar.app.rollback-<id>` 与 `.harvis.next-<id>/.harvis.rollback-<id>`，必须在对应 target parent初始不存在。candidate可跨volume；fd-anchored copier按immutable staging manifest逐项 `mkdirat/openat(O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW)`，复制bytes、mode及唯一allowlisted provenance xattr，拒绝ACL/flags/其他xattr，所有regular files fsync、directories postorder fsync、最后target parent fsync。复制中tuple为 `(old,partialNew,absent)`；复制完成才升级为new。复制前后candidate整树fingerprint相同；staged App重跑tree/receipt/helper/codesign，staged helper重跑file/codesign。mid-copy/fault/candidate drift时old target 0变化；cleanup只接受manifest path prefix、current uid、no symlink/special/hardlink，未完成file可与expected digest不同但不得有extra path。失配保留journal证据。

swap只允许同一parent fd上的 `renameatx_np(parentFD,targetBasename,parentFD,nextBasename,RENAME_SWAP)`；finalize只允许 `renameat(parentFD,nextBasename,parentFD,rollbackBasename)`；禁止 `AT_FDCWD`、absolute pathname和两次普通rename fallback。每次前后重验parent/entry identity并按 intent journal→fsync journal dir→syscall→fsync target parent→done journal。App先、helper后；rollback以相同fd-relative primitive逆序helper→App。staged bytes在swap前已逐file/dir/parent fsync，因此contract同时覆盖process crash和power loss。

apply/rollback合法component tuple为 `(old,absent,absent)`（untouched）、`(old,partialNew,absent)`（manifest-bound staging）、`(old,new,absent)`、`(new,old,absent)`、`(new,absent,old)`、`(old,absent,new)`；checkpoint/manifest/operationCursor必须一致，否则进入`manualRecoveryRequired/rollbackManual`。apply crash不forward resume，只允许status/rollback；rollback先安全清理partial next再按已发生swap逆序恢复。commit阶段只允许每component `(new,absent,old)` 或cleanup完成的 `(new,absent,absent)`，组合必须与operationCursor一致；partial cleanup失败进入committing或`manualRecoveryRequired/commitCleanupManual`，后者禁止rollback，只能status，待人工修复后由status签发commitResume。每个copy/batch-cursor/fsync/journal/swap/finalize/unlink点都有deterministic fault seam。

#### 9.2.1 Journal 与 fingerprint exact closure（覆盖本节前文简称）

transaction owner marker 固定为`owner.json`，以`openat(O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW,0600)`创建，必须current uid/regular/nlink1/≤4 KiB。canonical object exact required=`schemaVersion,transactionId,creationNonce,uid,rootIdentitySha256,createdAt`、`additionalProperties=false`；schema const=`harvis.install-owner.v1`，creationNonce在init存在时必须等于init，init发布删除后必须等于journal immutable creationNonce；uid=current uid，root identity是transaction directory exact `{dev,ino,uid,gid,mode}` canonical bare SHA。canonical write→file fsync→从parent fd重开并比对bytes/entry/root identity→transaction-dir fsync完成后，才可把init推进ownerCommitted。revision1把owner canonical bare SHA写入journal.ownerSha256；status/rollback/commit/cleanup及Oracle均先重算并同时对账journal nonce+ownerSha。marker预存、symlink/hardlink/replace/schema/transactionId/nonce/root/hash mismatch都`JOURNAL_INVALID`，不得重建或覆盖。

`journal.json` 是唯一committed authority。每次写前必须以transaction-dir fd证明`journal.next` absent；crash leftover绝不晋升，也不要求它的inode预先记进旧journal。恢复时只能从fd no-follow打开leftover，验证regular/current uid/nlink1/mode0600/≤1 MiB、transactionId相同、revision exact等于committed+1、canonical schema/bytes完整；通过后仅identity-bound unlink+dir fsync，再从committed journal重算新一次写，不通过则`JOURNAL_INVALID/manualRecoveryRequired`。new next用`O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW` mode0600，完成canonical write→file fsync→fd/entry/schema/revision重验→`renameat`→dir fsync；create/write/file-fsync/rename/dir-fsync每点crash都有fixture。`outer-oracle.next` 与`outer-oracle.preview.next`同理只验证same transaction/capture command/schema/canonical bytes后删除重算，不晋升leftover，不允许pathname overwrite。

`$defs.journal` 前文required roster由本段扩展为额外必须含`planConstructionState,oracleInput,rollbackOracle`。planConstructionState=`collectingFacts|awaitingOracle|complete`；只有complete可令plan返回Approval或让apply继续。`oracleInput` required-but-nullable；awaitingOracle/complete时non-null exact=`artifactSha256,scopeDigests,authorityInspectFingerprint`、`additionalProperties=false`，scopeDigests固定resolvedStateRoot/profileWorkspaceFallback顺序。artifact bytes不含self hash：`oracle-input.json` exact required=`schemaVersion,transactionId,journalRevision,scopeDigests,authorityInspectFingerprint`、`additionalProperties=false`，schema const=`harvis.oracle-input.v1`，journalRevision必须等于写intent所绑revision；journal `artifactSha256`才是该canonical bytes的bare SHA。coordinator以mode0600/current uid/regular/nlink1/≤64 KiB的`.next`原子规则写入并绑定journal后，才调capture；planSnapshot在Oracle成功前可null，Oracle成功后必须原子写入完整planSnapshot、outerOracle binding与planConstructionState=complete。crash/status见collectingFacts/awaitingOracle必须abort并identity-bound清理草稿，绝不签Approval。`rollbackOracle` exact required=`oldHttpVerified,oldTickBaselineCaptured,beforeOldTickLastTickAt,oldTickVerified,oldAppVerified`、`additionalProperties=false`；初始全false/null。reverse swap后old HTTP lightweight全通过才写oldHttpVerified=true；bootstrap old Tick前立即读bar-status并在同一fsynced revision写`oldTickBaselineCaptured=true` 与`beforeOldTickLastTickAt`。old Tick 只能以这个即时值做null→nonnull/strict-greater freshness，还要求old runtime version与A→health→B同PID；plan-time `oldTickBaseline`只用于恢复后兼容state class，不能作freshness基准。

`outerOracle` exact required由本段完整取代为 `captureId,artifactSha256,oracleFingerprint,sourceState,transitionState,previewCaptureId,previewArtifactSha256,previewOracleFingerprint`、`additionalProperties=false`。字段全部required-but-nullable；`aborted`或planConstructionState尚未complete且Oracle capture未成功时八项必须全null，`planSnapshot` 也必须null，不伪造unbound/zero digest。planConstructionState=complete的non-aborted state要求baseline三项和sourceState/transitionState non-null、planSnapshot完整non-null；`transitionState=none|rebaselined`时preview三项必须null，`transitionState=awaitingSourceRebaseline`时preview三项必须non-null且与baseline不同。若capture成功后后续最终journal写入失败而aborted，可保留完整baseline artifact/binding，但planSnapshot只有已通过schema且与Oracle一起原子提交时才non-null。preview artifact 固定`outer-oracle.preview.json`，mode0600/current uid/regular/nlink1/≤16 MiB，canonical schema/result SHA与journal三项逐字节绑定。crash后status先reconcile baseline/preview artifact、journal transition与continuation；孤儿preview只能identity-bound删除，不得自动rebaseline。每个plan failure stage都有aborted canonical golden。

`permissionProbe` exact required由本段扩展为 `attempt,submitIntent,startupStopped,resumeIntent,resumed,labelLoaded,managedPidFingerprint,stdoutIdentitySha256,stderrIdentitySha256,resultSha256,terminationClass,terminationCode,workerErrorCode,quiescent`、`additionalProperties=false`。`terminationClass=null|exit|signal|timeout`；`terminationCode=null|0...255`；`workerErrorCode`是 `null|AUTHORITY_INVALID|PREEXISTING_ENTRY|PERMISSION_DENIED|IO_FAILED|CHANGED_DURING_PROBE|CLEANUP_UNCERTAIN|INTERNAL_FAILURE|SUBMIT_FAILED|PROCESS_IDENTITY_MISMATCH|WORKER_SIGNALED|WORKER_TIMEOUT|OUTPUT_IDENTITY_INVALID|OUTPUT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED|RESULT_MISSING|RESULT_INVALID|EXIT_RESULT_MISMATCH|QUIESCENCE_FAILED` 的closed enum。attempt1...3的label由transactionId+attempt唯一派生，submit前先写attempt+intent且quiescent=false；只要`submitIntent=true`而结果尚未按下表闭合，status/rollback就必须枚举该label、launchctl/PID reconcile并先quiesce，不得因`labelLoaded=false`或result null当作未启动。`managedPidFingerprint`一旦捕获就保留其exact PID/start/executable identity hash，即使进程随后退出也不得清空；stdout/stderr identity只绑定fixed basename处经过no-follow验证的regular file身份。`startupStopped`只能在launchctl label/PID与libproc start/executable稳定且process status exact stopped时写true；`resumeIntent`必须在发送SIGCONT前先fsync为true，`resumed`只在同一PID/start/executable重验后SIGCONT成功才写true。closed branch truth table如下，schema用`oneOf`锁定每一行：

| branch | exact conditional |
| --- | --- |
| never attempted | `attempt=0,submitIntent/startupStopped/resumeIntent/resumed/labelLoaded=false,managedPidFingerprint/stdoutIdentitySha256/stderrIdentitySha256/resultSha256/terminationClass/terminationCode/workerErrorCode=null,quiescent=true` |
| submitted / waiting for stop | `attempt=1...3,submitIntent=true,startupStopped/resumeIntent/resumed=false,quiescent=false,resultSha256/terminationClass/terminationCode/workerErrorCode=null`；labelLoaded、PID与两个output identity可随已观察事实为null/非null，但每次变化必须先journal |
| stopped / resume intent | `attempt=1...3,submitIntent=true,startupStopped=true,resumed=false,quiescent=false`，PID与两个output identitynon-null；`resumeIntent`可false或true，result/termination/error全null |
| resumed / in flight | `attempt=1...3,submitIntent/startupStopped/resumeIntent/resumed=true,quiescent=false`，PID与两个output identitynon-null，result/termination/error全null |
| submit failed before managed process | `attempt=1...3,submitIntent=true,startupStopped/resumeIntent/resumed/labelLoaded=false,managedPidFingerprint/resultSha256/terminationClass/terminationCode=null,workerErrorCode=SUBMIT_FAILED,quiescent=true`；两个output identity只能为已成功创建并验证的值或同时null |
| canonical worker exit | 四个intent/handshake bool均true，label已unloaded、managed PID已消失，PID与两个output identity均non-null，`terminationClass=exit,terminationCode=0...255,resultSha256=bareSha256,quiescent=true`；resultSha只可绑定≤64KiB canonical parsed `permissionWorkerResult`，observed exit必须等于§9.3 worker-result precedence，`workerErrorCode`逐字等于result `error`（success为null） |
| exit with missing/invalid/mismatched result | label已unloaded、managed PID已消失、`terminationClass=exit,terminationCode=observed,resultSha256=null,quiescent=true`；workerErrorCode按事实唯一为`RESULT_MISSING|RESULT_INVALID|EXIT_RESULT_MISMATCH|OUTPUT_IDENTITY_INVALID|OUTPUT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`，不得保留partial parsed digest；若未完成stop/resume握手只能是`PROCESS_IDENTITY_MISMATCH`而非worker result class |
| signaled | label已unloaded、managed PID已消失、`terminationClass=signal,terminationCode=signalNumber,resultSha256=null,workerErrorCode=WORKER_SIGNALED,quiescent=true`；handshake bool保留已完成前缀 |
| timeout then proven quiescent | label已unloaded、managed PID已消失、`terminationClass=timeout,terminationCode=null,resultSha256=null,workerErrorCode=WORKER_TIMEOUT,quiescent=true`；handshake bool保留已完成前缀 |
| process/output identity changed | 能证明label已unloaded且managed PID消失时，termination记录实际观察值或尚未spawn时null，`resultSha256=null,workerErrorCode=PROCESS_IDENTITY_MISMATCH|OUTPUT_IDENTITY_INVALID,quiescent=true` |
| quiescence uncertain | `attempt=1...3,submitIntent=true,quiescent=false,resultSha256=null,workerErrorCode=QUIESCENCE_FAILED`；termination记录已知实际值或timeout/null，立即进入`manualRecoveryRequired`，不得swap helper或伪装rollback完成 |

只有`canonical worker exit`可以消费worker payload、进入direct-success/FDA分类或绑定`failedProbeSha256`；其余terminal branch都是coordinator failure。`PERMISSION_DENIED`只在canonical worker exit且result/exit parity成立时允许进入prompt；`CLEANUP_UNCERTAIN|INTERNAL_FAILURE`或quiescence uncertain必须manual，其余失败按protected-mutation状态走closed abort/rollback。每个branch都有schema/emitter/recovery golden。

所有fingerprint都是`sha256:`+SHA-256(canonical ASCII JSON of exact object)。`planFingerprint` exact等于valid `apply` Approval fingerprint，不是另一个未定义hash。apply payload exact required替换为 `schema,purpose,transactionId,mode,signatureClass,candidate,installed,plistFingerprints,tokenMetadataFingerprint,services,appProcess,approvedEffects,outerOracleCaptureId,outerOracleArtifactSha256,outerOracleFingerprint,createdAt,expiresAt`。sourceRebaseline payload exact required=`schema,purpose,transactionId,state,oldCaptureId,oldArtifactSha256,oldOracleFingerprint,newCaptureId,newArtifactSha256,newOracleFingerprint,journalRevision,createdAt,expiresAt`，purpose const=`sourceRebaseline`、state const=`pendingUserAcceptance`、TTL exact +15min、single-use/revision-bound。acceptance payload增加`outerOracleCaptureId,outerOracleArtifactSha256`，与已有`outerOracleFingerprint`三项同时绑定。

aggregate fingerprint 不留默认选择。`candidateFingerprint`是result candidate exact object的canonical hash。`helperFingerprint`的exact derived payload为`{pathDigest,fileIdentitySha256,contentSha256,signingIdentityFingerprint}`：每次status/commit对current stable helper重读产生，内容/签名必须等于candidate plan facts，file identity绑定当前安装inode。`servicesFingerprint`是services exact `{http,tick}` object hash，其中每个entry都包含`label,plannedState,currentState,pid,startSec,startUsec,executableFingerprint`，不使用map iteration order；所以同一PID被不同启动时间的新进程复用也必然产生不同fingerprint。`appProcessFingerprint`是exact `{wasRunning,currentState,pid,startSec,startUsec,executableFingerprint}` hash。`filesystemTupleSha256`是journal app/helper两个tuple exact object的bare SHA。前四项加`sha256:`。`plistFingerprints.http|tick`是exact `{fileIdentitySha256,contentSha256}` hash；`tokenMetadataFingerprint`是regular file identity payload排除contentSha256的hash；`authorityInspectFingerprint`是validated canonical `authorityInspectResult` success bytes hash；service/App entry的`executableFingerprint`是exact `{dev,ino,size,contentSha256,signingIdentityFingerprint}` hash，pid/start只属于service/appProcess payload而不混入executableFingerprint。App tree/sourceTree SHA固定为`SHA256(canonical ASCII JSON of {schemaVersion:"harvis.app-tree-digest.v1",records:[...]})`，records含root、按raw UTF-8 relative path byte升序，使用§9.2已定义directory/file record exact keys；manifest `sourceTreeSha256`、candidate `appTreeSha256`与walker都必须同值。permission-probe immediate-entry digest使用§9.4 Oracle同类u32/u64 length framing，domain=`permission-probe-directory`，每个entry parts=`rawNameBytes,ASCII(kind),u64be(dev),u64be(ino)`，不连接裸bytes。全部有cross-language golden。

### 9.3 LaunchAgent、process、token、API 与 safe full probe

部署交易对两份 plist 使用比 §5.5 UI inspector 更强的 exact contract。top-level key set必须精确为 `EnvironmentVariables,KeepAlive,Label,LimitLoadToSessionType,ProcessType,ProgramArguments,RunAtLoad,StandardErrorPath,StandardOutPath,WorkingDirectory`，`Program`必须absent；Label exact，`RunAtLoad=true,KeepAlive=true,LimitLoadToSessionType="Aqua",ProcessType="Interactive"`。WorkingDirectory exact为 fixed installed App 的 `Contents/Resources/HarvisPlugin`；HTTP/Tick stdout/stderr exact为 `<account-home>/.harvis/logs/{http|tick}.{out|err}.log`。

四个log target必须在plan前已存在且pathname互异：从account home到logs parent的每个component均root-anchored/no-follow、directory、owner=current uid、不可group/other writable；每个leaf均以`open(O_RDONLY|O_NOFOLLOW|O_CLOEXEC)`证明regular、owner=current uid、mode0600或0644、nlink1、size≤128 MiB，四者dev/ino互异。plan保存parent chain、leaf identity与baseline size/content SHA；每次bootstrap前后重走chain并重开leaf，必须同dev/ino/uid/mode/nlink，final size只可在baseline到baseline+16 MiB且baseline byte prefix SHA不变。coordinator不创建/rotate/chmod/truncate log；symlink/FIFO/device/socket/hardlink、owner/mode错误、ancestor replacement、leaf replacement/truncate/prefix rewrite或超cap都在对应mutation前拒绝或触发rollback，victim sentinel不得变化。runtimeManagedWriteScope对log只授权这四个已验证leaf的append，不授权其parent或任意重解析target。

HTTP ProgramArguments exact为 `[stableHelper,"http","--host","127.0.0.1","--port",uint16,"--root",absoluteRoot,"--auth-token-file",absoluteTokenFile]`；Tick exact为 `[stableHelper,"run-tick","--root",sameAbsoluteRoot,"--profile-root",absoluteProfileRoot,"--interval",positiveUInt,"--loop"]`。EnvironmentVariables required exact keys/values为 `HOME=<account-home>`、`HARVIS_HOME=<account-home>/.harvis`、`PATH=<account-home>/.local/bin:<account-home>/.local/node/current/bin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin`；HTTP额外且只额外含 `HARVIS_HTTP_AUTH_TOKEN_FILE=<same absoluteTokenFile>`。可选 hub root只能以 `HARVIS_HUB_WIKI_ROOT` 与 `HARZVA_HUB_WIKI_ROOT` 两项同时出现、值相等且为canonical no-follow absolute directory；其余key一律拒绝，尤其 `DYLD_*|LD_*|PYTHON*` 或任意未列 HARVIS key。plist bytes/inode/identity从plan到terminal不得变化；swap后相同WorkingDirectory pathname必须解析到candidate bundle payload。

plan要求 HTTP/Tick均loaded/running；`/bin/launchctl print gui/<uid>/<label>` exit0，stdout中恰有一个 `state = running` 与一个positive pid。每次服务 capture 都必须用libproc读取该PID的startSec/startUsec和executable identity，前后夹取两次launchctl并要求label/state/PID一致，再重取libproc身份；只有PID、启动时间与stable helper executable identity全部稳定才可发布。任一同PID不同start、PID复用、launchctl sandwich变化或部分身份缺失均为`PROCESS_IDENTITY_MISMATCH`，不得进入plan/status acceptance/commit。App process adapter只用libproc的 `proc_listallpids/proc_pidpath/proc_pidinfo(PROC_PIDTBSDINFO)`；连续两次稳定枚举后只允许0或1个 exact installed-App executable identity，不调用pgrep/ps。多实例、PID reuse、不稳定或歧义=`APP_PROCESS_AMBIGUOUS`。

App terminate是closed state machine而非单次signal：先两次稳定枚举；0 match直接进入zero barrier，1 match记录PID/start/dev/ino/SHA。整个terminate attempt共享35秒monotonic deadline且最多3个distinct matching identities；每个identity在每次signal前立即重验，先SIGTERM并poll最多`min(5s,remaining)`，每次poll重验PID identity，ESRCH只表示继续枚举而非成功。仍是同一identity才SIGKILL，再poll`min(5s,remaining)`；global remaining耗尽或第4个identity=`APP_PROCESS_AMBIGUOUS`。PID已被复用/identity不同则绝不signal该PID，EPERM或其他signal/proc错误按是否已protected mutation进入abort/rollback/manual。终态要求global deadline内两次连续完整枚举均0 exact installed-App match；非child绝不waitpid。登录项重启出的新matching PID回到同一global state machine。swap intent前还要重新执行两次0-match barrier；任何新match均先回到状态机，绝不在运行中swap。fake libproc/signal clock须覆盖exact 5/10/35秒边界、TERM成功、TERM超时后KILL、ESRCH、EPERM、PID reuse、多实例与反复relaunch。

token只从exact HTTP plist派生，以root-anchored no-follow fd打开；要求regular、owner=current uid、mode0600、nlink1。bytes只接受当前生成器的unpadded URL-safe 43字符 `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}\n?`，或兼容既有lowerhex 64字符 `[0-9a-f]{64}\n?`；禁止padding、space/tab、uppercase hex之外的宽松归一化与multiple LF，必须原样保留同一token bytes，不写、不旋转。前后fd/entry/bytes必须相等；只在内存形成Bearer，绝不进入argv/env/stdout/journal/report。

plan以Tick plist的workspace/profile为authority，不在Python重写Rust TOML语义。verified candidate bundled helper必须先提供hidden read-only exact argv `[candidateHelper,"internal-authority-inspect"]`；它从getpwuid与两份verified plist读取authority，强制`HARVIS_STATE_ROOT` absent，直接复用`harvis-runtime`的TOML parser/discovery/resolution且0 write。`$defs.authorityInspectResult` required exact=`schemaVersion,ok,config,resolvedStateRoot,profileFallback,workspaceStateId,runtimeOutputs,writesSkipped,redacted,error`、additionalProperties=false；schema const=`harvis.authority-inspect.v1`，writesSkipped/redacted=true。evidence五项均required-but-nullable：config null或exact`{present,workspaceAncestorDepth,identitySha256,contentSha256}`，present=false时后三项null、depth0...64；resolvedStateRoot null或exact`{relation,pathDigest,identitySha256}`且relation=`workspaceHarvis`；profileFallback null或exact`{workspaceStateId,pathDigest,identitySha256}`；workspaceStateId null或`[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,80}`并与fallback相同；runtimeOutputs null或固定kind `defaultInbox|defaultReports|statusFile|eventLog|archiveReports|memoryStore|packetStore|runStore|reportStatus` 的九项有序array，每项只含`kind,authority=resolvedStateRoot|profileFallback,relativePathDigest`，0 raw path。error为null或closed`AUTHORITY_INVALID|CONFIG_INVALID|OUTPUT_OUTSIDE_SCOPE|SECOND_STATE_TREE|IO_FAILED|INTERNAL_FAILURE`。schema oneOf固定：ok=true要求五项完整/error null/exit0；ok=false时五项全部null而非保留trusted prefix，error必须non-null，`AUTHORITY_INVALID|CONFIG_INVALID|OUTPUT_OUTSIDE_SCOPE|SECOND_STATE_TREE`各自exit3，`IO_FAILED` exit4，`INTERNAL_FAILURE` exit9。stdout≤64KiB single canonical JSON+LF、stderr empty；exit/ok/error必须exact parity。每个error及malformed/overflow/exit mismatch都有逐byte golden，不伪造partial path digest。

coordinator以plist中的raw workspace/profile做root-anchored walk，按helper的ancestorDepth重开fixed `.harvis/harvis.toml`并独立核对identity/content SHA，再独立重建workspace `.harvis`和`profileRoot/state/workspaces/<workspaceStateId>` identity/path digest；helper output与fd facts必须一致。resolved state root必须精确等于workspace `.harvis`，所有RuntimeConfig output只能归属该root或exact fallback，否则本次local transaction拒绝。workspace/profile/state root均须existing no-follow directory；state root/fallback不得与stable-helper/auth/transaction/log authority重叠。批准范围诚实覆盖整个resolved state root、exact profile fallback与四个exact log file，避免遗漏memory review/packet/run等既有Tick输出；但authoritative config bytes、tracked files、stable helper、auth token与profile其他位置仍独立canary-protected，不能被父scope吞掉。source worktree可以与workspace相同，不能靠path-disjoint伪造安全；Phase3 outer oracle必须证明Git logical refs/config view、index bytes与所有tracked-file bytes不变，只允许上述已批准runtime scope出现非tracked runtime delta。它不宣称检测packed↔loose ref或config comment/whitespace等logical-equivalent rewrite；这些同semantic-neutral churn一起属于本地同UID边界。Rust complex TOML/escape/dotted/multiline fixture必须证明inspect与real Tick resolution逐字段parity；Python不import TOML package且0 network/dependency。

plan只运行无写 lightweight GET：health、bar-status no-full、task-progress limit20、permission-probe no-query。old 0.35.1 baseline中health/auth/wrong-token必须满足contract；bar/task/permission允许`ready`，或且只允许schema-valid、redacted、无local path的legacy known-permission degraded（包括已知HTTP400 `Operation not permitted` class）并只在journal记录endpoint/status/class digest，不存raw body/message；non-permission 4xx/5xx、malformed/leak一律阻断plan。apply停App与HTTP→Tick后完成swap，只先bootstrap HTTP并等待exact PID/helper identity。HTTP client必须injected，production用AF_INET直连plist固定 `127.0.0.1:port`，完全忽略proxy/env，禁止redirect，closed path/query，connect2秒/endpoint total10秒/body1 MiB；验证peer loopback，任何3xx/peer变化失败且0外连。health要求HTTP200、`data.api_version="v0.40-rust-alpha"`、`data.auth_required=true`；fixed synthetic wrong Bearer访问受保护bar-status必须exact401。正确Bearer的bar-status full、task-progress limit20、permission-probe lightweight在preFDA阶段可暂时为schema-valid ready或redacted skipped/warning，permission响应固定 `writesSkipped=true,redacted=true,includesLocalAbsolutePaths=false`；这只是provisional，不把generic I/O skipped误标为FDA。随后必须运行launchd worker：只有winner=`PERMISSION_DENIED`才是可进入prompt的TCC class；`IO_FAILED|AUTHORITY_INVALID|PREEXISTING_ENTRY|CHANGED_DURING_PROBE|CLEANUP_UNCERTAIN|INTERNAL_FAILURE`均不得打开Settings。worker成功后重启HTTP为new PID并要求bar/task/permission全部HTTP200 ready；仍skipped/warning是`API_CONTRACT_FAILED`并rollback，不prompt。pending前无论direct-success还是FDA lane都必须完成这项new-PID ready复测，动态body不byte-exact且不记录。

现有installed Rust helper的HTTP full、public CLI `permission-probe`、service-manager direct probe与MCP `harvis_permission_probe`都可在caller path执行 `create_dir_all + fs::write`，具有symlink overwrite/confused-deputy风险；Phase3必须先盘点并消除该helper binary的全部公开write-probe entrypoint，修复通过前candidate deployment硬阻断。HTTP GET只从 `AppState.workspace_root`产生lightweight结果且query必须empty，router移除POST，旧full/root/profile query与POST返回closed400/405；public CLI/service-manager/MCP probe保留兼容命令名但只能返回lightweight/read-only结果，caller给root/profile也不得create/write，任何“full”请求指向用户调用hidden fixed-authority worker的说明而不代执行。HarvisBar、Rust/Python service-manager caller同步为GET no-query；全repo Rust callgraph/negative argv+RPC tests证明除下述hidden worker外0 `create_dir_all/fs::write/OpenOptions(create)` probe path。legacy Python interpreter probe不属于installed Rust helper/TCC authority，仍须由local-smoke证明LaunchAgent与HarvisBar不会调用它；本Goal不把它伪装成FDA oracle。

真正的FDA测试改为candidate stable helper的hidden `internal-permission-probe` one-shot，CLI exact argv只有 `[verifiedStableHelper,"internal-permission-probe"]` 且不接收任何path/option；worker忽略继承env。为消除one-shot在首次launchctl观察前快速退出的竞态，worker在解析closed subcommand后、读取任何plist/authority或执行任何probe I/O前，必须立即对自身发送SIGSTOP；此前stdout/stderr必须0 byte且0 filesystem/network write。coordinator预建transaction-owned stdout/stderr regular mode0600/nlink1，job label exact=`local.harzva.harvis.permission-probe.<transactionId>.<attempt>`，attempt单调1...3；唯一启动argv为`[/bin/launchctl,submit,-l,<label>,-o,<verified-stdout>,-e,<verified-stderr>,--,<verifiedStableHelper>,internal-permission-probe]`。coordinator以condition polling（不是固定sleep barrier）从`launchctl print gui/<uid>/<label>`取得唯一managed PID，并用libproc验证startSec/startUsec、candidate stable-helper executable identity与exact stopped status；A/B launchctl+libproc样本必须稳定。它先把`startupStopped=true`及PID/output identity fsync进journal，再写`resumeIntent=true`并fsync，立即重验同一label/PID/start/executable仍stopped后才向该identity发SIGCONT，成功后写`resumed=true`。crash在stop capture、resume intent、SIGCONT或resumed journal任一点，status/rollback都按journal+实时stopped/running/exit身份reconcile；绝不向复用PID发signal。未观察到exact stopped handshake、过早exit、SIGCONT失败或identity变化均不得解析成worker结果，也不得打开Settings。resume后worker才从 `getpwuid` 与两份exact LaunchAgent plist自行重建workspace/profile/config authority，只探测workspace root、workspace `.harvis`、profile root，因此同UID caller不能指定任意FDA路径。deadline后只用`bootout gui/<uid>/<label>`终止并重验PID，完成后也bootout/remove label。output leaf/parent identity前后相同、stderr empty、stdout按schema/exit解析后identity-bound unlink。禁止coordinator直接spawn worker，以免Terminal/Codex responsible-process FDA形成假阳性。

三处authority都必须是现存root-anchored/no-follow directory，禁止create_dir_all；每处只用fd-relative `openat(O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW,0600)`创建固定 `.harvis-permission-probe.tmp`、EINTR-safe写exact `ok\n`、fsync/fstat、按dev/ino重验后unlinkat、fsync parent。预存symlink/regular/special全部失败且不覆盖。directory fingerprint排除mtime/ctime与probe basename，只hashimmediate entries的sorted raw-name SHA-256+kind+dev/ino；前后相等且entry absent。

schema `$defs.permissionWorkerResult` required keys精确为 `schemaVersion,ok,checks,redacted,includesLocalAbsolutePaths,writesSkipped,error`、additionalProperties=false：schema const `harvis.permission-probe-worker.v1`；checks exact三项固定顺序，每项 `{kind:workspaceRoot|workspaceHarvis|profileRoot,ok:bool,exists:bool,writable:bool,probeAbsentBefore:bool,probeAbsentAfter:bool,directoryFingerprintMatched:bool,errorCode:null|AUTHORITY_INVALID|PREEXISTING_ENTRY|PERMISSION_DENIED|IO_FAILED|CHANGED_DURING_PROBE|CLEANUP_UNCERTAIN|INTERNAL_FAILURE}` 且additionalProperties=false；item ok当且仅当其余五bool全true且errorCode=null，top ok当且仅当三item ok。三个privacy bool固定为 `redacted=true,includesLocalAbsolutePaths=false,writesSkipped=false`。多项失败的top error/exit precedence固定为：任一`INTERNAL_FAILURE`→error同值/exit9；否则任一`CLEANUP_UNCERTAIN`→5；否则任一`PERMISSION_DENIED|IO_FAILED`时按checks固定顺序取首个code→4；否则任一`AUTHORITY_INVALID|PREEXISTING_ENTRY|CHANGED_DURING_PROBE`按固定顺序取首个code→3；全成功error=null/exit0。stdout是≤64 KiB single canonical JSON+LF，stderr empty；coordinator逐bytes/schema/exit parity验证，尤其exit5/9或PID/temp不确定必须manual而不能automatic rollback。组合/property corpus覆盖三checks的全部winner class。

coordinator在submit前先root-anchored记录三parent identity与probe basename absent；5秒active deadline后bootout job并以launchctl+libproc identity轮询10秒quiescence。只有managed PID消失、label unloaded、三parent identity仍相等、三处probe仍absent并fsync parent后才可继续/automatic rollback。若PID/label仍存在，或PID已消失但任一baseline-absent probe出现、parent改变/不可读，则进入`manualRecoveryRequired`；parent不知道worker创建entry的inode时绝不猜测unlink，也禁止在运行中的helper executable上swap。preexisting entry正常fail且保持原entry bytes/inode，可安全rollback。malicious symlink、hung syscall、kill-after-create、job PID reuse、bootout与quiescence fixture必须证明victim bytes/inode不变、label/PID 0残留且无后台写。

full worker成功后才bootstrap Tick。由于 `run-tick --loop` 会先执行一次tick再sleep，不能只看瞬时launchctl running：bootstrap前先记录bar-status `work.lastTickAt`；null baseline要求新值non-null，non-null baseline要求新值strict-greater。若需等待wall clock越过旧值，该等待与后续poll共享同一个30秒monotonic budget，future timestamp/clock rollback到期即automatic rollback。exact sandwich顺序为 launchctl/PID sample A→authenticated bar-status新tick→health barrier→launchctl/PID sample B，A/B必须同pid/start/executable且中途无restart，bar-status `runtime.sourceRuntimeVersion`等于candidate receipt runtime version；first-tick exit、KeepAlive crash-loop、version mismatch或deadline都rollback。

随后按plan的App `wasRunning`恢复candidate App：true时执行open后在30秒内由libproc捕获唯一installed executable PID/start identity，使用App sample A→authenticated health barrier→App sample B，A/B必须同PID/start/dev/ino/SHA且无额外match；open exit0但无PID、冷启动崩溃/restart、多PID/PID reuse都rollback。wasRunning=false时不open，并在pending前连续两次稳定枚举要求0 matching App PID。rollback恢复old App也使用相同true/false barrier，不能把open exit0当成功。

rollback不能调用old helper的hidden worker：0.35.1不含该subcommand，而且原系统可能正处于permission-degraded baseline。plan必须记录old health/auth/bar/task/permission-lightweight、Tick runtime version/state class与service/App identity；恢复old App/helper后只先bootstrap HTTP并要求这些read-only oracle恢复到兼容baseline，再bootstrap旧Tick，以同一个30秒null/strict-greater lastTickAt + A/health/B PID sandwich证明旧loop重新工作，允许原有permission-degraded/state-warning但不得比baseline新增contract failure；最后恢复旧App running/stopped。禁止调用old public unsafe full probe。

任何explicit/automatic rollback都先执行closed prelude，再碰swap tuple：`candidateAppStarted`或libproc发现candidate App时先按上述identity-bound state machine停App并达到two-zero barrier；permission worker process group必须已quiescent且三probe parent/absent oracle确定，否则直接`manualRecoveryRequired`；若Tick bootstrap intent/done或launchctl显示candidate Tick运行，先bootout Tick并以label/PID/helper identity确认stopped；随后对HTTP执行同样bootout/stopped barrier。intent存在但syscall结果未知时先用launchctl+PID identity reconcile；已stopped是idempotent no-op，unexpected executable/PID reuse/unknown label state均manual。只有App/worker/Tick/HTTP全quiescent后才按helper→App逆swap。pre-swap失败只清理manifest-bound partial next；App-only swapped、helper-swapped、HTTP-started、worker-failed、Tick-started、candidate-App-started、pendingUserAcceptance每个checkpoint都必须由同一table导出prelude/no-op与reverse-swap集合，禁止直接跳到restore chain。reverse swap完成后才走old HTTP lightweight baseline→old Tick barrier→old App barrier；old worker调用数必须0。

Tick与HTTP会更新上述approved runtime state/log；这不是coordinator直接写，但不能宣称workspace/profile byte-identical。coordinator filesystem adapter只可修改transaction root、App/helper siblings；full worker只可修改三处fixed temp。plist/token/defaults/Git protected bytes不变，runtime scope之外任一delta使交易失败。所有等待使用PID/API/clock-condition barrier，不用fixed sleep。

#### 9.3.1 LaunchAgent 与recovery exact closure（覆盖本节前文简称）

plist `ProgramArguments` 的每个元素都必须是String。port string满足`[1-9][0-9]{0,4}`、canonical decimal parse为1...65535；interval string满足`[1-9][0-9]{0,8}`、canonical decimal parse为1...86400。workspace/profile/token 项是strict UTF-8 canonical absolute String，不含NUL。CFNumber、leading zero、plus/minus、space、overflow或非canonical decimal都是`SERVICE_PRECONDITION_FAILED`。

runtime-delta 证明只对下列有限protected roster作出承诺，不虚称监控全机任意路径：App/helper target+parents、HTTP/Tick plist、auth token、authority `harvis.toml`、四个log identity/prefix、source preference与tracked source Oracle、transaction root/permission probe parents，以及authority-inspect列出的resolved state root/profile fallback。planSnapshot中已有candidate/installed/path/plist/token/log/authority/Oracle fingerprints就是该roster的exact baseline；apply/status/rollback/commit按各自checkpoint重验。approved runtime scopes内可以有§ 9.1.1明示的non-reversible writes；范围外只对上述protected roster的delta fail closed。其他untracked/ignored/无关本机路径不在本transaction完整性声明内；安全信心来自fixed code/argv/callgraph与fixtures，不写成无sandbox的全局filesystem monitor。

rollback prelude中“permission worker process group”简称作废。每次status/rollback均根据transactionId枚举attempt1...3派生label；对所有有submit intent、loaded label或managed PID的attempt依次reconcile→identity-bound bootout→等label unloaded→managed PID消失→probe-parent/absent oracle重验，才可`quiescent=true`。任一unknown label/PID/executable/probe residue直接manual，不把launchd job当作coordinator child group。

old 0.35.1 与candidate 0.36.0 的permission HTTP protocol必须分开：plan/rollback面对old helper时固定用legacy-safe `GET /permission-probe?root=<workspace>&full=false`，`profile_root`必须absent，从不设`full=true`，因此只走old lightweight分支且0 write；swap后candidate才要求`GET /permission-probe` empty query，任何root/profile/full query是closed400，POST是405。其他exact request为unauthenticated `GET /health` empty query，authenticated lightweight `GET /bar/status?root=<workspace>&full=false`，authenticated full `GET /bar/status?root=<workspace>&profile_root=<profile>&full=true`，authenticated `GET /task-progress?root=<workspace>&limit=20&include_inactive=true&include_local=false`；wrong-token复用同一bar full URL并必须401。query按上述key顺序构造，value是strict UTF-8后RFC3986 percent-encoding，仅ALPHA/DIGIT/`-._~`不转义，space=`%20`而非`+`，hex uppercase；duplicate/unknown/missing key、顺序、encoding或method/path偏离都在fixture中fail closed。

permission job output basename按attempt固定为`permission-probe.<attempt>.stdout`与`permission-probe.<attempt>.stderr`，只能位于verified transaction directory。submit前顺序精确为：证明basename absent→分别`O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW` mode0600创建regular/current uid/nlink1 leaf→fsync file+dir→把两个identity SHA与attempt写journal→写submitIntent+fsync→`launchctl submit`。launchd运行前后leaf dev/ino/uid/mode/nlink必须不变，各自size≤64 KiB；stderr成功时empty，stdout canonical result。precreate→journal、journal→intent、intent→submit任一crash均由derived basename+identity reconcile；terminal只在result/termination与job quiescence已原子记录后identity-bound unlink+dir fsync。

`oldApiBaseline` 不hash动态full body，exact required=`health,bar,task,permission`、additionalProperties=false。health只能是ready projection=`{class:"ready",httpStatus:200,apiVersion,authRequired}`。bar/task/permission各自使用schema `oneOf`：

- ready bar=`{class:"ready",httpStatus:200,schemaVersion,safeForHarvisBar,runtimeVersion,sourceRuntimeVersion,stateClass,safetyRedacted,includesSecrets,includesLocalAbsolutePaths}`，排除generatedAt/lastTickAt/counts/summary；
- ready task=`{class:"ready",httpStatus:200,schemaVersion,safeForHarvisBar,statusClass,safetyRedacted,includesSecrets,includesLocalAbsolutePaths}`，排除items/count/time/summary；
- ready permission=`{class:"ready",httpStatus:200,permissionProbeVersion,okClass,writesSkipped,redacted,includesLocalAbsolutePaths}`，排除legacy root/checks/message；
- permission-degraded projection对三endpoint分别exact=`{class:"permissionDegraded",httpStatus,endpoint,errorCode,bodyPrivacyClass:"knownPermissionNoPath"}`。它只能来自≤1MiB strict-UTF8 JSON envelope exact keys`ok,endpoint,data,warnings,error`：`ok=false,data=null,warnings=[]`，endpoint逐项const `/bar/status|/task-progress|/permission-probe`，error exact keys=`code,message`，且只接受两个conditional pair之一：`httpStatus=400,code="invalid_request",message="Operation not permitted (os error 1)"`或`httpStatus=403,code="permission_denied",message="filesystem permission denied"`。任何extra/missing key、不同大小写/空白/message、control、本机workspace/profile/account-home absolute-byte substring、lookalike权限文案或其他4xx/5xx都拒绝，raw body/message只在ephemeral parser内存在，不进projection/journal/error/log/evidence。

stateClass/statusClass/okClass是docs/02 closed classifier，不保留raw error。old restore要求每endpoint static projection exact；ready不可退为degraded，原本degraded只能恢复为same degraded projection或对应ready（严格改善），不能换endpoint/code/status。Tick freshness仍用即时lastTickAt独立barrier。candidate pending仍要求bar/task/permission全ready，不接受legacy degraded。captured-redacted真实0.35.1 task HTTP400 fixture与400 lookalike、non-permission、path leak negatives是硬门。

old 0.35.1 lightweight permission response存在已知遗留缺陷：它可能含一个`root` 字段，必须逐byte等于本次request中已验证workspace authority，且只能在ephemeral parser内用于一致性检查，不进DTO/journal/error/log/evidence/result。plan仅对这一个exact known field容忍legacy privacy缺陷，其他local path仍阻断；candidate empty-query permission response必须移除`root`且`includesLocalAbsolutePaths=false`为true statement。

Oracle/authority-inspect error到transaction result的映射固定：plan capture阶段的preference/source/Git/index/tracked/schema/authority/config/output validation失败→exit3 `VALIDATION_FAILED/plan`并写aborted；plan runner timeout/overflow/I/O→exit3 `VALIDATION_FAILED/plan`，只有经closed classifier证明的内部invariant failure在0 protected mutation时exit9 `INTERNAL_FAILURE/plan`。apply开始protected mutation前compare drift→exit3 `PLAN_DRIFT/prepare`并aborted，journal rootCauseStage可记录`verify`；mutation开始后或pending status的Oracle mismatch/timeout/overflow/internal先记根因event，再按recovery table成功时exit6 `ROLLED_BACK`、不可证时exit7 `MANUAL_RECOVERY_REQUIRED`。sourceRebaseline fingerprint类错误exit4，preview facts drift不属于fingerprint mismatch，而属status rollback/manual。任何raw Oracle/authority error/message/stderr不出transaction result；HVM-DEP-003/011/012逐error class验证exit/state/stage/event parity。

| journal state/checkpoint | candidate quiesce prelude | cleanup / reverse set | old-chain postcondition | manual-only condition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `planned` / `prepared` + partial staging | 枚举并quiesce probe attempts；App/Tick/HTTP尚未candidate intent时no-op | 只删manifest-bound partial next；0 reverse swap | old services/App保持plan baseline | partial path/identity/manifest无法证明 |
| `appStopped` / `servicesStopped` pre-swap | probe jobs quiescent；确认candidate App/Tick/HTTP 0 | 删partial/complete next；0 reverse swap | 依wasRunning恢复old HTTP→Tick→App | service/PID state无法reconcile |
| `appSwapped` | 停candidate App（如出现）；probe/Tick/HTTP 0 | reverse App | old HTTP lightweight→即时baseline后old Tick→old App | App tuple不是closed legal state |
| `helperSwapped` | 停candidate App；quiesce probes；bootout Tick→HTTP | reverse helper→App | 同上，old worker call count=0 | helper/App任一identity或parent不可证 |
| `httpStarted` / `verifying` / `awaitingFDAApproval` | 停candidate App；quiesceattempt1...3；bootout Tick→HTTP | reverse helper→App | old HTTP four lightweight→old Tick→old App | HTTP/probe PID、label、output/probe residue不确定 |
| `tickStarted` | 停candidate App；quiesce probes；bootout candidate Tick→HTTP | reverse helper→App | old HTTP→capture immediate lastTickAt→old Tick strict progress→old App | Tick PID/restart/label无法证明 |
| `candidateAppStarted` | exact terminate candidate App到two-zero；再probe→Tick→HTTP | reverse helper→App | old HTTP→old Tick→按wasRunning old App | App multi/PID reuse/35s内无two-zero |
| `pendingUserAcceptance` | 同candidateAppStarted；最后一次fresh status drift也用本行 | reverse helper→App；保留journal/result/evidence和non-reversible-effects truth | old HTTP→old Tick→old App，old worker=0 | Oracle/tuple/quiescence/old-chain任一不可证 |
| any `*Intent` without done / syscall outcome unknown | 先launchctl+libproc+fd tuple reconcile，不重放syscall | 只按观测到的closed tuple清理/逆swap | 恢复后跑完old chain | 观测不属任一closed tuple |
| `committing` | 不进入rollback prelude | 只从operationCursor继续helper→App rollback-sibling cleanup | candidate service/App继续当前verified state | cleanup identity不可证时`commitCleanupManual`；绝不rollback |

所有可达checkpoint必须恰好匹配表中一行；malformed boolean/tuple/intent组合或多行同时匹配均`manualRecoveryRequired`。HVM-DEP-005/006/008/009 逐行fault-inject，不允许用一条generic rollback测试代替。

### 9.4 FDA、source oracle、local checkpoint 与 final commit

Oracle result中`transactionId`永远是32-lower-hex，`captureId`则是required `null|32-lower-hex`；下文“transactionId/captureId为32-lower-hex”只是non-null分支简称，不得覆盖§9.4 capture/error conditional。

Oracle-only Git roster中ref command exact为`for-each-ref --sort=refname --format=%(refname)%00%(objectname)%00 refs`，每record raw grammar是validated full ref bytes→NUL→lowercase 40/64-hex OID→NUL→LF，按ref raw byte严格递增、无duplicate/trailing fragment。`ls-files -z --cached --stage` 每record exact grammar是`mode SP oid SP stage TAB path NUL`；mode只`100644|100755`，oid必须与本repository object format一致的40或64 lowercase hex，stage exact `0`，path是nonempty opaque raw bytes，必须relative、不以`/`开头/结尾、无empty/`.`/`..` component，按raw path byte严格递增且唯一；non-UTF8、LF/TAB在path内可用，只由NUL结束。wrong field separator/OID width/hash case/stage/order/duplicate/trailing partial均`INDEX_INVALID`，不进入openat。fixed vectors覆盖SHA-1/SHA-256 repo、non-UTF8/newline路径和malformed corpus。

source preference authority exact为application ID `local.harzva.harvisbar`、key `harvisBar.harvisSourceRoot`、scope `kCFPreferencesCurrentUser/kCFPreferencesAnyHost`。Phase2 `HarvisSourcePreferenceStore` 只用`CFPreferencesSetValue/CFPreferencesSynchronize`读写该domain，Oracle只用同domain的`CFPreferencesSynchronize`→`CFPreferencesCopyValue`→synchronize→copy，两次必须是同一raw String或同为null。不得用`Bundle.main.bundleIdentifier`、`UserDefaults.standard`、Oracle自己的domain、currentHost或`defaults` CLI；wrong-domain/host/user fixture必须证明不会误读。

Oracle 仅从`getpwuid(getuid())` 派生fixed transaction root，用§9.2 owner/journal root-anchored no-follow/cap/schema/revision规则打开`<transactionId>`；不从cwd/env/defaults/argv接受任何root或scope。它必须重算owner canonical SHA并与journal immutable `creationNonce/ownerSha256`交叉验证。`capture`只允许journal `planConstructionState=awaitingOracle`，`compare/rebaseline`只允许planConstructionState=complete且apply/status调用所需的closed states；coordinator保持exclusive transaction lock并在spawn前写command intent/revision，Oracle验证journal intent/state/revision后只读。`approvedRuntimeScope` 只能从journal-bound `oracleInput.scopeDigests`与`oracleInput.authorityInspectFingerprint`派生；complete后它必须与`planSnapshot.approvedEffects.runtimeManagedWriteScope`逐项相等。Oracle不自行重解析raw Tick path，不把raw scope写result。intent/state/revision/owner nonce或hash/scope任一不一致返回`INVALID_STATE`且0 write。

local provenance checkpoint 只包含可构建的产品源码、schema、tests、package/docs/skill contract，不包含`docs/harvis-version-management/**`。后者是本机开发记忆/验收路由册，精确允许的untracked roster为`01-需求分析.md,02-架构设计.md,03-开发计划.md,04-闭环测试矩阵.md,NEXT_GOAL.md,phase3-acceptance.md,phase3-evidence-index.json,05-验收报告.md`；前五项从Phase0开始存在，后三项只能在对应Phase生成。每个现存项必须current-uid regular/nlink1/non-executable/≤2 MiB，目录与文件均no-follow；这些文件永不stage，Oracle的`--untracked-files=no`也不会把它们冒充receipt source tree。

产品变化使用另一个exact `ownedChangeRoster`，不能把“当前已tracked”误当成唯一正例。它只能由docs/03已完成Phase 1–3 Ownership中逐字存在的产品/source/schema/test/skill path生成，排除上述planning docs与phase evidence；每项exact为`{pathB64,changeKind,baselineMode,baselineOid,targetMode,targetContentSha256,targetBlobOid}`、additionalProperties=false，按decode后的raw relative path byte严格递增。pathB64是canonical padded RFC4648，decode后nonempty、无NUL/empty/`.`/`..` component且非absolute；`modified`要求baseline二项与targetMode/content non-null，`added`要求baseline二项null而targetMode/content non-null，`deleted`要求baseline non-null而targetMode/content/blob全null。prepared stage时added/modified的targetBlobOid必须null；只有alternate add完成后才从validated `ls-files --stage`对应entry取得object-format匹配的OID并在alternateIndexReady revision exactly-once填为non-null，后续immutable。这样不要求实现者在Git attributes/eol归一化前猜blob算法。added leaf必须root-anchored no-follow current-uid regular/nlink1、mode100644或100755、≤128MiB；deleted必须在baseline index存在且worktree stable ENOENT；modified前后metadata/content按Phase1 reader闭合。raw status中每个modified/added/deleted path必须与该roster一一相等；roster外dirty/untracked/deleted、Ownership未完成项、symlink/special/目录添加全部阻断。alternate `add --`使用全部sorted roster path（包含new/deleted），随后tree diff必须只含同一roster且每项mode/OID与resolved target exact；added在post real index成为stage0/H，deleted消失。每代postcondition是real index与tracked worktree clean，untracked只剩fixed planning roster当时已存在子集。candidate receipt的sourceCommit/sourceTree必须精确等于该candidate generation完成后的HEAD/tree。

checkpoint source authority在Phase 0锁为full ref `refs/heads/codex/harvisbar-permission-recovery-20260829`、initial HEAD `6a9a9e3d1ff49381c09f36d2b818e6d2bc788ec0`、initial tree `3bd29299cf2526fe604f2fabab960924f38bc056`。每次generation开始、每个object/ref/index mutation前后及完成发布前，`symbolic-ref -q HEAD`必须exit0并返回该full ref+LF；`rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD`必须等于initial HEAD或前一completed generation commit；`rev-list --parents -n 1 <validated-head>`必须exact为commit+一个parent（拒root/merge）。git-dir内fixed `MERGE_HEAD,CHERRY_PICK_HEAD,REVERT_HEAD,BISECT_START,rebase-apply,rebase-merge,sequencer`逐项no-follow stable-ENOENT；detached/unborn/wrong-ref/rebase/merge/cherry-pick/revert/bisect/branch漂移全部在object mutation前阻断。不得checkout/switch/create/delete branch。

checkpoint Git common prefix exact为 `[/usr/bin/git,--no-pager,--no-replace-objects,--literal-pathspecs,-C,.,--work-tree=.,-c,protocol.allow=never,-c,core.hooksPath=/dev/null,-c,core.fsmonitor=false,-c,core.splitIndex=false,-c,index.sparse=false,-c,commit.gpgSign=false,-c,tag.gpgSign=false,-c,gc.auto=0,-c,maintenance.auto=false]`，`.`由topLevelFD解释并覆盖local core.worktree/bare；共享sanitized env/fd-cwd/30秒deadline/每流1MiB cap。每轮先复用§5.3 active-scope unsafe-config preflight，再执行closed read-only tails：`symbolic-ref -q HEAD`、`rev-parse --verify --quiet <validated-ref-or-oid>`、`rev-parse --verify --quiet <validated-oid>^{tree|commit}`、`rev-parse --shared-index-path`、`ls-files -z --cached --stage`、`ls-files -v -z --cached`、`diff --cached --name-only -z`、`status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=all`、`for-each-ref --sort=refname --format=%(refname)%00%(objectname)%00 refs/remotes refs/tags`、`config --local --null --list`、`rev-list --parents -n 1 <validated-oid>`。config/ref raw bytes只在内存hash。real与alternate index均要求shared-index-path exit0+stdout exact empty、无link/sdir extension、git-dir无新`sharedindex.*`；stage/flag roster要求所有tracked entry唯一stage0、mode100644/100755、tag exact`H`。lowercase assume-unchanged、`S` skip-worktree、duplicate/order/roster/terminator失配都在首个object mutation前阻断。preexisting staged、split/sparse index、Ownership外tracked drift或roster外untracked同样阻断。

checkpoint不直接在real index上`add`。fixed state root只能从`getpwuid(getuid())` account home派生为literal suffix`Library/Application Support/HarvisBar/SourceCheckpoints`；逐component mkdirat/openat/no-follow/owner/mode/fd-entry/fsync规则exact复用§9.2 InstallTransactions suffix，最终root exact0700，mkdir后crash留下的verified empty suffix可接受。root direct children exact为`source-checkpoint.lock,source-checkpoint-init.json,source-checkpoint-init.next,repository.json,repository.next,generations`；§9.4.2完成后再条件加入fixed distribution-runs/candidates，任何其他entry阻断。root与`generations`为current-uid directory mode0700；lock为regular/current-uid/mode0600/nlink1/size0，按§9.2同样O_EXCL-create-or-verified-existing、nonblocking flock及post-lock identity recheck。published generation basename只允许`01`...`08`；active init另可唯一允许derived temp basename`<NN>.<creationNonce>.next`，其他temp拒绝。published generation基础/conditional direct roster、mode与cap完全以§9.4.2最终定义为准，旧fixed `verification-result.next`不合法；generation dir/owner/journal分别0700/0600/0600，alternate index mode与baseline real index exact相等，verification/manifests为0600。root最多读取其final roster+1、generations最多读取`9 + 1`项（8 published+最多1 active temp），cap+1或unexpected entry固定validation failure且0新mutation。

`repository.json`是immutable canonical authority，required exact=`schemaVersion,creationNonce,repositoryIdentitySha256,branchRef,initialHead,initialTree`、additionalProperties=false；creationNonce固定为首次repository publish nonce。repository identity framing唯一为`SHA256("harvis-source-repository-v1\0" || u32be(objectFormatBytes) || ASCII(objectFormat) || u32be(branchBytes) || ASCII(fullRef) || u32be(recordCount) || records)`；objectFormat只`sha1|sha256`。records按kind`topLevel=1,gitDir=2,commonDir=3`、各chain root→leaf固定顺序，每条exact bytes=`u8(kind)||u32be(componentIndex)||u64be(dev)||u64be(ino)||u32be(mode)`；recordCount≤1024，raw path不入framing/journal。

源checkpoint schema的版本字面不可由实施者自选：`repository.json.schemaVersion` const=`harvis.source-checkpoint-repository.v1`，init const=`harvis.source-checkpoint-init.v1`，owner const=`harvis.source-checkpoint-owner.v1`，generation journal const=`harvis.source-checkpoint-generation.v1`，checkpoint CLI result const=`harvis.source-checkpoint-result.v1`，verification result const=`harvis.source-checkpoint-verification.v1`；六类object均required `schemaVersion`且`additionalProperties=false`。

首次authority/每代publish由root-level `source-checkpoint-init.json/.next`保护；init required exact=`schemaVersion,creationNonce,generation,repositoryCreationNeeded,stage,repositorySha256,ownerSha256,journalSha256,journalRevision`，nonce为32-lower-hex、generation=1...8、bool只首代true，stage=`allocated|repositoryCommitted|generationPrepared|published`。allocated时owner/journal/revision全null，repositorySha在create-needed时null、existing时non-null；repositoryCommitted时repositorySha non-null；generationPrepared/published时owner/journal non-null且revision=1。owner required exact=`schemaVersion,creationNonce,generation,repositorySha256`。creation exact顺序为：(1)提交allocated init；(2)首代O_EXCL repository.next，内容含同nonce，write+fsync+rename+root fsync，后提交repositoryCommitted；后代只重验existing repository并提交repositoryCommitted；(3)在generations下O_EXCL mkdir derived temp dir，直接O_EXCL写/fsync owner.json与revision1 prepared journal（整个temp尚未published，partial可按nonce安全清理），fsync temp dir并提交generationPrepared hashes；(4)`renameatx_np(temp,<NN>,RENAME_EXCL)`+generations fsync，提交published；(5)unlink init+root fsync。不存在generationCreated/ownerCommitted这类effect→stage gap。published后journal更新才使用journal.next；temp构造不需要owner.next/journal.next。

mutating `prepare|reject|bind-transaction|verifyDistribution`取得lock后先按各自closed state恢复init/next；`status`严格0 write，遇到任何init/next、generation journal.next、run-bound manifest.next或derived verification next只返回recovery-required，不清理、不晋升。base init absent时verified init.next无论partial/malformed都identity-bound删+root fsync；base存在时init.next只删不晋升。repository base absent时，只有create-needed allocated init存在才可删除derived repository.next并重算；repository base存在时verified repository.next一律删且base SHA/nonce必须等于authority。published generation的journal.next只按§9.2删除重算；run-bound manifests/verification temp只能由同runId的verifyDistribution按§9.4.2 instance/manifest/prefix状态机恢复，其他命令不得触碰。alternate.index.lock只属于该generation的Git alternate-index runner：prepared或failed alternate step可identity-bound删alternate.index/lock重建；alternateIndexReady以后lock必须absent、alternate SHA exact，任何额外sharedindex拒绝。

init recovery按observed prefix唯一收敛：(a)allocated/create-needed且repo absent：清partial repository.next/temp后重跑；若repository.json已由rename发布且其nonce/content/hash exact，则直接提交repositoryCommitted；(b)allocated/existing或repositoryCommitted：repository必须exact；derived temp absent可重建，partial temp因basename含nonce可整目录fd-postorder清理重建，owner+revision1 journal完整且可重算hash时即使init尚是repositoryCommitted也可提交generationPrepared；(c)generationPrepared：temp完整则RENAME_EXCL发布，fixed `<NN>`已存在且temp absent时只有owner/journal nonce/hash/revision exact才视为rename已发生并提交published；(d)published只在fixed generation四项binding匹配时删init。fixed generation与temp同时存在、prefix外entry、identity/hash/revision不符或无法证明nonce时保留全部并exit7。每个repository rename、temp owner/journal fsync、generation rename、init stage write、unlink/fsync前后fault重放必须收敛同一后态。

`schemas/harvis-source-checkpoint.schema.json`的generation journal required exact=`schemaVersion,creationNonce,ownerSha256,revision,generation,branchRef,repositoryIdentitySha256,baselineHead,baselineTree,baselineIndexSha256,ownedChangeRoster,authorEpoch,stage,alternateIndexSha256,expectedIndexSha256,expectedTree,commitOid,indexTempIdentitySha256,disposition,evidenceFingerprint,transactionIdDigest`、additionalProperties=false。`ownedChangeRoster`只能引用§9.4上文同名exact def，不得另建path/mode旧别名。stage closed=`prepared|alternateIndexReady|treeWritten|commitWritten|indexPublishIntent|indexTempReady|indexLockReady|refUpdated|indexUpdated|completed`：prepared时五个derived SHA/OID全null；alternateIndexReady起alternateIndexSha256+expectedIndexSha256 non-null且added/modified targetBlobOid完成exactly-once null→OID；treeWritten起expectedTree non-null；commitWritten起commitOid non-null；indexTempReady起indexTempIdentitySha256 non-null，后态全部保持exact不变。path/kind/baseline/targetMode/content从revision1 immutable，只有targetBlobOid上述一次转移。disposition=`pending|rejectedPreDeployment|rolledBack|aborted|candidatePending|accepted`；stage非completed时必须pending/evidence+transaction null；completed+pending两者仍null；其余disposition evidenceFingerprint non-null，transactionIdDigest只在rolledBack/aborted/candidatePending/accepted时non-null。revision从1开始每次exact+1。第9代固定阻断并要求修订计划。

disposition evidence不留自定义hash：transactionIdDigest=`SHA256("harvis-source-checkpoint-transaction-id-v1\0" || raw16TransactionIdBytes)` bare hex。install-backed evidenceFingerprint=`sha256:`+SHA256(canonical ASCII JSON of exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.source-checkpoint-install-evidence.v1",generation,transactionIdDigest,installJournalRevision,state,installedReceiptSha256,candidateAppTreeSha256}`)，字段均从verified committed install journal投影；candidatePending→accepted只能在同transaction digest上以更高revision和state committed exactly-once更新fingerprint。distribution verification 不在本段保留旧failure-only DTO：`verification-result.json`的passed/failed exact schema、pre-intent derived next、commands/evidence fingerprints与publish recovery只认§9.4.2最终定义；reject不生成或修改artifact，只验证argument、artifact fingerprint与current generation。raw command/path/output不进入artifact。

public `scripts/harvis_source_checkpoint.sh`只用fixed `#!/bin/sh`与verified同目录coordinator，exact exec isolated `/usr/bin/python3 -I -B -S`，环境/自校验规则复用§9.1 thin entry。source authority不来自cwd/env/defaults/argv：thin entry与coordinator先root-walk自身regular/nlink1/not-writable identity，从其fixed `scripts/` parent向上只用Phase1 bootstrap确定canonical top-level，再要求两脚本正是该top-level下Ownership path并绑定repository identity；同branch/HEAD/tree的另一clone、wrong cwd或脚本alias均拒绝。closed CLI只有`prepare`、`status --generation <1...8>`、`reject --generation <n> --reason <build|test|package> --evidence-fingerprint sha256:<64-lower-hex>`、`bind-transaction --generation <n> --transaction-id <32-lower-hex>`；无source/root/index path参数。prepare若有published未完成generation就只resume，只有前代disposition允许时才创建下一代。reject只验证fixed verification result并转rejectedPreDeployment。bind-transaction只读fixed install journal并按其terminal state唯一映射`aborted→aborted,rolledBack→rolledBack,pendingUserAcceptance→candidatePending,committed→accepted`；candidatePending replay在journal仍pending时idempotent，变committed后同命令唯一推进accepted，其他state拒绝。

checkpoint CLI stdout同样single canonical JSON+LF/stderr empty，result exact required=`schemaVersion,command,ok,generation,stage,disposition,head,tree,retryable,error`、additionalProperties=false；error=null或exact`{code,stage}`，code=`USAGE|VALIDATION_FAILED|JOURNAL_INVALID|INVALID_STATE|CONCURRENT_TRANSACTION|RECOVERY_REQUIRED|INTERNAL_FAILURE`，stage=`usage|prepare|status|disposition`。schema oneOf按observable shape锁：(a)success command=`prepare|status|reject|bind-transaction`,ok=true,retryable=false,error=null，generation/stage/disposition/head/tree全non-null且exact journal；(b)usage command=unknown/exit2/ok=false/retryable=false/五facts全null/error=USAGE+usage；(c)no-journal failure known command/exit3或9/五facts全null；(d)trusted-journal failure known command/exit3,5,7或9/五facts exact current journal。code mapping固定：validation/journal/state exit3且retryable false；concurrent exit5且唯一retryable true；recovery exit7 false；internal只在0 source-Git mutation exit9 false。prepare error stage=prepare、status=status、reject/bind=disposition，未列code×command×stage拒绝。status严格0 write；prepare/reject/bind idempotent replay返回同facts，若revision因合法recovery推进则返回新canonical bytes。HVM-DEP-013逐command/result/exit/schema match-count=1、first-init/replay/wrong-cwd/same-commit-clone golden。

每代exact state machine是：(1) published revision1 `prepared`绑定baseline branch/HEAD/tree/real-index SHA、repo identity及ownedChangeRoster；(2)generation-owned alternate index使用唯一额外env`GIT_INDEX_FILE=<verified-generation-index>`运行`read-tree <baselineTree>`→`add -- <全部sorted decoded ownedChangeRoster paths>`，new/deleted都包含；Git的generation-owned`alternate.index.lock`若crash只按上段规则清理。验证alternate shared-index empty、stage/flag roster，从validated stage entry回填targetBlobOid，并在同一次journal update锁`alternateIndexSha256=expectedIndexSha256`后写alternateIndexReady；(3)alternate `write-tree`返回exact OID并写treeWritten；(4)fixed ASCII identity和journal-bound epoch执行`commit-tree <expectedTree> -p <baselineHead> -m "feat(harvisbar): add local Harvis version management"`，写commitWritten。

(5)验证git-dir real `index`为regular/current uid/nlink1、split-index absent、mode受限0600|0644且hash=baseline，标准`index.lock`与derived `harvis-source-index-<creationNonce>.tmp`都stable ENOENT，先写indexPublishIntent。随后只创建derived temp：`openat(gitDirFD,O_RDWR|O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW|O_CLOEXEC,<baselineMode>)`，立即`fchmod(fd,baselineMode)`消除hostile umask并fstat exact，把verified alternate index bytes有界复制、fsync、重验SHA/size/identity；将temp identity canonical SHA写journal并进indexTempReady。再用`renameatx_np(derivedTemp,index.lock,RENAME_EXCL)`，保持同一open fd、fsync git-dir、证明standard lock entry等于recorded temp inode/hash，写indexLockReady。若别的Git已占`index.lock`，RENAME_EXCL失败且绝不删除它；只可清理自身derived temp并返回concurrent。

(6)持有已发布standard lock的同一fd时重读real index identity/hash和branch/owned bytes仍为baseline，执行`update-ref <exact-full-ref> <commitOid> <baselineHead>` CAS并写refUpdated；(7)再次证明real index仍为同一baseline inode/hash、standard lock仍为recorded temp identity/expected SHA、branch=commitOid，然后`renameat(index.lock,index)`+git-dir fsync，重开real index要求hash=expectedIndexSha/全H exact target roster，写indexUpdated→completed。这里不对real index运行Git read-tree；并发Git add要么在temp/lock publish前完成而被baseline recheck拒绝，要么看到standard lock而失败，不能被静默覆盖。ref CAS失败且branch仍baseline时只可identity-bound删自己的standard lock；branch已推进必须继续index publish。

crash recovery只接受closed observed pair。branch=baseline+real index baseline时：(a)indexPublishIntent下derived temp partial可因journal-bound nonce安全删/重建；(b)indexTempReady下derived temp必须匹配recorded identity；(c)若temp rename已发生而journal仍indexTempReady，standard index.lock必须匹配同一recorded inode/hash且derived temp absent，才可补写indexLockReady；任何preexisting/wrong standard lock都只报concurrent/recovery，绝不删。branch=commitOid+real index baseline+our standard lock expected时只可rename/fsync；branch=commitOid+real index expected时直接以预记录expectedIndexSha256+expected tree/full semantic roster/clean status晋升indexUpdated/completed；completed且三者exact返回already-complete。branch=baseline但real index expected、branch=commit而index既非baseline也非expected、wrong lock/temp、owned bytes drift或其他组合全部recovery-required，绝不reset/unstage/覆盖/删除用户bytes。fault fixture覆盖hostile umask、alternate lock、derived temp create/partial/copy/fsync、temp→standard RENAME_EXCL前后、concurrent add/index.lock、CAS前后、index rename/fsync前后及每次journal写，证明每代只有一个reachable single-parent commit。

Phase3允许最多8个checkpoint generation以支持真实candidate remediation。generation 1从initial HEAD开始；后一代baseline必须是前一completed commit，且前一disposition已由verified fixed emitter给出rejectedPreDeployment，或verified install journal给出aborted/rolledBack；candidatePending时禁止新代，Phase4 committed后只可把同代标accepted。前代commit不amend/reset/rewrite。每一失败代只留脱敏evidence fingerprint与transaction ID digest；final candidate receipt只允许指向唯一candidatePending/accepted的latest generation HEAD/tree。

Phase 3 的真实raw screenshot/AX/API evidence只在transaction-owned guarded directory内短期存活；CU完成后立即identity-bound cleanup，只把kind/digest/dimensions/assertion/cleanup receipt写入untracked `phase3-evidence-index.json`和`phase3-acceptance.md`。Phase 3 在`pendingUserAcceptance`收尾后回写03/NEXT不会触发Oracle tracked drift。Phase 4 的严格顺序是：先完成最后一次CU；若有sourceBindingCandidate，先消费sourceRebaseline并fresh compare；再调用fresh `status`、向用户展示并获得one-shot acceptance Approval；执行`commit`或必要的`commitResume`直到`committed`；最后才写untracked 05/NEXT、将overall Goal关闭。未到`committed`不得把Phase4/整体Goal标记完成。

`HarvisVersionAcceptanceOracle` 的package authority不留实施选择：`Package.swift` 新增 executable product/target `HarvisVersionAcceptanceOracle`，只依赖`HarvisVersionCore`；HarvisBarTests target同时显式依赖HarvisBar与Core。packager从本checkpoint source的独立scratch Release产物读取Oracle，在App整体签名前以O_EXCL/no-follow复制到`Contents/Resources/HarvisVersionAcceptanceOracle`，要求Mach-O regular/current uid/nlink1/mode0755/size 1...128 MiB，并绑定bare SHA进package plan/App-tree fingerprint。bundle contract 的Resources exact allowlist新增该一个fixed leaf；rogue extra/缺失/symlink/hardlink/wrong mode/non-Mach-O/tamper全拒绝。它不是可装载runtime payload，也不增加receipt的第四artifact role；receipt仍exact三角色，Oracle 由sealed App-tree digest、fixed allowlist、五组件codesign matrix与`codesign --verify --deep --strict`共同绑定。package/provenance/path-guard tests必须证明build→copy→sign→verify的SHA一致，且mixed signer、post-sign tamper与rogue resource fail closed。

Oracle `captureId` 分支规则精确为：`capture`成功新生16-byte random id；`compare` equal必须echo输入baseline capture id；`compare` 产生`sourceBindingCandidate`时才新生preview id；`rebaseline` 必须echo输入preview id。result中captureId是required-but-nullable；所有失败一律为null，即使compare/rebaseline输入已有trusted id也不echo，只有成功分支non-null。equal compare 以baseline canonical facts重算同oracleFingerprint；rebaseline 只有current facts与preview oracleFingerprint exact相等才返回equal。四个成功分支与所有failure code/stage都有fixed-random golden，禁止“每次随机id”导致假drift。

permission worker失败默认automatic rollback。`prompt`只允许interactive TTY；同一apply process/lock写 `awaitingFDAApproval`，通过root-anchored verified `/dev/tty`向同一TTY写入并flush one-shot exact ASCII行 `APPROVE-FDA <fingerprint>\n`，再只从该TTY最多120秒读取 exact 同行作为ack。TTY摘要、两份FDA payload与approvedEffects都必须明确`manualTCCChange=true`且`rollbackDoesNotRevertManualTCCChange=true`。consume后才执行 exact `/usr/bin/open x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security?Privacy_AllFiles`（10秒、每流1 MiB、exit0），随后向同一TTY写/flush `FDA-DONE <fingerprint>\n`并最多600秒等待exact ack。stdout直到terminal只写§9.1唯一result JSON行，stderr始终empty；TTY missing/open/read/write/flush/encoding失败按FDA failure rollback。收到ack后先运行新的one-shot candidate worker并要求成功；然后bootout仍在授权前启动的candidate HTTP，identity-bound等待旧PID消失，再bootstrap同一candidate helper为全新PID，依次重跑health/auth-required、wrong-token401、bar-status、task-progress和permission-lightweight全部contract，确认原`Operation not permitted`路径已局部恢复，之后才bootstrap Tick。decline/EOF/timeout/open/worker/restart/API复测任一失败都rollback。禁止tccutil、用户URL、预批准、后台awaiting状态与fixed sleep；fake runner/ApprovalIO/monotonic clock逐argv、TTY bytes、stdout隔离、PID转换和TTL验证。rollback不尝试撤销用户在System Settings中的TCC授权，只恢复App/helper/service/App-process与可回滚文件状态并在结果中保留该事实。

coordinator完全不读取production defaults或source Git，而是在持有transaction lock时同步spawn fixed candidate-bundle Oracle；Oracle不获取lock、不写journal，coordinator逐schema验结果后才原子写artifact/journal，避免递归CLI/deadlock。共享代码拓扑固定为Foundation/Darwin/CryptoKit system-framework-only、0 third-party的 `HarvisVersionCore` library target；HarvisBar与`HarvisVersionAcceptanceOracle`两个executable target都依赖它，Core不得import SwiftUI/AppKit/Combine，Oracle不得依赖`@main` HarvisBar executable。Oracle源码是`apps/HarvisBar/Sources/HarvisVersionAcceptanceOracle/main.swift`，打包到candidate fixed relative path并由App tree/signing/plan SHA绑定。

Oracle closed argv只有`capture --transaction-id <id>`、`compare --transaction-id <id> --capture-id <id>`、`rebaseline --transaction-id <id> --capture-id <preview-id>`；command由plan（transaction已创建、Approval尚未发布）、apply pending transition、pending status/rebaseline内部调用，用户不直接插入plan中间态。每次完全替换为Phase1 Git env/prefix，shared 60秒deadline、每Git stream4MiB、总16MiB、tracked count≤20,000、单file≤128MiB、tracked total≤1GiB。`schemas/harvis-version-acceptance-oracle.schema.json`的result required exact=`schemaVersion,command,comparisonState,ok,transactionId,captureId,sourcePreference,repository,approvedRuntimeScope,oracleFingerprint,error`、additionalProperties=false：schema const=`harvis.version-acceptance-oracle.v1`；command=`capture|compare|rebaseline`；transactionId始终为validated 32-lower-hex，captureId为null或32-lower-hex。sourcePreference只允许null或exact `{state:unbound|bound,valueDigest:null|bareSha256}`：unbound↔valueDigest null，bound↔valueDigest non-null，双向锁定。repository只允许null或required exact=`topLevelPathDigest,headState,headRef,headCommit,refsSha256,configSha256,indexSha256,trackedRosterSha256,trackedContentSha256,trackedCount`、additionalProperties=false，digest均bare、count0...20000；HEAD nested oneOf只允许attached=`headRef validated full refs/heads + headCommit nonzero 40|64-lower-hex`、detached=`headRef null + headCommit nonzero OID`、unborn=`headRef validated full refs/heads + headCommit null`。approvedRuntimeScope只允许null或exact`{scopeDigests}`，scopeDigests按`resolvedStateRoot|profileWorkspaceFallback`固定顺序各含kind+bare pathDigest；oracleFingerprint为null或sha256Fingerprint。error为null或exact`{code,stage}`，code=`INVALID_STATE|PREFERENCE_UNSTABLE|SOURCE_INVALID|GIT_UNSAFE|GIT_CHANGED|INDEX_INVALID|TRACKED_FILE_INVALID|LIMIT_EXCEEDED|SCHEMA_INVALID|INTERNAL_FAILURE`、stage=`preferences|source|git|files|compare`。

Oracle result schema必须用下列nested discriminator oneOf，而非只做nullable type检查：(a)`capture-success-unbound|bound`：command=capture,ok=true,comparisonState=captured,error=null，新生captureId；(b)`compare-equal-unbound|bound`：command=compare,ok=true,comparisonState=equal,error=null，captureId exact echo trusted baseline；(c)`compare-source-binding-candidate`：command=compare,ok=true,comparisonState=sourceBindingCandidate,error=null，sourcePreference=bound/repository non-null并新生preview captureId；(d)`rebaseline-equal-bound`：command=rebaseline,ok=true,comparisonState=equal,error=null，captureId exact echo input preview，sourcePreference必须bound且repository non-null；不存在rebaseline-unbound成功分支；(e)`failure`：任一closed command,ok=false,comparisonState/captureId/sourcePreference/repository/approvedRuntimeScope/oracleFingerprint六项全部null且error non-null。每个success分支sourcePreference/approvedRuntimeScope/oracleFingerprint必须non-null；unbound分支repository必须null，bound分支repository必须non-null并满足HEAD oneOf。error code→stage也是schema const映射：`PREFERENCE_UNSTABLE→preferences`、`SOURCE_INVALID→source`、`GIT_UNSAFE|GIT_CHANGED|INDEX_INVALID→git`、`TRACKED_FILE_INVALID|LIMIT_EXCEEDED→files`、`INVALID_STATE|SCHEMA_INVALID|INTERNAL_FAILURE→compare`；未列code×stage拒绝。每个command×每个error固定stage都要canonical golden；failure绝不echo input captureId或保留prefix，success绝不带error/zero digest，negative golden必须拒绝rebaseline-unbound。

Oracle先以exact `CFPreferencesSynchronize(applicationID,kCFPreferencesCurrentUser,kCFPreferencesAnyHost)`→`CFPreferencesCopyValue(key,applicationID,kCFPreferencesCurrentUser,kCFPreferencesAnyHost)`→再次同参数synchronize→copy读取固定source key，两次raw String必须一致；禁止`CFPreferencesAppSynchronize`、`defaults`或Python plist。bound时复用Core source/marker/fd capture，并增加Oracle-only closed Git tails：`status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=no --ignore-submodules=all`必须empty；`ls-files -z --cached --stage`只接受unique stage0、mode100644/100755、opaque relative path且≤20k/4MiB；`symbolic-ref -q HEAD`与`rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD`按attached/detached/unborn exact组合解析（symbolic-ref exit0输出唯一full `refs/heads/...`+LF，exit1 empty；HEAD exit0唯一OID+LF，exit1 empty；stderr均empty，其他组合GIT_CHANGED）；`for-each-ref --sort=refname --format=%(refname)%00%(objectname)%00 refs`必须与§9.4开头的raw grammar完全一致；`config --includes --null --show-origin --list`只在内存hash并丢raw；`rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-path index`后root-anchored读regular/current-uid/nlink1/≤128MiB index。tracked roster中的每项用top-level fd no-follow打开regular/nlink1，逐file前后metadata+SHA并共享cap；不接受symlink/gitlink/conflict。refs/config/index/tracked roster/content只落aggregate digest。每次capture/compare内部运行两遍ordered query+file transcript并要求exact digests/roster，任一race fail closed。untracked/ignored files不属于receipt source tree且不由Oracle宣称保护；它们由checkpoint fixed local-planning roster与transaction runtime-scope canary分别管理，不能冒充tracked integrity。

Oracle aggregate digest使用唯一binary framing：`D(domain,parts)=SHA256("harvis-oracle-digest-v1\0" || u32be(domainByteCount) || ASCII(domain) || Σ(u64be(partByteCount)||part))`，不做Unicode normalization。valueDigest domain=`source-preference`、part为raw UTF-8 String；refs/config/index分别domain=`git-refs-output|git-config-output|git-index-bytes`且part为exact raw bytes。tracked records按opaque raw path bytes升序；roster每项parts=`pathBytes,ASCII(mode),ASCII(indexOID)`并以domain=`tracked-roster-record`先hash，再用有序record digest parts和domain=`tracked-roster`汇总；content每项parts=`pathBytes,ASCII(mode),u64be(size),raw32ByteFileSHA256`/domain=`tracked-content-record`后以`tracked-content`汇总。captureId来自16个cryptographic random bytes的lower-hex，不从path/clock派生。oracleFingerprint是`sha256:`+SHA256(canonical ASCII JSON of exact `{schemaVersion,transactionId,captureId,sourcePreference,repository,approvedRuntimeScope}`)，排除command/comparisonState/ok/error/self；artifactSha256是完整canonical result bytes的bare SHA。Swift Oracle与Python coordinator共享tracked fixed vectors，覆盖empty、NUL/LF、prefix collision、non-UTF8 path与不同record边界。
