allowed runtime scopes只从verified Tick plist、authority-inspect artifact与journal digests重建并绑定进Oracle fingerprint；实际runtime delta仍由coordinator canary验证。`outer-oracle.json`只含上述canonical aggregate结果，mode0600/nlink1/≤16MiB；journal的outerOracle binding同时锁captureId、artifact bare SHA与oracle fingerprint。plan内部capture后写artifact+binding再生成Approval；apply/status compare逐项重验。initial bound的preference/path/Git或任何tracked fact变化都由持锁coordinator立即按checkpoint table rollback（不能递归调用rollback CLI），失败则manual，绝不签acceptance。

initial unbound保持unbound可正常compare。若pending后用户从真实UI首次绑定，第一次status调用`compare`；Oracle只对该唯一transition返回ok=true/comparisonState=`sourceBindingCandidate`与new preview capture，绝不替换baseline。coordinator把canonical preview写`outer-oracle.preview.json`、绑定其capture/artifact/oracle到journal并生成`sourceRebaseline` continuation，不签acceptance。用户显式以`status --source-rebaseline-fingerprint`消费后，coordinator调用`rebaseline --capture-id <same-preview-id>`重新捕获；current oracle facts必须与preview oracleFingerprint exact相等，才原子以preview/new result替换baseline、identity-bound删除preview、记录`unboundToBound`并递增revision，再执行fresh compare/acceptance。initial bound→different bound、bound→unbound、未批准rebaseline、第二次rebaseline及preview drift全部拒绝并rollback/manual。commit只接受journal已记录transition与最后fresh status fingerprint。

Phase0收尾记录cached diff为空、tracked HEAD/tree等于上述initial authority、仅五份规划文档为本Goal untracked baseline。以后每个Phase开始时若已有staged项或owned file出现未归属前序Phase evidence的drift立即阻断请示；每个Phase收尾把本Goal实际changed path+blob SHA写入local-untracked脱敏evidence。Phase3源码/测试通过后只在上述专用full branch工作，不checkout/switch/create branch。checkpoint runner复用Phase1 fd-bound repository identity，30秒deadline、每流1MiB，并完全替换环境；base env exact为`PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin,HOME=/var/empty,XDG_CONFIG_HOME=/var/empty,LC_ALL=C,LANG=C,GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1,GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null,GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1,GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1,GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0,GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0,GIT_AUTHOR_NAME=Harvis Local Checkpoint,GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL=harvis-local-checkpoint@invalid,GIT_COMMITTER_NAME=Harvis Local Checkpoint,GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL=harvis-local-checkpoint@invalid`；只有alternate-index三条命令增加journal-derived `GIT_INDEX_FILE`，commit-tree增加journal-derived两项date，其他变量/merge一律拒绝。

本段任何“add→write-tree→commit”旧简称均由§9.4 alternate-index/CAS state machine完整取代；`docs/harvis-version-management/**`永不stage，postcondition是tracked worktree/real index clean加fixed local-untracked roster子集，不是全status empty。任一generation都不创建remote、不push/tag/publish；receipt sourceCommit/sourceTree必须等于latest candidate generation HEAD/tree再构建candidate。

成功状态 `pendingUserAcceptance` 保留 rollback siblings/journal；失败恢复原0.35.1 App/helper、plist/service/App state并复测API。`rollbackManual`允许identity可证明的人工授权rollback；一旦Phase4写入`committing`就不可rollback。final commit按helper rollback→App rollback清理：regular file仍要求nlink1，directory不要求nlink1而以recorded `(dev,ino)` visited/postorder；逐entry `openat/fstatat/unlinkat`，拒绝symlink/special/identity漂移，绝不用pathname递归或`shutil.rmtree`。每个intent/unlink/fsync/journal点fault inject。retryable失败保持committing，status签发15分钟one-shot commitResume；不可证明的partial tuple进入commitCleanupManual，只允许status/在人工修复后commit-resume，禁止rollback。两项cleanup done后写committed，保留脱敏final journal。全程0 remote/fetch/push/tag/publish/RepoAtlas write，证据只含kind/digest并通过private-path/credential scan。

#### 9.4.1 Source checkpoint 实施收口（覆盖本节前文简称）

本小节是源checkpoint、distribution failure emitter与acceptance Oracle的最终规范。与前文简称不一致时以本小节为准；不改变上文0 remote、不覆盖用户Git bytes、planning docs永不stage的边界。

**创建模式与签名临时文件。** transaction coordinator、source-checkpoint coordinator和`internal-permission-probe`使用两段self-authority顺序：先只用in-process syscall完成isolated flags、当前script/executable fd-chain、owner/mode/nlink、interpreter/sys.path基础验证，足以绑定正在执行的verified code；紧接着、任何subprocess和任何`mkdirat|openat(O_CREAT)`之前，各自单线程exactly-once设置并保持process `umask(0o077)`到exit。然后才允许运行只读Git/codesign/security self-authority subprocess完成第二段；第二段成功前仍禁止任何filesystem create、certificate extract、transaction/checkpoint publish或protected mutation。不恢复继承umask值；任何一段失败0 create。新建fixed directory/file仍分别请求0700/0600并在发布前fstat exact；只验证不chmod已存在leaf。certificate extract的每个DER leaf必须current uid、regular、nlink1、mode exact `0600|0644`，“mode writable”在此唯一指group/other writable；owner-write正例不得拒绝。hostile inherited umask `000|0777`必须与正常值得到同一新建mode与可恢复后态。

`security find-identity`的stdout grammar固定为0...N条identity line加一条summary，整体strict UTF-8/无CR/NUL/exact terminal LF。identity line exact为1...8个ASCII space、从1连续的decimal index、`) `、40-UPPER-HEX、space、双引号包围的1...512-byte display name（允许UTF-8，拒绝C0/DEL/CR/LF/NUL/未转义双引号）、LF；summary exact为1...8 spaces+decimal N+` valid identities found\n`。hash必须唯一、N等于line count；N=0是valid inventory但stable signer unavailable。extract与identity-display只针对前文fixed inner executable。

**schema 与result projection。** 六个source object的const分别为`harvis.source-checkpoint-repository.v1|harvis.source-checkpoint-init.v1|harvis.source-checkpoint-owner.v1|harvis.source-checkpoint-generation.v1|harvis.source-checkpoint-result.v1|harvis.source-checkpoint-verification.v1`；都required `schemaVersion`且`additionalProperties=false`。checkpoint result前文oneOf由下列完整取代：(a) success：known command/exit0/ok=true/retryable=false/error=null；(b) usage：command=unknown/exit2/ok=false/retryable=false/error=USAGE+usage；(c) no-authority：known command/exit3或9/ok=false/retryable=false；(d) untrusted-concurrent：known command/exit5/ok=false/retryable=true/error=CONCURRENT_TRANSACTION+对应command stage；(e) untrusted-recovery：known command/exit7/ok=false/retryable=false/error=RECOVERY_REQUIRED+对应command stage；(f) trusted-journal failure：known command/exit3,7或9/ok=false/retryable=false；(g) trusted-concurrent：known command/exit5/ok=false/retryable=true/error=CONCURRENT_TRANSACTION+对应command stage，只用于已持source lock且完整验证journal后在derived-temp→standard-lock `RENAME_EXCL`前/时发现他者的standard Git `index.lock`；branch/ref/index bytes已漂移仍是non-retryable validation/recovery，不得冒充concurrent。(b)–(e)在未取得lock或尚未完成owner/journal/revision完整验证时`generation,stage,disposition,head,tree`全null；抢锁失败禁止无锁读journal或echo argv generation。(a)/(f)/(g)五facts按唯一projection：`generation=journal.generation,stage=journal.stage,disposition=journal.disposition`；journal stage=`completed`时`head=commitOid,tree=expectedTree`，其他stage时`head=baselineHead,tree=baselineTree`。全shape必须恰好match一个branch。

**checkpoint state root 与temp roster。** 首代在verified root下发现`generations` absent时只允许`mkdirat(0700)`→open/fstat/fstatat same identity→root fsync；crash留下的verified exact-empty directory可幂等接受，prepopulated/wrong identity/mode拒绝。每个active `<NN>.<creationNonce>.next`目录的direct-child roster只能是`owner.json,journal.json`子集，枚举cap=`2+1`；leaf均current uid/regular/nlink1/mode0600，owner≤4KiB、journal≤1MiB；nested directory/extra/symlink/hardlink/special/overflow一律保留证据并exit7。只有derived basename+nonce、上述exact roster和记录identity都成立才可按leaf unlink→temp-dir fsync→rmdir→generations fsync清理，不得使用通用递归删除。`indexTempIdentitySha256` framing固定为`SHA256("harvis-source-index-temp-identity-v1\0"||u64be(dev)||u64be(ino)||u32be(mode)||u64be(size)||raw32(contentSha256))`的bare hex。

所有checkpoint state leaf都要求current uid、regular、nlink1、无ACL、无named xattr、file flags中immutable/append位全为0；state directory同样无ACL/named xattr/上述flags。size caps固定为lock=0，repository/init=8KiB，owner=4KiB，generation journal=1MiB，verification result及其derived next=64KiB，candidate-copy manifest=256MiB、scratch-cleanup page manifest=16MiB、candidate record=64KiB，alternate index/lock=128MiB；`.next`与base同cap/mode。preexisting object不得chmod/chown/strip ACL/xattr/flags。每个发布或删除都使用parent-fd identity sandwich与file+directory fsync。

prepared时`alternate.index`bootstrap唯一为：证明basename/`.lock` stable ENOENT，O_RDWR|O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW以mode0600新建alternate leaf，立即fchmod0600，有界复制baseline index bytes、fsync并重验identity/hash/cap，然后才运行alternate `read-tree`。generation-owned `alternate.index/.lock`的mode恒为0600，不继承real index的0644；baseline real-index mode只在journal中单独保留，并且只用于final git-dir derived temp的fchmod与standard index publish。Git生成的`alternate.index.lock`只在该generation与持有source lock的runner内合法；每次Git返回后必须stable absent，alternate leaf必须mode0600、无shared-index extension且等于预期semantic roster。crash在prepared/failed alternate step时，只能对fixed basename、current-uid regular/nlink1、mode0600、size≤128MiB的alternate leaf/lock做identity-bound unlink；wrong owner/type/link/mode/cap/ACL/xattr/flags均exit7且保留。从`alternateIndexReady`起`.lock`必须absent，`alternate.index` identity/hash必须与journal与expected index exact，不再允许删除重建。

**owned path authority。** runtime绝不读取、解析或信任untracked `docs/03`来扩张stage范围。Phase3实现时在`scripts/harvis_source_checkpoint.py`内定义版本化、raw-byte sorted、exact `OWNED_PATH_ALLOWLIST_V1`，只含docs/03 Phase1–3 Ownership中的产品/source/schema/test/skill path并显式排除planning/evidence roster；CLI无roster/path参数。`ownedChangeRoster`只能是该constant与worktree/baseline diff的交集且必须覆盖全部actual allowed diff；修改docs/03、添加Ownership文本或传入额外path都不得扩张集合。Phase3 human gate与tests对照docs/03只做完整性复核，不把它当runtime authority。

generation 1是唯一bootstrap例外：checkpoint coordinator/thin-entry/schema/test与distribution coordinator/thin-entry/managed worker/sandbox profile在initial HEAD中尚未tracked，因此本Phase的independent code review必须先对它们开放的fd bytes、mode、`OWNED_PATH_ALLOWLIST_V1`和完整target diff生成`bootstrapReviewFingerprint=sha256:<64hex>`并展示给操作者一次批准。generation1的closed argv唯一扩展为`prepare --bootstrap-review-fingerprint <same>`；coordinator重算exact canonical payload required=`schemaVersion,repositoryIdentitySha256,branchRef,initialHead,initialTree,coordinatorSha256,thinEntrySha256,schemaSha256,distributionCoordinatorSha256,distributionThinEntrySha256,distributionWorkerSha256,sandboxProfileSha256,ownedPathAllowlistSha256,ownedChangeRosterSha256`，schema const=`harvis.source-checkpoint-bootstrap-review.v1`、additionalProperties=false，不等即在object mutation前拒绝。review fingerprint写入generation1 journal的required-but-nullable `bootstrapReviewFingerprint`；generation1 non-null，generation2...8必须null并拒绝该option。generation1 commit/index publish后、completed前，必须从new HEAD tree读回上述7个显式source/schema/profile blob并与各自open-fd bytes/targetBlobOid exact；tests及其他Ownership bytes由ownedChangeRosterSha256/targetBlobOid逐项覆盖。generation2...8才要求当前运行的coordinators/thin entries/worker/profile在baseline HEAD中已tracked且blob等于运行bytes。这个例外只解决信任自举，不放宽path roster、Git CAS或0 remote边界；approval后任一byte/mode/roster漂移使指纹失效。

**active config、时间与Git transcript。** checkpoint不再以`config --local --null --list`作漂移权威；固定使用`config --includes --null --show-origin --list`并复用§5.3 parser/unsafe-key policy，因而包含`extensions.worktreeConfig`的config.worktree与include链。前文checkpoint Git common prefix在`core.splitIndex=false`前再exact加入`-c,core.sharedRepository=0`；active config中任何local/worktree/include来源的`core.sharedRepository`非empty/false/0值都是unsafe，即佾command override也阻断，防止Git显式chmod alternate lock。每个object/ref/index mutation前后两遍ordered active-config/refs/HEAD/index transcript必须byte-exact稳定；raw origin/path只在内存解析/hash，不出DTO。`authorEpoch`是revision1由injected wall clock一次采样的integer seconds，范围`0...253402300799`并从prepared起immutable；commit-tree唯一增加env `GIT_AUTHOR_DATE=@<canonical-decimal-epoch> +0000,GIT_COMMITTER_DATE=@<same> +0000,TZ=UTC`，crash replay必须产生同commit OID。

Git mutating command的transport grammar固定：`read-tree <baselineTree>`、`add -- <sorted roster>`成功都exit0/stdout empty/stderr empty；`write-tree`、`commit-tree`成功都exit0/stdout exact唯一object-format-matching lowercase OID+LF/stderr empty。每条都deadline 30s/每流1MiB；nonzero/timeout/overflow/malformed/trailing bytes均fail closed并不推进对应effect stage。ref推进只使用下文files-ref custom CAS，不调用`update-ref`。对应read-only Git tail沿用前文逐命令exit/stdout/stderr grammar；active config/include与mutating-output变体必须有captured malformed/race golden。

**distribution coordinator。** `harvis_bar_distribution_check.sh`在Phase3改为fixed thin entry，只接受`--checkpoint-generation <1...8> [--retry-infra-fingerprint sha256:<64-lowerhex>]`；retry option只允许§9.4.2 terminal infra tombstone的exact fingerprint授权重试，其他状态、错误格式或未知参数均usage failure。所有实现细节以§9.4.2最终契约为准：在gate1前取得并贯穿全流程的source lock、generation commit/tree scratch、9项closed gate roster、passed/failed artifact、pre-intent derived verification next、manifest-bound candidate/cleanup与terminal disposition。旧“gate失败后才取锁”及fixed `verification-result.next` emitter都禁止实现；reject只在distributionRun=null时读取已发布failed artifact，因此不存在partial/race视图。

本段旧版按单一`gateId/exitCode`且把timeout/signal折叠成数字的`commandsFingerprint`公式已废弃，禁止实现；唯一有效公式是§9.4.2最终 exact `{schemaVersion,generation,head,tree,attempts[]}` + `gateReceiptSha256` 契约，termination class不得归一成exit124/130。`evidenceFingerprint="sha256:"+SHA256(canonical ASCII JSON of verification object excluding evidenceFingerprint)`。raw command/path/stdout/stderr不入artifact。分发脚本不接受gate-id/reason/evidence的自由注入；reason由首个失败fixed gate推导。fault/race tests覆盖gate运行时源漂移、抢锁失败、next/rename/fsync与reject并发。

**install ↔ source generation binding。** transaction `$defs.candidate`/`installed`前文exact keys由本段扩展为`version,receiptSha256,appTreeSha256,identityFingerprint,helperSha256,sourceCommit,sourceTree`，后两项为null或object-format-matching OID；complete plan的candidate两项必须non-null。`$defs.planSnapshot`再required exact `sourceCheckpoint`，其object exact keys=`generation,repositoryIdentitySha256,head,tree,receiptSha256,appTreeSha256,verificationEvidenceFingerprint,reservationRevision,reservationSha256`、additionalProperties=false，全部从verified latest completed/pending generation、passed verification artifact、durable install reservation与candidate receipt投影；candidate sourceCommit/tree必须等于checkpoint head/tree，candidate receipt/app tree SHA必须等于passed verification与同名facts。未完成该binding就aborted的transaction不能作为下一generation的install evidence。§625的apply Approval exact required roster由本段再扩展一个`sourceCheckpoint`，逐字节复用上述planSnapshot object；acceptance Approval同样required该object。所以generation/repository identity/reservation任一漂移都改变planFingerprint并使旧Approval失效；unknown/未完成reservation绝不签Approval。

generation journal前文required roster再增加`bootstrapReviewFingerprint,installReservation`，两者required-but-nullable；前者按generation1/后代conditional见上文。`installReservation`为null或exact `{transactionIdDigest,installIntentRevision,installIntentSha256,state}`、additionalProperties=false，digest/SHA为bare lowercase hex，revision positive，state=`planned|active|candidatePending|terminalSyncRequired`。completed+pending可以reservation null（pre-deployment）或planned/active；candidatePending必须同transaction reservation state=candidatePending；rejectedPreDeployment/aborted/rolledBack/accepted必须reservation null。reservation non-null时prepare/reject/新plan全拒绝，第9代规则不变。

两个authority的唯一global lock order为`source-checkpoint.lock → install.lock`，解锁严格反序；任何code path不得在已持install.lock时再请求source lock。所有source-bound transaction `plan|apply|status|rollback|commit`和source `prepare|reject|bind-transaction`都按此顺序；distribution coordinator持source lock运行gate，并在gate前按同序短暂取install.lock做reservation/intent reconcile。source侧在verified InstallTransactions/lock absent时可在持source lock下证明stable ENOENT而不创建；若存在就必须获lock。第二锁concurrent一律逆序释放已持锁并返回exit5，0新journal/source mutation。

install journal前文required roster再增加`sourceSync`，exact required keys=`operationId,phase,generation,expectedSourceRevision,targetState,sourceRevision,sourceJournalSha256`、additionalProperties=false。phase=`none|intent|acknowledged`；none时除phase外全null；intent时operationId=32-lower-hex、generation/expectedSourceRevision non-null、targetState=`reservationPlanned|reservationActive|candidatePending|aborted|rolledBack|accepted|reservationCleared`，sourceRevision/SHA null；acknowledged时全facts non-null且必须对应已发布source journal。每次双authority transition固定为：持双锁写install `intent`→以该install revision/hash写source reservation/disposition→重验source后写install `acknowledged`→再重验双方才输出。crash recovery也只在双锁下接受source仍expected-old并幂等执行，或source已等target+same operation而补ack；其他组合双方保留并exit7。

`plan`在双锁下先完成candidate/Oracle/passed-verification facts，把install journal写到`planConstructionState=awaitingSourceReservation`的reservation intent，再将generation写为`installReservation.state=planned`，最后把install journal写为`planned + sourceSync.acknowledged`并签发包含reservation revision/SHA的Approval。任何crash都先按上段收敛，Approval只在双方ack后出现。第二plan、reject或prepare看到reservation/intent必须拒绝。`apply`消费Approval前持双锁重验reservation fingerprint并推进state=active，整个command（包括FDA等待）持双锁；status/rollback/commit同样持双锁。plan expiry/0-protected abort把source收敛为aborted；达到pendingUserAcceptance收敛为candidatePending；任意之后的explicit/Oracle rollback允许同transaction digest+更高install revision唯一转移`candidatePending→rolledBack`；commit唯一转移`candidatePending→accepted`且不可逆。manualRecoveryRequired保留reservation=terminalSyncRequired且禁新代。正常路径不需要事后bind；`bind-transaction`只是下段的recovery/idempotent入口。

`bind-transaction`持双锁，以fixed transaction authority完整验证install owner/journal/revision、`sourceSync`、同一`planSnapshot.sourceCheckpoint`、candidate receipt/sourceCommit/sourceTree/App-tree SHA与current generation identity/number/commitOid/expectedTree。它只能幂等返回已acknowledged状态，或按上述intent recovery补齐source/ack；不得绕过reservation从任意terminal state直改disposition。它还要扫描最多8个verified generation，同一transactionIdDigest若已存于另一generation则`INVALID_STATE`。install-backed evidence payload exact为 `{schemaVersion:"harvis.source-checkpoint-install-evidence.v1",generation,repositoryIdentitySha256,sourceCommit,sourceTree,transactionIdDigest,installJournalRevision,state,candidateReceiptSha256,candidateAppTreeSha256}`，缺少、wrong-generation、reused transaction或非法state edge拒绝。candidatePending下同交易pending可幂等，更高revision rolledBack→rolledBack，committed→accepted；accepted不可逆。

**Acceptance Oracle transport。** Oracle每次stdout只能是一行canonical ASCII JSON+LF，stderr exact empty；schema success固定exit0，`INVALID_STATE|PREFERENCE_UNSTABLE|SOURCE_INVALID|GIT_UNSAFE|GIT_CHANGED|INDEX_INVALID|TRACKED_FILE_INVALID|LIMIT_EXCEEDED|SCHEMA_INVALID`固定exit3，`INTERNAL_FAILURE`固定exit9。command×error×stage×exit必须恰好match一个schema branch；timeout/overflow/signal/malformed/multiple-line/nonempty stderr都是coordinator transport failure，不得把部分result当trusted Oracle fact。

#### 9.4.2 Source checkpoint 最终写入与分发契约

本小节完整取代 §9.4 与 §9.4.1 中关于 generation direct-child roster、journal字段/阶段、Git mutating env/command、update-ref/reflog、distribution emitter/verification schema、planConstructionState、reservation pairing 与 Approval sourceCheckpoint 的旧简称；其余 authority、result projection、0 remote、planning docs永不stage及最多8代边界保持不变。实现与测试不得同时保留被取代的旧分支。

**Plan construction 与无环 reservation。** transaction journal 的 planConstructionState 最终 enum 为 collectingFacts|awaitingOracle|awaitingSourceReservation|complete；oracleInput 在后三态 non-null。Oracle 与 candidate facts 完成后，coordinator先写 awaitingSourceReservation + sourceSync.intent；此时 planSnapshot 必须仍为 null，且 provisional installIntentSha256 只哈希 canonical exact object required= schemaVersion,transactionIdDigest,generation,repositoryIdentitySha256,head,tree,candidateReceiptSha256,candidateAppTreeSha256,verificationEvidenceFingerprint,nextInstallRevision，schemaVersion const harvis.install-source-reservation-intent.v1、additionalProperties=false。该payload不含install journal hash、source revision、reservationSha256、planSnapshot或sourceSync ack，因而不存在自引用。

持 source→install 双锁时先原子提交上述install intent revision R，再把generation.installReservation写为 exact required=transactionIdDigest,installIntentRevision,installIntentSha256,state（state=planned），发布source revision S；reservationSha256 是该四字段canonical object的bare SHA。随后install写 sourceSync.acknowledged，绑定 S 与source journal SHA，最后在新revision写完整 planSnapshot、planConstructionState=complete并签apply Approval。planSnapshot.sourceCheckpoint exact为 generation,repositoryIdentitySha256,head,tree,receiptSha256,appTreeSha256,verificationEvidenceFingerprint,reservationRevision,reservationSha256；这里reservationRevision=S。crash只允许在双锁下按同operationId把old→source-published→ack→complete前缀幂等推进或收敛aborted，其他组合exit7且0 Approval。

generation installReservation pairing最终为：(a) disposition=pending时允许null或state=planned|active|terminalSyncRequired；(b) disposition=candidatePending时只允许state=candidatePending|terminalSyncRequired；(c) rejectedPreDeployment|aborted|rolledBack|accepted时必须null。terminalSyncRequired只可由verified install manualRecoveryRequired + sourceSync intent/ack产生，并永久阻断prepare/reject/第二plan，直到同交易恢复为合法terminal edge。sourceSync.targetState enum最终追加terminalSyncRequired。正常plan/apply/status/rollback/commit在同一双锁命令中同步source；bind-transaction只作幂等恢复/核验入口。

apply Approval中的 sourceCheckpoint 必须逐byte等于immutable planSnapshot的planned reservation；消费它时先重验planned snapshot，再推进active。acceptance Approval另含同keys的 currentSourceCheckpoint，但必须从fresh candidatePending reservation revision/hash重新投影，禁止复用planned revision/hash；commit同时消费fresh acceptance并唯一执行candidatePending→accepted。显式/自动rollback唯一执行candidatePending→rolledBack；plan expiry或protected mutation前abort执行pending→aborted。每个terminal命令必须先完成sourceSync acknowledged，才可返回terminal result。

**Generation object quarantine 与journal。** published generation direct children基础roster只允许 owner.json,journal.json,journal.next,alternate.index,alternate.index.lock,objects,verification-result.json；distributionRun非null时另条件允许candidate-copy-manifest.json,candidate-copy-manifest.next,scratch-cleanup-manifest.json,scratch-cleanup-manifest.next与唯一`verification-result.<runId>.<nonce>.next`，任意时点总枚举cap为12+1；旧fixed `verification-result.next`不得存在。objects从alternate add前起必须是current-uid、mode0700、无ACL/named-xattr/immutable-or-append-flags的directory。其direct-child basename必须恰为2位lower-hex、最多256个fanout directory；每个fanout只允许object-format对应38或62 lower-hex completed object，或仅在gitObjectCommandIntent non-null时允许exact `tmp_obj_[A-Za-z0-9]{6}` partial leaf。directory mode0700；completed object current-uid/nlink1/mode0444|0600，partial temp current-uid/nlink1/mode0600|0444，单项compressed≤128MiB，object count≤100000、compressed总量≤512MiB、decompressed总量≤2GiB。crash recovery在private quarantine内只可按intent、preManifest与full post-enumeration identity-bound清理上述partial temp；已完成OID逐项解压验证后保留并在同command重跑后重算manifest。pack、info、错误temp grammar、symlink、hardlink、special、额外层级或cap+1一律exit7并保留证据。

repository.json最终再required exact refBackend,refNamespaceAuthoritySha256,commonObjectsIdentitySha256；refBackend const=files。refNamespaceAuthoritySha256以length-framed identities绑定commonDir→refs→heads→fixed branch parents、commonDir→logs→refs→heads→fixed branch parents、worktree gitDir→logs与packed-refs stable absent identity；本阶段不实现packed-refs parser，任何present都在首个mutation前阻断。live source Git 的 `sourceGitXattrs` 只允许空集合或唯一 `com.apple.provenance`，按name raw byte排序并把value SHA-256写入下段identity record；`com.apple.quarantine`、任何其他name、duplicate、读取失败或capture前后value漂移都阻断，绝不在preexisting Git node上删除/改写xattr。xattr capture只在已no-follow打开并完成stat identity sandwich的FD上使用`flistxattr/fgetxattr`：name-list≤4KiB、单value≤64KiB、aggregate≤128KiB，EINTR重试；size probe/read后再完整capture一次，两份sorted name+value SHA必须exact相等。commonObjectsIdentitySha256绑定commonDir/objects open fd及info/pack directory：root/info/pack都必须current-uid directory、非group-or-other-writable、无ACL、xattrs满足sourceGitXattrs、immutable-or-append flags为0；info与pack在本计划初始authority中必须exact empty，尤其alternates/http-alternates absent。2-hex fanout同样逐dir验证上述facts，loose leaf只允许current-uid regular/nlink1、非group-or-other-writable、无ACL、xattrs满足sourceGitXattrs、flags为0，并有界解压重算OID；每generation另在revision1记录baselineCommonObjectRosterSha256，后态只允许该roster加journal-published OID。任一authority、xattr name/value或额外object在generation间/命令间漂移都在下个mutation前阻断，不能静默重建marker。

本节所有authority/identity/roster digest统一使用唯一framing：A(domain,records)=SHA256("harvis-source-authority-digest-v1\0" || u32be(ASCII-domain byteCount) || ASCII-domain || u32be(recordCount) || Σ(u64be(recordByteCount)||recordBytes))，records按各domain指定ASCII key raw-byte升序且不得duplicate，recordBytes为该domain exact schema的canonical ASCII JSON，不做Unicode normalization。identity record required exact=key,state,dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,nlink,size,contentSha256,xattrs，additionalProperties=false；xattrs为null或按name raw byte严格递增的array，每项required exact=`name,valueSha256`、additionalProperties=false、valueSha256为64-lower-hex。state=absent时dev/ino/uid/gid/mode/nlink/size/contentSha256/xattrs全null；state=directory时dev/ino/uid/gid/mode与xattrs non-null而nlink/size/contentSha256全null；state=file或symlink时全部facts与xattrs non-null，symlink的contentSha256只哈希raw link-target bytes。directory会因本协议合法create/rename/unlink改变nlink/size，所以这两项不得进入immutable authority；ACL/flags与成员变化仍由每次重验及closed roster/intent捕获，xattr name与value已直接进入authority。所有owned可增长temp另使用非hash的`fileInstance`，required exact=`dev,ino,uid,gid,nlink,creationMode,creationXattrs`、additionalProperties=false；它只能在O_EXCL空file的parent fsync后、首个content byte前写journal，creationMode固定0600，creationXattrs为当时sorted exact xattr array。恢复时dev/ino/uid/gid/nlink必须与instance逐值相等；creationXattrs是不可改写的历史创建事实，不要求永远等于current xattrs。current xattrs必须按所属状态机唯一规则验证：object/verification temp恒等creationXattrs，ref temp只允许empty→baseline sourceGitXattrs的有序闭合前缀，candidate file temp只允许empty→manifest final xattrs的闭合前缀；其他变化拒绝。当前mode同样只允许该状态机声明的0600→final mode；size/content始终由expected prefix/final SHA另验，绝不能用会随写入/fchmod/xattr变化的完整identity hash替代instance。ref-namespace-authority、common-objects-authority、common-object-roster、baseline-ref-identity、branch-reflog-state、head-reflog-state、ref-guard-temp-identity与ref-guard-identity的xattrs只接受sourceGitXattrs；vendor-source-cache-authority只接受下文vendorCacheXattrs；其余私有/generated domain只接受empty array。除vendor-source-cache-authority的`link/*` key可用symlink外，所有domain都拒绝symlink state。domain与key grammar不留实现选择：ref-namespace-authority只含`common-dir`,`worktree-git-dir`,`packed-refs`,`ref-parent/<4位十进制index>`,`branch-log-parent/<4位十进制index>`,`head-log-parent/<4位十进制index>`；`packed-refs`必须是absent identity，dev到xattrs全null，任何present都拒绝。common-objects-authority只含`objects-root`,`objects-info`,`objects-pack`；common-object-roster只含`fanout/<2lowerhex>`与`object/<object-format-lowerhex-oid>`；baseline-ref-identity/branch-reflog-state/head-reflog-state各自唯一key为`branch-ref`/`branch-reflog`/`head-reflog`；object-quarantine-identity唯一key=`quarantine-root`；ref-guard-temp-identity/ref-guard-identity唯一key=`ref-guard-temp`/`ref-guard`；distribution-scratch-identity/candidate-temp-identity/verification-next-identity唯一key=`scratch-root`/`candidate-next-root`/`verification-next`。gate-receipt-roster按attempt顺序使用`gate/<2位十进制gateIndex>/command/<2位十进制commandIndex>`，record exact=`key,gateId,commandIndex,commandReceiptSha256`。parent/command index从0开始且不得跳号，动态path component只通过对应record的identity与另行path digest绑定，不进入key。quarantine-object-manifest record exact=key,oid,type,size,compressedBytes,compressedSha256且key=oid；size是解压payload bytes，compressedBytes是loose leaf raw bytes且0...128MiB，objectCompressedBytes必须等于全部record compressedBytes之和。vendor-source-cache-authority沿解析顺序使用`link/<4位index>`,`index-root`,`crate/<package-key-sha256>`并复用identity record；package-key-sha256是canonical exact `{name,version,source,checksum}` bytes bare SHA。vendor-input-cargo-home使用tree record exact=`key,kind,mode,size,contentSha256,xattrs`，key为raw-relative-path bare SHA、kind=`directory|file`，xattrs必须empty array，directory的size/contentSha256=null、file全non-null。每个digest字段只能使用对应domain；`scratchIdentitySha256,candidateTempIdentitySha256,verificationNextIdentitySha256,gateReceiptsSha256,vendorSourceCacheAuthoritySha256,vendorInputCargoHomeSha256`分别只能用上述同名语义domain，toolchainFactsSha256则是canonical toolchain-facts file bytes直接bare SHA。baselineRefSha256仍是loose-ref raw bytes直接bare SHA，不套A。Swift/Python golden覆盖每个domain、absent/file/directory/symlink、fileInstance create/write/fchmod/xattr状态转移稳定性、compressedBytes aggregate、empty/allowlisted/unknown/duplicate/xattr-value drift、directory合法child churn不改identity digest、record/key reorder/prefix/extra/gap、64-bit边界与SHA-1/SHA-256 roster。

Digest例外的最终规范：上段中`distribution-scratch-identity`/`scratch-root`的A-domain声明及把`scratchIdentitySha256`映射到该domain的语句均视为删除；`scratchRootIdentitySha256`、`scratchOwnerIdentitySha256`与`scratchIdentitySha256`只使用本节后文scratch段三个显式canonical object的bare SHA256。candidate-temp、verification-next及其他A-domain映射保持不变；实现与golden不得为scratch再提供A-domain兼容分支。

generation journal在§9.4.1字段外再required exact baselineRefIdentitySha256,baselineRefSha256,baselineBranchReflogStateSha256,baselineHeadReflogStateSha256,objectQuarantineIdentitySha256,gitObjectCommandIntent,quarantineObjectManifestSha256,objectCount,objectCompressedBytes,objectPublishCursor,currentObjectIntentOid,currentObjectTempNonce,currentObjectTempInstance,currentObjectExpectedCompressedBytes,currentObjectExpectedCompressedSha256,refGuardNonce,refGuardExpectedBytes,refGuardExpectedSha256,refGuardTempInstance,refGuardTempIdentitySha256,refGuardIdentitySha256；前五项从revision1或objects创建时写入后immutable。gitObjectCommandIntent为null或exact {command,preManifestSha256}，additionalProperties=false；command只add|writeTree|commitTree，spawn前non-null、post-manifest fsync后清null。其余字段按阶段required-but-nullable/conditional：commitWritten前manifest/count/bytes与全部currentObject*字段为null且cursor=0；commitWritten起manifest/count/bytes non-null且objectCount/bytes分别等于manifest record count/compressedBytes之和；objectPublishIntent起cursor为0...objectCount。cursor<objectCount且无单项intent时五个currentObject*字段全null；单项publish intent先原子写OID、32-lowerhex random nonce、manifest中exact compressedBytes与compressed SHA，temp instance仍null；O_EXCL创建空temp并fsync parent后、任何content write前再写fileInstance。instance non-null后才允许写入。publish+fanout fsync后cursor+1并一次清空五字段；objectsPublished起cursor=objectCount且五字段全null。refGuardIntent起refGuardNonce、refGuardExpectedBytes与refGuardExpectedSha256 non-null；refGuardTempCreated起refGuardTempInstance non-null而final temp identity仍null；refGuardTempReady起final temp identity non-null；refGuardReady起refGuardIdentitySha256 non-null并保持。stage最终有序为 prepared|alternateIndexReady|treeWritten|commitWritten|objectPublishIntent|objectsPublished|indexPublishIntent|indexTempReady|indexLockReady|refGuardIntent|refGuardTempCreated|refGuardTempReady|refGuardReady|refUpdated|indexUpdated|refGuardReleased|completed；旧update-ref state machine不得实现。

Git add/write-tree/commit-tree只允许coordinator内部增加 GIT_OBJECT_DIRECTORY=<root-walk canonical quarantine absolute path> 与 GIT_ALTERNATE_OBJECT_DIRECTORIES=<root-walk canonical common objects absolute path>；Darwin `/dev/fd/N/child`不是可解析目录，生产实现与测试都必须拒绝该旧形式。两条canonical path必须absolute、无NUL/C0/DEL/colon，防alternate冒号分隔歧义；raw path只存在child env，不进DTO/artifact/journal。两directory FD持续打开；每条Git前后都从root fd no-follow重走absolute path并要求entry/dev/ino/uid/mode与open FD identity exact，失败即GIT_CHANGED。common objects/info/alternates 与 http-alternates必须stable ENOENT；caller任何GIT_OBJECT_DIRECTORY、alternate或replace env均拒绝。每个Git object命令前先journal exact gitObjectCommandIntent={command,preManifestSha256}，command只add|writeTree|commitTree；返回并完成post-manifest验证后才清null。每条mutating command后enumerate quarantine，zlib有界解压为 exact “type SP decimal-size NUL payload”，type只blob|tree|commit、declared size=payload size，并按repository object format重算OID等于fanout+leaf；blob必须匹配owned target content，tree必须递归匹配expected roster，commit必须exact tree=expectedTree、唯一parent=baselineHead、fixed author/committer/epoch/message且无额外header。ordered manifest record exact为 key,oid,type,size,compressedBytes,compressedSha256且key=oid，compressedBytes来自同一已打开loose leaf的raw byte count，按raw OID升序，其canonical SHA与record count/compressedBytes aggregate写journal。commitWritten到objectsPublished之间所有解析new OID的read-only rev-parse/cat-file/ls-tree也必须使用同一quarantine+common overlay与前后identity sandwich；禁止fallback到真实object path的无overlay查询。

object publish只在objectPublishIntent后按manifest cursor逐项执行。目标OID已存在时必须regular/current-uid/nlink1/非group-or-other-writable、无ACL、xattrs满足sourceGitXattrs、flags为0并解压重算同OID。absent时先按上段持久化OID/nonce/expected compressedBytes/SHA；以verified commonObjectsFD no-follow打开或mkdirat0700目标fanout，在同fanout以O_EXCL/O_NOFOLLOW创建`.harvis-checkpoint-obj-<generation>-<nonce>.next`、mode0600，重验empty及sourceGitXattrs并fsync parent，随后在任何content write前持久化currentObjectTempInstance。每次恢复若instance=null且derived temp absent，在目标/fanout/baseline仍exact时允许按已记录nonce执行首次O_EXCL创建；若同nonce temp已存在，则只接受current-uid regular/nlink1/mode0600、size0、无ACL、xattrs满足sourceGitXattrs、flags为0的唯一empty temp并先记录其fileInstance；任何nonempty或额外leaf都exit7。instance non-null时要求fileInstance的dev/ino/uid/gid/nlink与creationXattrs历史值逐值相等，且current xattrs按object rule恒等creationXattrs；current mode为0600|0444且bytes为expected quarantine compressed bytes的exact prefix、size≤expected；可从当前size续写，完成后重算expected SHA/compressedBytes、fsync/fchmod0444/重验same instance与final identity，再用renameatx_np RENAME_EXCL发布并fsync fanout。目标在rename crash后已存在时须验证同OID且temp absent，再幂等推进cursor。任何prefix mismatch、foreign instance/temp、unexpected mode/xattr或EEXIST wrong object都保留证据exit7。每项发布后才journal cursor+1并清空全部currentObject*字段；content-addressed loose object不回删。common objects/fanout/target parent identity任何替换、objects/info alternate、victim symlink或malformed existing object都0 victim mutation、exit7。

**Files-ref CAS 与index收口。** 首个object mutation前，rev-parse --show-ref-format必须exit0/stdout exact files+LF/stderr empty；目标full branch必须是existing loose ref，target entry不得来自packed-refs、symbolic ref或reftable。commonDir→refs→heads→每个ref parent逐component root-fd no-follow，均current-uid directory且非group-or-other-writable、无ACL、xattrs满足sourceGitXattrs、immutable-or-append flags为0；loose ref为current-uid regular/nlink1/mode0600|0644、xattrs满足sourceGitXattrs、bytes exact baseline OID+LF。target ref.lock stable ENOENT。common logs/refs/... target与worktree logs/HEAD分别以absent或regular/current-uid/nlink1/mode0600|0644/≤16MiB/sourceGitXattrs closed state记录identity+size+content SHA，分别写baselineBranchReflogStateSha256与baselineHeadReflogStateSha256；本流程永不append/truncate/create reflog。

objectsPublished后才发布standard index.lock；其fd/identity/hash与baseline index均重验后，先journal refGuardIntent、random refGuardNonce、expected bytes=`new commit OID+LF`的size与SHA。ref parent中的derived temp basename固定为 `.harvis-checkpoint-ref-<generation>-<nonce>.next`：仅在intent已fsync后以O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW mode0600创建，重验empty并fsync parent，首个write前写refGuardTempCreated+fileInstance；随后写expected exact-prefix、fchmod为baseline ref mode，并把baseline loose ref的sourceGitXattrs逐name/value复制到该owned temp（baseline为空则temp必须为空；不得复制任何其他xattr）；fsync/same-instance/final identity重验后才写refGuardTempReady+refGuardTempIdentitySha256。恢复在instance=null时只接受exact nonce的唯一empty0600 temp并先记录instance；instance non-null/final identity null时只接受same instance、mode0600|baseline、bytes为expected prefix、xattrs为空或exact baseline的闭合前缀，继续写/fchmod/xattr；任何其他tuple保留exit7。再次证明standard target ref.lock ENOENT后，使用renameatx_np(temp,targetRef.lock,RENAME_EXCL)+ref-parent fsync；重开证明standard lock identity=recorded final temp后写refGuardReady/refGuardIdentitySha256。crash在temp create/instance journal/write/xattr/fsync/final journal/rename/dir-fsync任一点，只能按nonce+instance+expected prefix幂等继续；foreign temp或standard lock绝不删除。然后重验parent、loose ref identity+old bytes、packed target absent、HEAD/full ref、real index与两份reflog baseline；只允许同parentFD renameatx_np(targetRef,targetRef.lock,RENAME_SWAP)，fsync ref parent并重开证明ref=new且xattrs与baseline逐值相等、ref.lock=old，写refUpdated。RENAME_SWAP保留同名Git guard，正常Git writer无法在index发布窗口取得ref lock。

recovery只接受：(a) ref=new、owned ref.lock=old、index=baseline且owned index.lock=expected，继续index rename；(a2) journal=refUpdated、ref=new、owned ref.lock=old、real index=expected且standard index.lock absent，重验expected SHA/全stage0-H semantic roster与ref/reflog/guard后补git-dir fsync，再写indexUpdated并继续guard release；这是index rename syscall或git-dir fsync成功而indexUpdated journal尚未发布的唯一合法tuple；(b0) journal=indexUpdated、ref=new、owned ref.lock=old、real index=expected且standard index.lock absent，重验ref/index/reflog/guard后继续identity-bound guard unlink、ref-dir fsync与refGuardReleased；(b) ref=new、ref.lock absent、index=expected且journal≥indexUpdated，补写或确认refGuardReleased→completed；(c) ref=old、owned ref.lock=new、index仍baseline，重验后重试swap；(d) journal refGuardTempReady、derived temp absent、standard lock identity=recorded temp且ref=old时补写refGuardReady；(e0) journal=refGuardIntent、derived temp absent、standard lock absent、ref/index与全部baseline identity仍exact时，允许按已记录nonce创建empty temp并继续；(e) journal处于refGuardIntent|refGuardTempCreated|refGuardTempReady且derived temp仍为own expected-prefix/final new bytes时按nonce+fileInstance继续写入或标准锁发布。其他OID/lock/index/reflog tuple一律recovery-required/manual，不得以“branch已推进必须继续”覆盖。index rename前后都重验ref=new与guard=old；atomic index rename+git-dir fsync+semantic roster通过后写indexUpdated，再identity-bound unlink旧ref guard、ref-dir fsync、证明ref仍new且两份reflog逐byte不变，写refGuardReleased→completed。custom CAS只承诺与遵守Git同名lock协议的并发互斥；同UID恶意ABA仍属于§5.3明确边界，但任何静态symlink/parent/leaf替换均在本契约内阻断。

active config最终拆成两类query：先原样执行§5.3 unsafe-key preflight并新增core.sharedRepository、core.lockfilePid、core.autocrlf、core.eol、core.safecrlf与任意filter.*；sharedRepository/lockfilePid只允许empty|false|0，autocrlf只允许absent|false，eol只允许absent|lf，safecrlf只允许absent|true，任何filter key都unsafe。checkpoint common prefix在core.splitIndex=false前固定追加 -c core.sharedRepository=0 -c core.lockfilePid=false -c core.autocrlf=false -c core.eol=lf -c core.safecrlf=true。随后config --includes --null --show-origin --list只作为opaque raw transcript：exit0、stderr empty、stdout≤4MiB，不解析origin/value、不出DTO；两遍raw bytes必须exact。对sorted owned paths另运行`check-attr -z --stdin text eol working-tree-encoding filter`，stdin为每path+NUL，stdout每path严格四组`path NUL attr NUL unspecified NUL`且顺序text,eol,working-tree-encoding,filter，stderr empty；任何set/value/missing/reorder拒绝。因此blob payload必须逐byte等于owned targetContentSha256并OID=targetBlobOid，不把CRLF/encoding/filter normalization留给实现者。旧“用§5.3 parser直接解析show-origin framing”的简称无效。Git mutating roster最终只有read-tree,add,write-tree,commit-tree；update-ref完全移除。read-tree/add成功stdout/stderr empty，write-tree/commit-tree stdout唯一OID+LF，所有命令nonzero/timeout/overflow/malformed均不推进stage。

**Distribution coordinator 与隔离runner。** Phase3新增 scripts/harvis_bar_distribution_check.py 与 scripts/harvis_distribution_command_worker.py；现有harvis_bar_distribution_check.sh改成固定 #!/bin/sh thin entry，只接受 `--checkpoint-generation 1...8 [--retry-infra-fingerprint sha256:<64lowerhex>]`，retry参数只可消费同代terminal infra，其他状态usage失败；随后exec /usr/bin/env -i固定env /usr/bin/python3 -I -B -S该coordinator。coordinator、thin entry、managed worker、tracked sandbox profile与tests全部加入OWNED_PATH_ALLOWLIST_V1。四项source authority逐byte进入bootstrap review/gate roster；它们执行与transaction/source相同两段self-authority，第一段后、任何create/spawn前exactly-once umask077；旧env-bash readiness脚本语义完全删除。

distribution在gate1前取得source lock并持有到verification/journal publish完成；若InstallTransactions存在则按source→install短取install lock，先recover sourceSync并要求generation reservation=null、无未决install intent，再释放install lock。second-lock concurrent/reservation/intent/source drift都0 gate spawn、0 artifact。每个gate后重验repository/generation revision、full ref/HEAD/tree；最后一次live-source重验固定发生在仍为running、candidate/verification任何write intent之前。通过该屏障后所有candidate/verification bytes只从immutable generation/scratch authority派生，不再读取live source，也不在publish阶段制造late-infra分支；之后的普通Git漂移留给下一次source-bound plan/status检测。屏障前漂移返回SOURCE_CHANGED且不把它伪装成build/test/package failure。

SourceCheckpoints root direct roster最终再允许fixed `distribution-runs` 与 `candidates` 两个mode0700 directory，root cap相应为8+1；root/两dir及其后代完全复用§9.4.1 state directory的current-uid、fd-entry identity sandwich、无ACL/named-xattr/immutable-or-append-flags、preexisting不chmod与fsync规则。distribution-runs direct child在source lock下最多一个 `<generation>.<32lowerhexRunId>.scratch`，另观察cap=1+1；generation direct roster另允许run-bound `candidate-copy-manifest.json`,`candidate-copy-manifest.next`,`scratch-cleanup-manifest.json`,`scratch-cleanup-manifest.next`与唯一`verification-result.<runId>.<nonce>.next`。candidate manifest及next固定0600/≤256MiB；scratch cleanup current-page manifest及next固定0600/≤16MiB；verification next固定0600/≤64KiB，只在对应distributionRun条件下存在。generation journal再required exact `baselineCommonObjectRosterSha256,distributionRun`。distributionRun的最终字段与state conditional见下段；任何旧字段集合不得作为宽松fallback。

`distributionRun`为null或required exact=`runId,state,runRevision,outcome,terminalError,infraResultFingerprint,scratchRootIdentitySha256,scratchOwnerTempInstance,scratchOwnerSha256,scratchOwnerIdentitySha256,scratchIdentitySha256,controlJobsIdentitySha256,gateCursor,completedCommandReceipts,gateReceiptsSha256,currentCommand,gateRosterFingerprint,toolchainAuthorityFingerprint,toolchainFactsSha256,vendorSourceCacheAuthoritySha256,vendorInputCargoHomeSha256,provenanceSha256,expectedReceiptSha256,expectedAppTreeSha256,candidateManifestWrite,candidateCopyEntryCount,candidateCopyCursor,candidateTempIdentitySha256,candidateCurrentCopyIndex,candidateCurrentKind,candidateCurrentNonce,candidateCurrentExpectedBytes,candidateCurrentExpectedSha256,candidateCurrentTempInstance,candidateRecordWrite,candidateRecordSha256,verificationNextNonce,verificationExpectedBytes,verificationExpectedSha256,verificationNextInstance,verificationNextSha256,verificationNextIdentitySha256,scratchManifestWrite,scratchCleanupPageNumber,scratchCleanupDeletedCount,scratchEntryCount,scratchCleanupCursor`、additionalProperties=false。runId=32-lowerhex；runRevision在首个intent为1，此后每次generation journal成功发布exact +1，任何syscall side effect之前必须先发布对应intent revision，no-op不得加revision，overflow/gap/replay拒绝。outcome=`pending|passed|failed|infra`；terminalError只在infra non-null，exact required=`code,stage`、additionalProperties=false，code→stage只允许`SOURCE_CHANGED→source|TOOLCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE→toolchain|SANDBOX_UNAVAILABLE→sandbox|MATERIALIZATION_FAILED→materialize|GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED→gateTransport|CANDIDATE_PROMOTION_FAILED→promotion|INTERNAL_FAILURE→internal`。首次infra error在副作用前journal并保持immutable；cleanup failure不改变outcome/error，只即时返回exit7 RECOVERY_REQUIRED/cleanup并保留run。infraResultFingerprint只在infra terminal `state=scratchCleaned` non-null，值固定为`F(exact distribution-result object)`；F只canonicalize该JSON object，不包含stdout terminal LF，并用于显式retry。

`manifestWrite`用于candidateManifestWrite/scratchManifestWrite/candidateRecordWrite，为null或exact required=`expectedBytes,expectedSha256,nextInstance,nextIdentitySha256,baseIdentitySha256`、additionalProperties=false。intent先写role-specific expectedBytes与bare expectedSha256，其他三项null：candidate manifest=1...256MiB、scratch current-page manifest=1...16MiB、candidate record=1...64KiB；wrong-role cap match-count=0。O_EXCL empty next+parent fsync后、首byte前写fileInstance；same instance exact-prefix完成、fsync/fchmod0600后写nextIdentitySha256；RENAME_EXCL+parent fsync后写baseIdentitySha256。两种identity都是bare SHA256(canonical exact `{state:"file",dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,nlink,size,contentSha256,xattrs:[]}`)，但next/base必须分别在各basename重开后capture；candidate/scratch manifest位于generation，candidateRecordWrite位于fixed candidate generation并使用candidate.next→candidate.json。closed recovery只接受：instance=null时next absent则create、唯一empty则先record；instance non-null/final null时same-instance expected prefix；nextIdentity non-null/baseIdentity null时`next=full+base absent`继续rename，或`next absent+base为same recorded inode/bytes`补parent fsync并写baseIdentity；baseIdentity non-null的active-write态只接受next absent+base exact。仅有两个closed delete-prefix例外仍保留historical baseIdentity而允许base absent：(1)`state=scratchCleanupIntent && scratchCleanupCursor=scratchEntryCount && scratch root present`的nonfinal page，next/base均absent且下一revision只能推进page/deleted counters；(2)下文artifactPublished或terminal-infra run-bound manifest cleanup，按固定candidate→scratch manifest顺序接受当前role base exact-present或已absent。除此以外cleanup、nonempty-without-instance、wrong prefix/identity或base+next并存都exit7。

completedCommandReceipts是0...11项ordered array，每项required exact=`gateIndex,gateId,commandReceipt`、additionalProperties=false，commandReceipt使用下文exact schema；canonical array总bytes≤768KiB。gateCursor只计已完成gate且为0...9；array必须是GATE_ROSTER_V1从gate0/command0开始的无gap command prefix，多command gate可使array增长而gateCursor不变。gateReceiptsSha256始终等于该prefix的gate-receipt-roster A-digest，空prefix也有唯一digest。currentCommand在两command间null；receiptPrepared→jobCleanupIntent→jobCleaned期间nested receipt只保留在currentCommand、array/gateCursor不变。jobCleaned后的下一revision才一次把它append到array、清current，并同时推进gateCursor或准备下个command；故crash后不需从单向digest重建nested receipt，也不得重跑已完成command。

state/outcome/nullability是closed oneOf：(a)`intent`：gateCursor=0、receipt array empty/currentCommand null、gateRoster/toolchainAuthority/vendorSource三facts non-null；scratch create prefix只允许五tuple全null，或仅scratchRootIdentitySha256 non-null，或rootIdentity+scratchOwnerTempInstance non-null而最终三项null，或五项全non-null，toolchainFacts/vendorInput/provenance及candidate/verification facts为null。正常branch outcome=pending/error/fingerprint null；early failure branch outcome=infra/error non-null/fingerprint null；root-only/owner-prefix必须先按下文补全或identity-bound清回all-null。(b)`materialized`：scratch五tuple、toolchainFacts、vendorInput全non-null；outcome可pending或infra。(c)`running`：materialized facts保持，gate prefix/currentCommand按上段；outcome=pending时terminalError null；完成9 gate后可在仍为running/outcome=passed时持久化candidateManifestWrite intent→next→base prefix，base完成才转candidateCopyIntent；首个normal product exit转failed并允许verification intent，transport/source/toolchain等转infra。(d)`candidateCopyIntent`：candidateManifestWrite必须baseIdentity non-null、entryCount/cursor non-null；candidateTempIdentitySha256在root mkdir/fsync前允许null且cursor=0/current六项null，之后non-null；outcome正常为passed，promotion失败可转infra并按manifest清own candidate。(e)`candidateReady`：outcome=passed、candidateRecord及final factsnon-null、current六字段全null；verification write prefix可在本state开始。(f)`verificationWriteIntent|verificationNextCreated|verificationPrepared`：outcome只passed|failed、terminalError null；failed时全部candidate facts null，passed时保持candidateReady facts；verification expected/nonce从WriteIntent起non-null，instance从NextCreated起non-null，final SHA/identity只在Prepared non-null。上述(a)...(f)的scratchManifestWrite/pageNumber/deletedCount/entryCount/cleanupCursor全null；满足下文进入条件后必须先原子转`scratchCleanupIntent`。(g)`scratchCleanupIntent|scratchCleaned`：outcome可passed|failed|infra；passed/failed必须有verificationPrepared全facts，infra的verification facts恒null；pageNumber=0...4294967295、deletedCount=0...9223372036854775807 non-null。`scratchCleanupIntent`只允许三种page prefix：between-page为manifest null且entryCount=cursor=0；publishing为manifestWrite处于intent→next→base任一prefix、entryCount=1...4096、cursor=0；active为baseIdentity non-null、entryCount=1...4096、cursor=0...entryCount。普通`scratchCleaned`要求final page baseIdentity non-null、cursor=entryCount、scratch root absent，totalDeleted=deletedCount+entryCount不溢出。(h)`artifactPublishIntent|artifactPublished`只允许passed|failed并保持verification与final scratch-page terminal facts。(i) terminal infra固定`state=scratchCleaned`、artifact/disposition/candidate全absent、run-bound manifest files已identity-bound清空、terminalError与infraResultFingerprint non-null；若曾创建scratch，则五tuple与final page facts保持且cursor=entryCount；若scratch从未创建，则五tuple/manifest全null，pageNumber/deletedCount/entryCount/cursor全为0。cleanup syscall失败不改任何branch，只返回exit7。run保持不清并按下文幂等重放。任何其他null组合、state跳跃、page/counter回退或溢出、outcome回退、extra field必须match-count=0。

candidate conditional对上段简称作最终收窄：failed/infra及passed的App rename前，candidateRecordWrite与candidateRecordSha256都必须null；candidateCopyIntent只在App base已exact后允许candidateRecordWrite按null→intent→next→base前缀推进，baseIdentity non-null时candidateRecordSha256必须同时为其expectedSha256并进入candidateReady；candidateReady及后续passed states两项保持non-null immutable。上段“candidateRecord及final facts”即此exact pair，不存在直接写candidate.json的兼容分支。

controlJobsIdentitySha256在scratch root未创建及其五tuple未完整时必须null；完整scratch五tuple后的intent/materialization prefix允许null→non-null一次，materialized及后续有scratch states必须non-null immutable。terminal infra从未创建scratch branch为null，有scratch branch保持non-null。其exact capture与crash恢复按下文control/jobs authority，不能用jobRootIdentity替代parent authority。

failed path的全部expected/candidateCopy/candidateTemp/candidateCurrent/candidateRecord facts恒null；passed path在gate9后先按manifestWrite协议发布下段candidate copy manifest并绑定SHA/count、expected两SHA，才写candidateCopyIntent/cursor0；next root创建/fsync后立即记录stable directory identity。candidateCopyIntent中cursor为0...entryCount：没有active item时六个candidateCurrent*全null；处理下一项前先写candidateCurrentCopyIndex=cursor与kind。directory分支的nonce/expected/tempInstance恒null，mkdirat+parent fsync+exact identity完成后cursor+1并清current；file分支在同一intent先写random nonce、manifest expected bytes/SHA且tempInstance=null，再按下段derived temp协议复制，final rename+parent fsync后cursor+1并一次清六字段。cursor=entryCount时current六字段全null；base App+candidate.json完成后才写candidateReady及record SHA，此后所有candidateCurrent*保持null。passed从candidateReady、failed从running生成完整canonical verification bytes，在任何temp create前先写verificationWriteIntent + random32-lowerhex nonce + expected byte count/SHA；随后O_EXCL创建derived verification next、fsync generation、首byte前记录fileInstance并写verificationNextCreated。只接受same instance的expected exact-prefix续写；full bytes→fsync→fchmod0600→final identity/SHA重验后才写verificationPrepared。instance=null且derived basename absent时，在generation/base authority仍exact下允许执行首次O_EXCL创建；若derived basename已存在，则只接受唯一empty0600 temp并先记录instance；nonempty-without-instance、wrong prefix/size/SHA/identity一律保留exit7。整个期间base artifact必须absent且plan/reject/prepare不可消费。

passed/failed只有verificationPrepared后，infra则只有currentCommand=null且所有own candidate partial已按candidate manifest清零后，才可进入scratch paged cleanup；root-only/owner-prefix须先补成完整五tuple。若infra发生在scratch创建前且五tuple全null，必须先证明distribution-runs child absent，再原子发布`state=scratchCleaned,outcome=infra,scratchManifestWrite=null,scratchCleanupPageNumber=0,scratchCleanupDeletedCount=0,scratchEntryCount=0,scratchCleanupCursor=0`的terminal tombstone与fingerprint。其余分支先原子进入`scratchCleanupIntent`的between-page prefix：pageNumber=0,deletedCount=0,manifest=null,count=cursor=0。

每个scratch cleanup page只描述至多4096个当前可安全删除的node，不需要也不允许把全tree装进单一manifest。manifest required exact=`schemaVersion,runId,generation,scratchIdentitySha256,pageNumber,deletedBefore,entryCount,entries`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-scratch-cleanup-page.v1`；pageNumber/deletedBefore逐值等于journal，entries.length=entryCount=1...4096。entry按postorderIndex升序且required exact=`nameB64,parentIdentitySha256,postorderIndex,priorSameInodeUnlinks,identity`：非root的nameB64是canonical padded RFC4648 raw basename（nonempty、无NUL或slash、非dot/dot-dot、decode≤255 bytes），parentIdentitySha256 exact等于`SHA256(canonical ASCII JSON exact {state:"directory",dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,nlink:null,size:null,contentSha256:null,xattrs:[]})`的64-lower-hex bare digest，不套A-domain、不含`key`；root entry只允许nameB64 empty、parentIdentity null，且其nested identity必须为state=directory并逐值提供recorded scratch root的dev/ino/uid/gid/mode/xattrs、nlink/size/contentSha256为null；再把这些facts代入既有`{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-scratch-root-identity.v1",dev,ino,uid,gid,mode}`重算bare SHA，必须exact等于journal的scratchRootIdentitySha256，不能把nested object与该hash直接相等。nested identity required exact=`state,dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,nlink,size,contentSha256,xattrs`、additionalProperties=false；directory只绑定dev/ino/uid/gid/mode/xattrs且nlink/size/contentSha256=null；file/symlink的全部factsnon-null、nlink为captured positive integer，symlink的content SHA哈希raw target，xattrs固定empty。directory/root的priorSameInodeUnlinks恒0；file/symlink的该值必须等于本page中postorderIndex更小且identity具有相同`(state,dev,ino)`的entry数，并严格小于captured nlink。完整entry canonical ASCII JSON含comma≤1024 bytes、fixed envelope≤1MiB，故page exact bytes≤`1MiB+4096*1024<16MiB`；candidate的256MiB full manifest不受此page公式替代。

page builder只在managed worker/rootRef、same-PGID与observed descendants、vnode writers及pool socket holders均按上文双遍empty后运行；scratch此后视为immutable。每个page选出≤4096 entries但在发布manifest前，先把该page中nlink>1的distinct `(state,dev,ino)`放入最多4096项in-memory map，再从recorded root FD做一次完整no-follow scan，只为这些keys统计整个当前scratch的raw directory entry数并交叉验证全部link的mode/content/xattrs一致；要求每个key的当前`st_nlink == scratch内计数`。active page crash/retry在本次invocation继续任何unlink前，对`cursor...entryCount-1`仍可能存在的hardlink keys重跑同一全树scan，并要求current nlink/internal count同时等于manifest captured nlink减去同page已推进的priorSameInodeUnlinks。map cap由page entry cap自然保证。automatic hardlink census支持边界exact为：每次full-scratch scan自独立CLOCK_MONOTONIC起点最多120秒，node visits（root与每个raw dirent各计1）≤1,048,576，root depth=0且max depth≤256，累计decoded raw basename bytes≤536,870,912；每个no-follow visit只做bounded identity/count工作。只在这些node/depth/byte/deadline边界内不对全tree distinct hardlink group另设terminal cap，4097+组可跨page继续闭环；超任一边界固定在本page 0 unlink时exit7并保留run/evidence，自动cleanup不承诺完成，后续只能在相同边界内重试或进入明确remediation/manual，不能宣称任意有限tree都可自动清理。计数不等（即存在scratch外link）或scan期间identity/nlink漂移同样在本page任何unlink前exit7；先前已完成page只可能删除其他已证明owned的scratch entries，当前外部sentinel及其scratch link均未unlink，sentinel inode/nlink/ctime/bytes逐值不变。scan与page walk都从recorded root fd使用no-follow directory stack，不拼接absolute/full relative path；每个nonroot locator只存parent identity+basename，恢复时重新FD遍历并要求parent identity唯一命中。文件/链接（含已通过page census的scratch-internal hardlink）可直接入page；directory只有全部current children已absent或排在本page更早位置才可入page；root只可位于最终page末项。page selection无需跨crash确定性：任何manifest intent前0 delete，选定后expected bytes/SHA先入journal，再复用manifestWrite发布next→base。baseIdentity non-null后才可按cursor逐项重新搜索parent；directory要求nested identity exact，file/symlink要求除nlink外全部identity exact且current nlink exact=`captured nlink-priorSameInodeUnlinks`，随后才unlinkat/rmdir no-follow、parent fsync并推进cursor。已推进cursor的locator必须absent；未推进cursor只接受上述present exact，或在crash recovery中locator absent且parent fsync/effect可由同page严格前缀唯一证明后补推进。captured后新增/移除link、同page decrement不匹配、内容/identity漂移、parent 0/多命中、mount/device escape或nonempty directory均保留exit7；不再把已证明全在scratch内的positive-nlink hardlink误判为错误。

cursor=count且root仍present表示nonfinal page：先按base identity unlink page manifest并fsync generation；crash在unlink后journal前只接受next absent+base absent+root present。下一revision原子令pageNumber+1、deletedCount+=entryCount、manifest=null、count=cursor=0，再扫描下一page；page/deleted counter overflow直接RECOVERY_REQUIRED且不删除未知项。cursor=count且该page包含root并已证明scratch root absent表示final page：保持final manifest facts并写scratchCleaned，total deleted exact为deletedCount+entryCount；final base manifest由既有artifact/infra terminal cleanup按identity删除。这样满足上述node/depth/byte/deadline边界的managed child输出tree以constant-size持久page闭环；250001个nlink1 empty file的success/timeout fixture无需hardlink full-scratch census，只增加page数且仍在node cap内，不再需要不存在的per-create mediation。

scratchCleaned且outcome=passed|failed才写artifactPublishIntent，再将同instance/SHA的derived verification next以RENAME_EXCL发布为verification-result.json、fsync generation并写artifactPublished。failed outcome随后在同source lock推进rejectedPreDeployment；passed保持pending。最后重验artifact/outcome/disposition/candidate tuple。此时仍保持distributionRun non-null，先由同一verifyDistribution按candidateManifestWrite/scratchManifestWrite的baseIdentity删除present manifests（对应`.next`与derived verification next必须absent），每删一项都fsync generation；artifactPublished recovery明确允许两份base manifest各自处于“exact present或已absent”的前缀，并幂等清到全absent。只有两份run-bound manifest都证明absent且terminal journal再次fsync后，才原子清distributionRun、fsync journal/generation并输出terminal result。

scratchCleaned且outcome=infra走独立0-artifact terminal：verification-result base/next必须absent、disposition不得变化、candidate next/base/record必须absent；candidateManifestWrite若存在与scratchManifestWrite一样按base identity清到absent，各next必须absent。全部run-bound leaves absent/fsync后，以完整canonical infra result（ordinary mapped code exit3，INTERNAL_FAILURE exit9）计算infraResultFingerprint并发布terminal scratchCleaned revision；distributionRun保持non-null。普通`verifyDistribution --checkpoint-generation N`此后只重验tuple并幂等重放同一JSON，不启动新run，因此crash在stdout任一点不丢error。只有显式`--retry-infra-fingerprint <exact current fingerprint>`可在source lock下消费该terminal：它先重验repository/generation与0 run-bound leaf，再以新runId/revision1原子替换为新intent；wrong fingerprint不改state。cleanup失败永不改outcome/terminalError，只返回exit7 RECOVERY_REQUIRED/cleanup并保留原branch。任意distributionRun非null时candidate/artifact都不可消费；严格只读`status`返回RECOVERY_REQUIRED且0 write，plan/reject/prepare同样拒绝。artifact rename已发生但journal未推进只能由verifyDistribution按exact next-absent/base-same tuple补artifactPublished。crash只能按runId+instance+manifest+cursor/effect facts继续或closed cleanup，未知scratch永不递归删除。

candidates direct child只允许01...08 generation directory；每代exact roster=candidate.json,candidate.next,HarvisBar.app,`.HarvisBar.app.next-<runId>`的stage-valid子集，parent/candidate dir0700、metadata0600。全gate通过后，coordinator从仍受scratch authority保护的@OUTPUT App生成generation-local canonical candidate copy manifest；manifest required exact=`schemaVersion,runId,generation,entryCount,totalBytes,sourceAppTreeSha256,entries`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-candidate-copy.v1`。entry exact=`relativePath,kind,mode,size,contentSha256,xattrs,copyIndex,postorderIndex`；relativePath strict UTF-8 raw bytes≤1024且canonical JSON escaping后每个完整entry（含comma）≤8192 bytes，fixed envelope excluding entries≤1MiB。entryCount必须等于entries.length，copyIndex与postorderIndex各自都是0...entryCount-1的唯一permutation；目录在copyIndex中先于descendant、在postorderIndex中晚于descendant，file≤128MiB、tree≤20,000项/512MiB；因此capture在任何manifest intent/candidate root create前必须证明exact encoded bytes≤`1MiB + entryCount*8192 ≤ 256MiB`，max+1/oversized-escaped entry直接CANDIDATE_PROMOTION_FAILED且0 candidate side effect。全部path/order/count/total/App-tree自重算；source @OUTPUT在capture前后及每次resume都须same identity/tree。先把exact expected bytes/SHA写入candidateManifestWrite，再按统一manifestWrite protocol发布next/base；只有baseIdentity/count已进入distributionRun才可创建`.HarvisBar.app.next-<runId>`。root创建/fsync后立即以directory invariant identity记录candidateTempIdentitySha256，cursor按copyIndex每个file/dir durable effect推进。

candidate file不直接向final relative path半写：current file intent fsync后，在其verified parent下只允许唯一derived basename `.harvis-candidate-file-<runId>-<8decimalCopyIndex>-<nonce>.next`，以O_EXCL/O_NOFOLLOW mode0600创建empty、parent fsync，并在首byte前把fileInstance写入candidateCurrentTempInstance。恢复时instance=null且derived temp absent，在candidate root/parent/manifest authority仍exact时允许按记录nonce执行首次O_EXCL创建；若derived temp已存在，则只接受该唯一empty temp并先记录instance；instance non-null只接受same dev/ino/uid/gid/nlink/creationXattrs、current mode0600|manifest mode、bytes为source/manifest expected exact prefix及xattrs为空或manifest final xattrs，随后继续copy、fsync、fchmod、写allowlisted provenance、重验full SHA/size/same instance，再RENAME_EXCL到final leaf并fsync parent。rename crash后只接受derived temp absent且final leaf逐项exact等于manifest，再推进cursor；两者同时present、foreign instance、wrong prefix/mode/xattr或unexpected derived temp均exit7。directory item只允许intent→mkdirat exact final path→parent fsync→identity recheck→cursor，不存在partial directory content。next tree枚举cap=entryCount+1，除manifest paths外最多只允许当前intent绑定的一个derived temp。若需要放弃copy，只能在candidateCurrent temp已按instance删除后，以manifest postorder在recorded candidate root fd内逐项验证current uid/no-follow/kind/nlink/mode/content/xattrs并unlinkat/rmdir；允许的唯一内容不一致是仍由same fileInstance绑定的current temp，extra path或任一identity漂移都保留exit7，绝不pathname递归删除。

next完整且cursor=count后逐file/dir fsync、receipt/App-tree/codesign重验，RENAME_EXCL为HarvisBar.app并fsync parent；再以candidateRecordWrite统一协议发布candidate.next→candidate.json。candidate exact required=schemaVersion,generation,head,tree,receiptSha256,appTreeSha256,candidateIdentitySha256,signingMode，schema const=harvis.source-checkpoint-candidate.v1、signingMode const=adHocLocalCandidate、additionalProperties=false。candidateIdentitySha256 exact=`SHA256("harvis-source-candidate-identity-v1\0"||u64be(appRootDev)||u64be(appRootIno)||u32be(appRootMode)||raw32(appTreeSha256))` bare hex；candidateRecordWrite.expectedSha256与candidateRecordSha256都必须等于SHA256(canonical candidate.json bytes) bare hex，只有baseIdentity non-null后才进入candidateReady。closed recovery tuple只有：(a)manifest bound + base/json absent + own next absent|manifest-valid partial→按cursor继续或identity-bound重建；(b)own complete next + base absent→RENAME_EXCL；(c)App base exact + candidateRecordWrite null|intent|next-prefix|prepared→按manifestWrite协议创建/续写/发布record；(d)base+record exact且candidateRecordWrite.baseIdentity non-null、verification write intent尚未建立→准备该intent；任何missing/wrong manifest、foreign next、base/record/verification mismatch均exit7。verification passed branch必须绑定candidateRecordSha256与candidateIdentitySha256。source-bound plan不接受任意candidate path，closed input只含checkpoint generation并从该fixed candidate authority派生；candidate bytes因此可在distribution scratch清理后继续供plan读取，但distributionRun non-null时仍不可消费。failed/infra的candidateRecordWrite恒null且不promotion；aborted/rolledBack/accepted后只按verified terminal evidence做candidate identity-bound cleanup，pending/candidatePending必须保留。

本Goal的transaction public CLI由本段连同§9.1旧code block一起最终覆盖：`plan --checkpoint-generation <1...8> --mode adHocLocalCandidate --sign-identity - --allow-adhoc-candidate`；`apply --transaction-id <id> --approval-fingerprint <sha256> [--fda-on-failure rollback|prompt]`，不再接收candidate-app；status/rollback/commit tails保持§9.1。plan从fixed candidate authority写入candidate identity/manifest到journal，apply只重验journal派生candidate，不重新接收pathname。任意candidate-app、stableCertificate或其他sign identity在本Goal production CLI都usage exit2/0 transaction；通用stable/custom candidate属于冻结backlog，不能形成第二schema branch。

scratch由verified generation commit/tree物化，不读live worktree文件。derived root basename固定`<generation>.<runId>.scratch`、mode0700；intent在首次mkdir前已持久化runId，root absent时才允许mkdirat。mkdir+distribution-runs parent fsync后立即把bare SHA256(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-scratch-root-identity.v1",dev,ino,uid,gid,mode}`)写scratchRootIdentitySha256；crash在mkdir或parent fsync后而journal前，只接受该唯一derived basename为current-uid/mode0700 empty directory、无ACL/xattr/flags且parent authority exact，补fsync并capture同一identity，否则exit7。root-only prefix只允许direct roster empty。随后O_EXCL创建唯一mode0600 `owner.json`；owner exact required=`schemaVersion,runId,generation,uid,rootIdentitySha256`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-scratch-owner.v1`、uid=current uid且rootIdentitySha256逐值等于journal。create+root fsync后、首byte前写scratchOwnerTempInstance=fileInstance；crash在create后而instance前只接受唯一empty/current-uid/mode0600/nlink1/empty-xattr owner并先记录instance，instance non-null后只接受same instance与canonical owner expected exact prefix，继续写/fsync/fchmod0600。scratchOwnerSha256为owner canonical bytes bare SHA；scratchOwnerIdentitySha256为bare SHA256(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-scratch-owner-file-identity.v1",dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,nlink,size,contentSha256,xattrs}`)，要求current uid、mode0600、nlink1、size/content SHA等于owner、xattrs empty。scratchIdentitySha256固定为SHA256(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-scratch-identity.v1",rootIdentitySha256,ownerSha256,ownerIdentitySha256}`) bare hex，不使用含糊连接或A-domain别名。owner file+root+distribution-runs parent全部fsync后，owner/ownerIdentity/final identity三项在同一revision由null变为non-null，形成五tuple全non-null。intent crash若root absent可按runId创建；root-only或owner-prefix按上述协议续成完整五tuple。若此时已锁定infra，也必须先补全五tuple再生成cleanup manifest，不得从partial prefix直接terminal；完整五tuple后只接受下述immutable tree-roster prefix。foreign marker/extra/symlink/special保留exit7。

root direct children最终exact为`owner.json,source,build,output,stage,tmp,vendor-input-cargo-home,empty-cargo-home,control`；八directory均0700，source materialized后只读，control只供coordinator/managed worker且永不进入sandbox parameter/env/inner argv，其他可写且彼此为sibling，`@BUILD`不能覆盖vendor-input/control。materialized只在owner、完整source、minimal Git view、vendor input、toolchain facts及全部root/direct-child fsync后发布；此前保持intent。唯一reader transcript先运行repositoryPrefix + `ls-tree -r -z --full-tree --format=%(objectmode)%x20%(objecttype)%x20%(objectname)%x20%(objectsize)%x09%(path) <validatedCommit>`，deadline60s、stdout≤64MiB、stderr empty；每record exact grammar=`mode SP type SP oid SP canonicalDecimalSize TAB rawPath NUL`，无padding/leading-zero，只接受100644|100755 + blob、object-format OID、size0...128MiB、unique valid relative raw path，≤20000项/总≤2GiB。随后单次运行repositoryPrefix + `cat-file --batch`，stdin为roster顺序每OID+LF，stdout每项exact=`OID SP blob SP canonicalDecimalSize LF` + exact size raw bytes + LF，missing/ambiguous分支不存在；shared aggregate monotonic deadline1200s、stdout≤2GiB+4MiB header budget、stderr empty。用root-fd mkdirat/openat O_EXCL/O_NOFOLLOW写入、SHA/OID重算、fsync并fchmod source mode，tree digest必须等于generation expectedTree。任一exit/timeout/overflow/duplicate/type/path/size/OID/trailing-byte mismatch先journal outcome=infra/terminalError=MATERIALIZATION_FAILED，再按owner+immutable generation roster生成cleanup manifest；0 gate/artifact。

`control` direct roster固定唯一`jobs` directory。scratch五tuple完整后，先按root-fd no-follow创建/接受current-uid mode0700 empty control，再创建/接受current-uid mode0700 empty control/jobs；每个mkdir后fsync parent，crash在mkdir后journal前只接受对应唯一empty/no-ACL/xattr/flags directory。两层重开后写`controlJobsIdentitySha256=SHA256(canonical exact {schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-control-jobs-identity.v1",controlDev,controlIno,controlUid,controlGid,controlMode,jobsDev,jobsIno,jobsUid,jobsGid,jobsMode})` bare hex。jobs roster按state exact：currentCommand=null时empty；jobCreateIntent允许empty或唯一attemptId的pre-journal empty root gap；jobRootCreated起至任一cleanup cursor<9恰有该唯一root；jobCleaned/rootedFailureCleaned/preRootCleaned及cleanup cursor=9时empty。任何时刻观察cap=2（预期项+1）；每次job side effect/恢复/cleanup前后都从scratch root fd重走control/jobs并要求hash exact。parent swap、extra sibling或第二attempt root一律exit7；两directory最终由scratch cleanup manifest按recorded identity删除。

为兼容packager/provenance内部只读Git，coordinator在scratch source内创建唯一minimal `.git` view：directory0700，HEAD为validated detached commit+LF，config只含core repositoryformatversion/bare=false/worktree与object-format conditional，objects/info/alternates只写root-walk verified commonObjects canonical path，index由fixed scratch Git `read-tree <expectedTree>`生成并验证全stage0/H；无refs/logs/remotes/hooks。该view只属于scratch；source-oriented Git由cwd=@SOURCE或现有脚本的`git -C @SOURCE`自然发现它，任何gate都不注入/继承GIT_DIR或GIT_WORK_TREE，transaction tests切换到自己的fixture cwd。source lock持有期间common object authority仍逐gate重验。cleanup只接受marker+identity+closed source/build/output/stage/git-view roster，逐项unlinkat/postorder rmdir，禁用pathname rm -rf。

Rust source-cache authority只从getpwuid account home fixed `.cargo` entry取得；该entry允许current-uid symlink，但必须用root-fd/no-follow逐跳解析最多8个absolute/relative target component，记录每个link bytes+dev/ino/uid/mode/xattrs与每段directory identity。`vendorCacheXattrs`只允许下列name的任意无duplicate子集并按name raw byte排序：`com.apple.fileprovider.ignore#P`,`com.apple.metadata:com_apple_backup_excludeItem`,`com.apple.provenance`；每个value SHA-256进入identity，`com.apple.quarantine`/unknown/read failure/value drift均阻断且绝不strip。最终cache只能是current-uid、非group-or-other-writable、无ACL、xattrs满足vendorCacheXattrs、flags为0的directory。真实cache始终只读且绝不直接传给gate。

coordinator先从immutable generation tree用同一strict TOML parser建立local workspace package roster：root `Cargo.toml`必须只有explicit、unique、canonical relative `[workspace].members`（本Goal不接受glob/absolute/dot component，cap64），逐member no-follow读取tracked regular `<member>/Cargo.toml`，从`[package].name`与canonical explicit version或`version.workspace=true`+root `[workspace.package].version`派生unique `{name,version,manifestPath,manifestBlobSha256}`。Cargo.lock parser随后把每个unique package严格分成两类：(a) local workspace entry的source/checksum必须同时absent，且name/version与上述roster一一对应；(b) external registry entry必须source为exact literal `registry+https://github.com/rust-lang/crates.io-index`、checksum为valid lowercase SHA-256。source/checksum只缺一项、无manifest可证明的source-less entry、local entry携带source/checksum、git/alternate registry、duplicate或missing/invalid registry checksum全部拒绝。只有(b)构成`registryPackageRoster`并进入archive/index/vendor；(a)只由generation source tree参与build，绝不查cache或生成vendor child。真实`registry/cache`与`registry/index`各自direct child先bounded枚举，唯一匹配oracle是两侧存在同一ASCII cache-key basename，且该cache root包含全部exact registry `<name>-<version>.crate`、每个raw SHA逐byte等于lock checksum，index root的canonical UTF-8 `config.json` exact JSON只含`dl="https://static.crates.io/crates"`,`api="https://crates.io"`。0或多于1个key匹配都失败。每个package name必须lowercase ASCII Cargo name、version必须canonical SemVer；registry roster拼接basename `<name>-<version>`必须全局唯一。sparse index leaf只为registry name唯一派生：length1=`1/<name>`、length2=`2/<name>`、length3=`3/<first>/<name>`、其他=`<first2>/<next2>/<name>`，只读取`config.json`及`.cache/<derived leaf>`，intermediate directories和这些regular leaves构成exact copied index roster，真实index中的其他cache项不读取正文也不复制。leaf missing/symlink/special/hardlink/oversize或前后identity/bytes/xattrs漂移失败。明确不信任也不读取/copy live `registry/src`。

以fd-anchored copier写scratch sibling `@VENDOR_INPUT_CARGO_HOME`：只含registryPackageRoster所需`registry/cache/<matched-key>/<name>-<version>.crate`、上述closed `registry/index/<matched-key>` projection与empty `.package-cache`，local workspace entry为0 archive/index/vendor child；不预建src，排除/拒绝config.toml、credentials、token、bin、git、symlink/special/hardlink。目录≤20000、文件≤50000、单file≤128MiB、总≤4GiB，mode0700/0600、xattrs必须为空、全file+dir fsync。copier不复制live cache xattrs；只可在其本次O_EXCL/mkdirat创建且仍被identity绑定的private descendant上清除意外继承的allowlisted xattr，无法清空即失败，不得触碰真实cache。copy前后重验真实archive/index bytes/identity/xattrs并将source authority与private tree digest写入distributionRun；missing/drift/cap/checksum失败为TOOLCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE，真实cache 0 write。

vendor command可在private home内自行创建registry/src；成功后coordinator不信任其结果，而是用bounded gzip+tar reader从registryPackageRoster每个已认证archive独立生成expected payload roster：只接受唯一exact `<name>-<version>/` root、strict UTF-8 relative path、directory或regular file、无absolute/empty/dot-dot/duplicate/link/special/PAX path rewrite，且archive payload不得自带`.cargo-checksum.json`；单file≤128MiB、总≤4GiB、entry≤50000，每个regular payload SHA与mode进入roster。因为命令固定`--versioned-dirs`，vendor root direct children必须逐registry package一一等于`<name>-<version>`，不得使用`<name>`简称、出现local workspace child或extra/collision；每个registry package directory除archive-derived payload外只额外允许Cargo生成的一份`.cargo-checksum.json`。该JSON只接受exact parsed `{files,package}`、additionalProperties=false，package=lock checksum，files map必须逐项等于archive payload的relative-path→SHA且不得自列；排除该generated leaf后，`@BUILD/vendor/<name>-<version>`必须root-anchored/no-follow与archive roster一一相等。tampered live src完全不参与结果；tampered archive、private unpack、checksum JSON或vendor file任一byte都失败。

rust-release-build commandIndex=0固定为 `@CARGO vendor --manifest-path @SOURCE/Cargo.toml --locked --offline --versioned-dirs @BUILD/vendor`，且只该command的CARGO_HOME=`@VENDOR_INPUT_CARGO_HOME`；它可在private copy内取得`.package-cache` O_RDWR/flock及生成private registry/src。coordinator随后按上段archive-derived registry roster、local workspace manifest roster与Cargo.lock两类闭包重验vendor/source、无symlink/special、同一caps，并以0600、file+parent fsync原子写exact `@BUILD/cargo-home/config.toml`。canonical UTF-8 TOML bytes只含 `[source.crates-io] replace-with="vendored-sources"`、`[source.vendored-sources] directory="@BUILD/vendor"`、`[net] offline=true`，固定section/key顺序与terminal LF，不创建nested `.cargo/config.toml`。commandIndex=1与rust-tests使用CARGO_HOME=@BUILD/cargo-home，不获得vendor-input sibling authority。vendor任何non-success或roster/checksum/local-manifest失配为TOOLCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE infra，0 consumable artifact。Rust build/test env再固定 `CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS=--remap-path-prefix=@SOURCE=/src/harvis<US>--remap-path-prefix=@BUILD=/build`；Swift manifest必须0 remote dependency。sandbox对所有role都deny network create/bind/listen/connect/DNS/Unix/external；唯一测试通信只使用下段worker预先建立并显式继承的numeric-loopback connected FD pool与allocation-ledger FD，不给sandbox内命令访问任意127.0.0.1/::1服务的能力。

为使真实rust-tests与transaction-tests在该profile下可执行，Phase3给Rust fixture seam、`tests/test_local_smoke.py`与`scripts/harvis_local_smoke.sh`增加只在下文exact pool env存在时启用的闭合adapter。allocator通过固定ledger FD做跨进程`fcntl`互斥并原子领取唯一pairIndex，再分别dup对应server/client connected TCP FD；formal child与HTTP client只可read/write/close这些dup，替代socket/curl/launchctl/account-home读取。adapter不得socket/bind/listen/connect，仍保留health/task/permission请求、PID退出、超时与断言语义，全部状态只在verified `HARVIS_TEST_TMP_ROOT=@BUILD/tmp`。每次领取把fixed-size canonical allocation record写入预绑定usage leaf并fsync；重复/越界/gap/partial record拒绝。普通测试未设置pool env时行为逐byte不变。transaction-tests另独有`HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_TEST_MODE=synthetic-loopback-v1`；worker在command结束后从same-instance usage leaf重算领取前缀、证明formal child/client退出、pool FD/dup已关闭且0 writer，才能生成worker result。

tracked常量GATE_ROSTER_V1按下表锁定。argv/env token只允许canonical @SOURCE,@BUILD,@OUTPUT,@STAGE,@CARGO,@RUSTC,@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_BIN,@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT,@VENDOR_INPUT_CARGO_HOME,@COMMON_OBJECTS,@DEVELOPER_DIR,@AUTHOR_EPOCH,@TOOLCHAIN_FACTS_SHA,@PROVENANCE_SHA,@LOOPBACK_POOL_BASE,@LOOPBACK_POOL_COUNT,@LOOPBACK_LEDGER_FD,@COMMAND_STARTED_MONOTONIC_NS,@COMMAND_DEADLINE_MONOTONIC_NS,@BOOT_SESSION_IDENTITY_SHA256占位；pool三项与command-time三项只允许rust-tests与transaction-tests environment template。@DEVELOPER_DIR只来自root-walk verified `/usr/bin/xcode-select -p` 的唯一absolute stdout并绑定目录identity，@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT来自`@RUSTC --print sysroot` exact exit0/stdout=唯一canonical absolute path+LF/stderr empty且`@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_BIN=@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT/bin`，并与rustup which cargo/rustc同root交叉验证。@AUTHOR_EPOCH只来自immutable generation journal，@TOOLCHAIN_FACTS_SHA来自下文fixed canonical toolchain facts。@PROVENANCE_SHA只在bundle commandIndex=0 success后从fixed provenance manifest no-follow读取并重算：coordinator在该command receipt移入completedCommandReceipts的同一revision先把bare值写distributionRun.provenanceSha256，随后bundle commandIndex=1与bundle-contract均只从该immutable field展开；此前二者不得构造argvFingerprint。pool三占位固定展开为198、32、197；command-time三占位逐值展开为currentCommand中已持久化的canonical unsigned decimal started/deadline与64-lower-hex boot digest，并进入environment/planned/final transport。其他占位只能在各command `plistCreated`前从verified fd/tool/currentCommand facts展开。gateRosterFingerprint只哈希static segments；per-command argv/transport及最终commandsFingerprint才绑定expanded value digest，因此动态provenance/FD/time不形成预计算环且不落raw private path。signing不是占位：本地candidate固定ad-hoc literal `-`，verification/candidate record明确adHocLocalCandidate，transaction plan仍要求allow-adhoc Approval。任何caller gate/reason/argv/env/sign输入均usage failure。

| gateId | reason | exact executable / argv template |
| --- | --- | --- |
| swift-release-build | build | /usr/bin/swift build --package-path @SOURCE/apps/HarvisBar --scratch-path @BUILD/swift-release -c release -Xswiftc -gnone -Xswiftc -file-prefix-map -Xswiftc @SOURCE=/src/harvis |
| rust-release-build | build | ordered commands: (0) @CARGO vendor --manifest-path @SOURCE/Cargo.toml --locked --offline --versioned-dirs @BUILD/vendor；(1) @CARGO build --manifest-path @SOURCE/Cargo.toml --workspace --release --locked --offline --target-dir @BUILD/cargo-release |
| swift-tests | test | /usr/bin/swift test --package-path @SOURCE/apps/HarvisBar --scratch-path @BUILD/swift-test |
| rust-tests | test | @CARGO test --manifest-path @SOURCE/Cargo.toml --workspace --locked --offline --target-dir @BUILD/cargo-test |
| path-guard-tests | test | /usr/bin/python3 -I -B -S @SOURCE/tests/test_package_path_guard.py |
| transaction-tests | test | /usr/bin/python3 -I -B -S -m unittest discover -s @SOURCE/tests -t @SOURCE -p test_*.py |
| bundle-package | package | ordered commands: (0) /usr/bin/python3 -I -B -S @SOURCE/scripts/harvis_release_provenance.py stage --source @SOURCE --output @STAGE/release --source-date-epoch @AUTHOR_EPOCH --toolchain-facts @BUILD/toolchain-facts.json --toolchain-facts-sha256 @TOOLCHAIN_FACTS_SHA --artifact harvis-runtime @BUILD/cargo-release/release/harvis bin/harvis --artifact harvis-system-extension @BUILD/swift-release/release/HarvisPerAppProxyExtension artifacts/HarvisPerAppProxyExtension --artifact harvisbar-main @BUILD/swift-release/release/HarvisBar artifacts/HarvisBar --artifact harvis-version-oracle @BUILD/swift-release/release/HarvisVersionAcceptanceOracle artifacts/HarvisVersionAcceptanceOracle；(1) /bin/bash --noprofile --norc @SOURCE/scripts/harvis_bar_package_app.sh --app-path @OUTPUT/HarvisBar.app --sign-identity - --provenance-stage @STAGE/release --provenance-sha256 @PROVENANCE_SHA；commandIndex=1 exact env overlay=`HARVIS_PLUGIN_ROOT=@SOURCE,HARVIS_PACKAGE_OUTPUT_ROOT=@OUTPUT` |
| bundle-contract | package | /usr/bin/python3 -I -B -S @SOURCE/scripts/harvis_app_bundle_verify.py --app @OUTPUT/HarvisBar.app --source @SOURCE --provenance-stage @STAGE/release --provenance-sha256 @PROVENANCE_SHA --variant bar --bundle-id local.harzva.harvisbar --version 0.36.0 --executable HarvisBar --codesign-policy required |
| codesign-verify | package | /usr/bin/codesign --verify --deep --strict @OUTPUT/HarvisBar.app |

/usr/bin swift/python3/codesign、/bin/bash与/usr/bin/env逐component root-owned/no-follow/非group-or-other-writable验证。@CARGO/@RUSTC只由getpwuid account-home下已验证rustup proxy的bounded symlink chain解析；rustup which stdout必须是唯一absolute toolchain/bin/cargo|rustc+LF，resolved parent chain current-uid/no-follow/非group-or-other-writable、leaf regular executable/nlink1，二者同toolchain root；`rustc --print sysroot`再绑定完整root及lib/rustlib closed identity，process-exec只允许bin而file-read允许完整root，否则TOOLCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE。`xcode-select -p`、rustup which、rustc sysroot均deadline5s/每流1MiB、exit0、stderr empty、stdout唯一值+LF；每个probe/command前后都重验输出与root identity。所有gate cwd=@SOURCE fd；stdin=/dev/null，CLOEXEC_DEFAULT，由下文managed job持有唯一rootRef。

coordinator在gate前只运行四个version probe：`@CARGO --version`、`@RUSTC -Vv`、`DEVELOPER_DIR=@DEVELOPER_DIR /usr/bin/swift --version`、`DEVELOPER_DIR=@DEVELOPER_DIR /usr/bin/xcodebuild -version`；另运行不作为version field的`@RUSTC --print sysroot` authority probe。全部stdin=/dev/null、deadline5s、每流1MiB、exit0、stderr exact empty；version stdout必须nonempty valid UTF-8、无NUL/C0（LF除外）、exactly one terminal LF、无trailing blank line且两遍raw bytes相等，versionOutputSha256只哈希该raw stdout。sysroot stdout仍按上文唯一canonical absolute path+LF grammar。sandbox-exec/git/env/python3/codesign/bash/launchctl禁止version probe且versionOutputSha256 const null，但其exact executable file identity在每次使用前后重验。

coordinator以四份version bytes、sysroot、现有release-provenance public-safe sanitizer与platform/python facts生成canonical `@BUILD/toolchain-facts.json`，mode0600、≤64KiB、file+parent fsync；@TOOLCHAIN_FACTS_SHA是其bare SHA，toolchainAuthorityFingerprint绑定全部tool/root identities和上述conditional version SHA。每个probe/gate前后重验xcode-select output+Developer root及Rust root，A→B replacement为TOOLCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE且0继续。Phase3给harvis_release_provenance.py新增且只新增paired `--toolchain-facts/--toolchain-facts-sha256`：两项必须同时出现，no-follow重验canonical schema/hash后作为manifest toolchain，禁止再次PATH探测或混用override。missing/mismatch/unknown pair为TOOLCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE，0 stage/artifact。

所有distribution fingerprint统一为`F(payload)="sha256:"+SHA256(canonical ASCII JSON(payload))`，payload均additionalProperties=false。静态`templateValue` exact required=`segments`、additionalProperties=false；segments为nonempty ordered array，每段required exact=`kind,token`，kind=`literal|placeholder`。literal token通常只允许nonempty bytes 0x20...0x7E；唯一例外是environment key exact `CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS`的template可在两个remap flags之间含exactly one raw US byte 0x1F（不在首尾），其canonical JSON token必须编码为`\u001f`并在parse后还原单个0x1F，五个printable bytes`<US>`绝不等价。其他C0、DEL、non-ASCII与NUL全部拒绝。placeholder token只允许GATE_ROSTER_V1占位且自身为0x20...0x7E。禁止相邻literal、empty segment与把placeholder字面塞进literal。执行期`expandedTemplateValue` exact required=`segments,expandedValueSha256`；segments逐byte等于static template，全部literal时digest必须null，否则为各段展开后raw bytes连接的bare SHA。因此`@STAGE/release`、`@SOURCE=/src/harvis`与含raw US分隔的RUSTFLAGS都有唯一segments编码，而尚未产生的@PROVENANCE_SHA仍可进入static roster。`argvFingerprint` payload exact keys=`schemaVersion,gateId,commandIndex,executable,arguments`，schema const=`harvis.distribution-argv.v1`；executable与每个argument都是expandedTemplateValue，array保持argv顺序。environment item required exact=`key,value`，key为ASCII且按key byte升序唯一，value为expandedTemplateValue。

`plannedTransportFingerprint`先于任何job-root side effect计算，payload exact keys=`schemaVersion,attemptId,argvFingerprint,environment,cwdIdentitySha256,stdinMode,sandboxProfileSha256,sandboxParameterRole,sandboxParameters,sandboxOuterArgvFingerprint,loopbackPoolPolicy,startedMonotonicNs,deadlineMonotonicNs,bootSessionIdentitySha256,deadlineMs,stdoutCapBytes,stderrCapBytes,aggregateCapBytes,managedJobContract`，schema const=`harvis.distribution-planned-transport.v1`；attemptId与三个absolute-time facts必须先在jobCreateIntent同一revision产生/采样/探测且逐值进入expected plist，deadline-start exact=`deadlineMs*1_000_000`。loopbackPoolPolicy只在rust-tests/transaction-tests为exact `{baseFd:198,pairCount:32,ledgerFd:197}`，其他command const null。`transportFingerprint`是每个command独有，payload exact keys=`schemaVersion,attemptId,plannedTransportFingerprint,argvFingerprint,environment,cwdIdentitySha256,stdinMode,sandboxProfileSha256,sandboxParameterRole,sandboxParameters,sandboxOuterArgvFingerprint,preconnectedLoopbackFingerprint,startedMonotonicNs,deadlineMonotonicNs,bootSessionIdentitySha256,deadlineMs,stdoutCapBytes,stderrCapBytes,aggregateCapBytes,managedJobContract`，schema
