const=`harvis.distribution-transport.v1`、stdinMode=`devNull`、sandboxParameterRole=`vendorInput|preconnectedLoopback|standard`、managedJobContract=`transientLaunchdRootRefV1`。两payload的其他同名字段必须逐byte相等；sandboxParameters按ASCII name升序，item exact=`{name,expandedValueSha256}`并覆盖全部fixed `-D` name→expanded raw value；sandboxOuterArgvFingerprint使用schema const=`harvis.distribution-sandbox-outer-argv.v1`与expandedTemplateValue绑定从`/usr/bin/sandbox-exec`到`--`及inner argv的完整ordered outer argv。preconnectedLoopbackFingerprint只在rust-tests/transaction-tests non-null，其他command const null。`gateRosterFingerprint` payload exact keys=`schemaVersion,sandboxProfileSha256,profileParameterNames,gates`，schema const=`harvis.distribution-gate-roster.v1`；parameter names按ASCII升序，gates按表顺序，每项exact=`gateIndex,gateId,reason,commands`，command exact=`commandIndex,argvTemplate,environmentOverlayTemplate,sandboxParameterRole,deadlineMs,stdoutCapBytes,stderrCapBytes,aggregateCapBytes`，两类template只能使用static templateValue，不含expandedValueSha256。

`toolchainAuthorityFingerprint` payload exact keys=`schemaVersion,developerDirIdentitySha256,rustToolchainRootIdentitySha256,tools`，schema const=`harvis.distribution-toolchain-authority.v1`，tools按fixed role byte排序，每项exact=`role,pathDigest,fileIdentitySha256,versionOutputSha256`并覆盖sandbox-exec/git/env/python3/swift/xcodebuild/codesign/bash/launchctl/sysctl/cargo/rustc。只有cargo、rustc、swift、xcodebuild四role的versionOutputSha256 non-null且来自§9.4.2 fixed probes；其余无稳定version接口的role该字段必须null并只由root-walk file identity授权，不能自选probe。sysctl role exact path=`/usr/sbin/sysctl`，每次boot-session probe前后重验同一file identity。`commandsFingerprint` payload exact keys=`schemaVersion,generation,head,tree,attempts`，schema const=`harvis.distribution-commands.v1`，attempts按执行顺序，每项exact=`gateIndex,gateId,gateReceiptSha256`；gateReceiptSha256为canonical完整receipt bytes bare SHA。reorder/prefix/extra/null/unknown segment/parameter或不同expanded `-D` value必须改变fingerprint或schema失败；Python/Swift共享golden逐字段证明bare-vs-prefixed规则。

每个commandReceipt对应一次独立的verified `/usr/bin/sandbox-exec` 与managed job，不能在multi-command gate中复用profile实例或权限。profile只能来自tracked `scripts/harvis_distribution_sandbox.sb` 的exact SANDBOX_PROFILE_V1，首行version1且top-level deny default。fixed outer argv=`/usr/bin/sandbox-exec -D SOURCE=@SOURCE -D BUILD=@BUILD -D OUTPUT=@OUTPUT -D STAGE=@STAGE -D TMP=@BUILD/tmp -D COMMON_OBJECTS=@COMMON_OBJECTS -D VENDOR_INPUT_CARGO_HOME=<role-derived> -D RUST_TOOLCHAIN_BIN=@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_BIN -D RUST_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT=@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT -D DEVELOPER_DIR=@DEVELOPER_DIR -D COMMAND_ROLE=<vendor|preconnectedLoopback|standard> -f @SOURCE/scripts/harvis_distribution_sandbox.sb -- <command executable+argv>`；禁止`-p|-n`、额外-D或caller profile，完整outer argv与全部expanded `-D` mapping必须进入该command transport。只rust-release-build commandIndex=0使用vendor及scratch sibling @VENDOR_INPUT_CARGO_HOME；rust-tests与transaction-tests使用preconnectedLoopback；其余全部standard，非vendor都把vendor参数指向fixed empty mode0700 `@BUILD/empty-cargo-home`。profile只允许process-fork/wait/signal-own-managed-job、sysctl-read，以及exact Mach global-name roster=`com.apple.cfprefsd.agent|com.apple.cfprefsd.daemon|com.apple.logd|com.apple.system.logger|com.apple.system.opendirectoryd.libinfo`；其他Mach lookup全deny，尤其securityd/Keychain、launchd、LaunchServices/lsd。process-exec只允许root-owned `/usr/bin`,`/bin`,`/usr/sbin`,`/sbin`、verified @DEVELOPER_DIR/@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_BIN、以及verified generation @SOURCE/@BUILD/@STAGE executables，并以literal deny `/usr/bin/sandbox-exec`,`/usr/bin/open`,`/usr/bin/security`,`/bin/launchctl`,`/usr/bin/ssh`,`/usr/bin/scp`,`/usr/bin/sftp`,`/usr/bin/curl`作defense-in-depth。file-read-content只允许`/System,/usr,/bin,/sbin,@DEVELOPER_DIR,@RUST_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT,@SOURCE,@BUILD,@OUTPUT,@STAGE,@COMMON_OBJECTS`，vendor另允许private @VENDOR_INPUT_CARGO_HOME；不允许泛`/Library`，并specific deny `/Library/Keychains`、account-home Keychains、live worktree、真实Cargo cache与其他account home内容。file-write*只允许@BUILD,@OUTPUT,@STAGE,TMP，vendor另允许private vendor-input；所有role的network*均deny。preconnectedLoopback role只允许继承currentCommand已绑定的64个connected TCP FD与一个allocation-ledger FD，不新增sandbox network capability。固定ad-hoc candidate不调用security identity或timestamp服务。profile bytes+parameter name/value roster进入上述exact fingerprints；任何profile parse/unknown param/exec/read/write/network/Mach canary失败都是infra failure且0 consumable artifact。真实canary必须覆盖把security/open/launchctl/curl复制到@BUILD再exec、Security.framework SecItem、NSWorkspace、direct sensitive Mach lookup、System.keychain read，以及两个pool command自行socket/connect任意loopback/DNS/外网，均失败；只有预连接FD上的HTTP成功，不能只依赖原binary pathname deny。

base env完全替换为 PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin,HOME=/var/empty,TMPDIR=@BUILD/tmp,LC_ALL=C,LANG=C,TZ=UTC,DEVELOPER_DIR=@DEVELOPER_DIR,GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1,GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null,GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0,GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1,GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1,GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0；不全局设置GIT_DIR/GIT_WORK_TREE，避免transaction tests中的临时repo被重定向，source Git依赖cwd=@SOURCE内synthetic `.git`。Rust vendor commandIndex=0只额外CARGO_HOME=@VENDOR_INPUT_CARGO_HOME,CARGO_NET_OFFLINE=true；Rust build/test只额外CARGO_HOME=@BUILD/cargo-home,CARGO_NET_OFFLINE=true,RUSTC=@RUSTC与fixed remap flags；四个test gate另且只另加`HARVIS_TEST_TMP_ROOT=@BUILD/tmp`。rust-tests与transaction-tests再且只再加`HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_LOOPBACK_POOL_BASE=@LOOPBACK_POOL_BASE,HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_LOOPBACK_POOL_COUNT=@LOOPBACK_POOL_COUNT,HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_LOOPBACK_LEDGER_FD=@LOOPBACK_LEDGER_FD,HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_STARTED_MONOTONIC_NS=@COMMAND_STARTED_MONOTONIC_NS,HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_DEADLINE_MONOTONIC_NS=@COMMAND_DEADLINE_MONOTONIC_NS,HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_BOOT_SESSION_IDENTITY_SHA256=@BOOT_SESSION_IDENTITY_SHA256`，固定pool values为198/32/197，时间值为journal-bound canonical unsigned decimal/64-lower-hex；transaction-tests额外有`HARVIS_DISTRIBUTION_TEST_MODE=synthetic-loopback-v1`。allocator必须逐值解析三项authority、要求deadline>started且与worker argv/handshake/transport fingerprint一致，所有mutex/fcntl/EINTR retry只到该absolute deadline；缺一、extra于其他command或值漂移均poolViolation。其他command对应七key必须absent。Phase3把tests/test_package_path_guard.py、tests/test_local_smoke.py与scripts/harvis_local_smoke.sh的测试临时根统一改为“该env存在时必须是verified existing @BUILD/tmp，否则保留原正常测试默认”，并按上文closed adapter保证distribution tests不写@SOURCE/private tmp/account-home。provenance不再PATH探测toolchain，只消费paired canonical toolchain facts。bundle-package commandIndex=1只额外且必须有`HARVIS_PLUGIN_ROOT=@SOURCE,HARVIS_PACKAGE_OUTPUT_ROOT=@OUTPUT`，其他package command无这两个key；@STAGE是existing mode0700 parent，release provenance final output固定@STAGE/release，其`.release.stage-*`只在@STAGE内创建。所有本地candidate的codesign timestamp固定none；notary/timestamp发布另属冻结远程范围。DYLD|LD|PYTHON|proxy|SSH|RUSTC_WRAPPER|CARGO_*|SWIFT_*继承值一律不存在。

build/test/package gate deadline分别1200s/1200s/1200s；codesign-verify为60s。除codesign每流1MiB/aggregate2MiB外，其余每流16MiB/aggregate64MiB。Phase3新增verified `scripts/harvis_distribution_command_worker.py`。attemptId为32-lowerhex且在本run唯一；coordinator必须先把`currentCommand.state=jobCreateIntent`与attemptId、labelDigest、plannedTransportFingerprint、expected plistSha256持久化，才可在coordinator-only `control/jobs/<attemptId>`产生首个filesystem side effect；该control path不进入任何sandbox allowlist、parameter、environment或inner argv，inner command的pathname open必须deny，只有显式继承FD197/198...261可用。job root固定mode0700；jobRootIdentitySha256=SHA256(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-job-root-identity.v1",dev,ino,uid,gid,mode}`) bare hex。root absent时mkdirat；crash在mkdir/fsync后而identity journal前只接受该唯一empty/current-uid/mode0700/no-ACL/xattr/flags derived root并补写identity。随后按fixed role顺序O_EXCL/O_NOFOLLOW预建七个0600 empty leaf：`handshake,result,loopback-usage,command-stdout,command-stderr,worker-stdout,worker-stderr`。每项leaf record exact=`role,pathDigest,fileInstance`，additionalProperties=false；pathDigest只哈希job-root-relative raw bytes，fileInstance复用§9.4.2 schema。每次create+root fsync后、首个write前把record追加到currentCommand.jobLeafInstances；crash在create后append前只接受当前role唯一empty exact leaf并先记录，array只能是fixed roster的0...7无gap prefix。七项完成后ioAuthoritySha256=SHA256(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-job-io-authority.v1",jobRootIdentitySha256,leaves}`) bare hex并写`jobIOCreated`。worker stdout/stderr两leaf分别作为plist StandardOutPath/StandardErrorPath，其他五leaf以closed argv传给worker；launchd只open已存在wrapper leaves，worker只open已存在same-instance handshake/result/usage/command-output leaves，任何一方不得按pathname重建或truncate。canonical XML transient plist fixed basename=`job.plist`并在job root O_EXCL创建，closed direct roster始终只允许七leaf+该plist的state-valid prefix；expected bytes/SHA已在jobCreateIntent绑定，create+root fsync后、首byte前写plistInstance，instance=null时允许absent→O_EXCL或唯一empty→record，随后只续写expected exact prefix，full fsync/fchmod0600后计算plistIdentitySha256=SHA256(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-job-plist-identity.v1",dev,ino,uid,gid,mode,nlink,size,contentSha256,xattrs:[]}`) bare hex并写`plistCreated`。job root/plist/七leaf均由currentCommand历史事实绑定；任何foreign/extra/wrong prefix为exit7，不得当新attempt重跑。

plist top-level exact keys=`Label,ProgramArguments,RunAtLoad,KeepAlive,AbandonProcessGroup,ProcessType,StandardOutPath,StandardErrorPath`，additional keys拒绝；RunAtLoad=true、KeepAlive=false、AbandonProcessGroup=false、ProcessType=Background，Program/WorkingDirectory/EnvironmentVariables必须absent。label固定ASCII `local.harzva.harvis.distribution.<runId>.<gateIndex>.<commandIndex>`；`labelDigest`是raw label ASCII bytes的bare SHA256，`domainDigest`是raw ASCII `gui/<canonical uid>` bytes的bare SHA256。ProgramArguments exact为`/usr/bin/python3,-I,-B,-S,@SOURCE/scripts/harvis_distribution_command_worker.py,--run-id,<runId>,--attempt-id,<attemptId>,--gate-index,<decimal>,--command-index,<decimal>,--started-monotonic-ns,<canonical u64>,--deadline-monotonic-ns,<canonical u64>,--boot-session-identity-sha256,<64lowerhex>,--handshake,<fixed handshake leaf>,--result,<fixed result leaf>,--loopback-usage,<fixed loopback-usage leaf>,--stdout,<fixed command-stdout leaf>,--stderr,<fixed command-stderr leaf>,--,<完整sandbox outer argv>`。attemptId与三个authority argv必须逐值等于currentCommand/planned transport，deadline-start exact等于gate deadline；worker在任何leaf open/clock comparison前strict parse，missing/duplicate/leading-zero/overflow/mismatch均退出且由coordinator按preRoot或transportFailure闭合。jobIO/plist bytes/identity、labelDigest、expanded argv及plannedTransportFingerprint完成并处于`plistCreated`后，才在下一revision写currentCommand.submitIntent；planned fingerprint绑定expanded command/role/fixed FD roster、attempt与三项time authority而preconnected channel digest尚null，final transportFingerprint必须等worker handshake产生channel fact并在resumeIntent前持久化。submitIntent前0 bootstrap。

currentCommand为null或required exact=`gateIndex,gateId,commandIndex,attemptId,state,labelDigest,bootSessionIdentitySha256,jobRootIdentitySha256,jobLeafInstances,plistSha256,plistInstance,plistIdentitySha256,ioAuthoritySha256,plannedTransportFingerprint,transportFingerprint,handshakeSha256,rootRefIdentitySha256,startedMonotonicNs,deadlineMonotonicNs,preconnectedLoopbackFingerprint,overflowObservedSha256,timeoutFenceSha256,terminationIntent,transportFailureReason,termSent,killSent,launchctlTranscript,workerResultSha256,teardownEvidence,preRootCleanupEvidence,preparedCommandReceipt,jobCleanupCursor,jobScratchCleaned`、additionalProperties=false。state exact=`jobCreateIntent|jobRootCreated|jobIOCreated|plistCreated|submitIntent|bootstrapped|rootedStopped|resumeIntent|running|terminationIntent|termSent|killSent|bootoutIntent|bootedOut|receiptPrepared|jobCleanupIntent|jobCleaned|rootedFailureCleanupIntent|rootedFailureCleaned|preRootCleanupIntent|preRootCleaned`。`bootSessionIdentitySha256`来自verified `/usr/sbin/sysctl -n kern.bootsessionuuid` exact uppercase UUID+LF raw bytes bare SHA，probe exit0/stderr empty/deadline5s/每流1MiB；恢复先重探，变化一律RECOVERY_REQUIRED。jobCreateIntent要求label/boot/planned/plist expected SHA non-null，root/io/plist instance+identity/final transport/handshake/rootRef/times/channel/overflow/timeoutFence/termination/failure/result/evidence/receipt为null，jobLeafInstances empty、cleanupCursor0、bool false；jobRootCreated要求root identity non-null且leaf array为0...7 fixed prefix；jobIOCreated要求7项+io authority；plistCreated要求plist instance/identity non-null；submitIntent才允许bootstrap，bootstrapped只推进state。rootedStopped起handshake/rootRef/times/final transport non-null，handshakeSha256与rootRefIdentitySha256均为same canonical handshake bytes bare SHA；`startedMonotonicNs<deadlineMonotonicNs`且差值exact=`gate deadlineMs*1_000_000`，恢复必须同boot identity。preconnected channel只在rust-tests/transaction-tests non-null；resumeIntent在发送CONT前持久化，running只在libproc证明same rootRef已继续后进入。overflowObservedSha256只允许下文durable marker full且已重验时由null变为non-null并保持；timeoutFenceSha256只允许同一result lock下durable timeout fence full且已重验时由null变为non-null，二者互斥。terminationIntent起其字段non-null；`transportFailureReason`只在terminationIntent=transportFailure non-null，closed enum=`partialResult|malformedResult|workerNotStopped|wrapperOutput|outputMismatch|unexpectedDeath|poolViolation|launchctlFailure|overflowCleanupFailed|resultArbitrationFailed`；workerResultSha256按下段branch nullable。除下文full durable overflow marker授权的worker-side inner member signal外，任何preRoot/rooted transport failure，或已分类timeout/signal/overflow/spawnFailure/vendor failure，必须在首个coordinator launchctl/bootout/unlink side effect前于同一run revision锁定outcome=infra与immutable terminalError；该唯一例外中marker本身就是worker-side teardown的write-ahead intent，worker无journal读取/ack通道；coordinator首次观察marker后仍必须在自己的任何teardown side effect前持久化overflowObservedSha256与outcome=infra/terminalError，不能把例外扩张到coordinator或filesystem cleanup；normal product exit则先锁outcome=failed，success保持pending。termSent/killSent在各side effect前后按state+boolean单调推进；bootoutIntent必须先于bootout，bootedOut要求teardownEvidence non-null。正常receipt branch要求receiptPrepared.preparedCommandReceipt non-null，随后jobCleanupIntent/cursor0；rooted transportFailure则从bootedOut直接进入rootedFailureCleanupIntent，receipt恒null。两branch按fixed删除序`plist,handshake,result,loopback-usage,command-stdout,command-stderr,worker-stdout,worker-stderr,root`推进0...9；每步只接受recorded same instance/identity present或该项已absent，present时no-follow unlink/rmdir+parent fsync后cursor+1。jobCleaned要求cursor9/root absent/jobScratchCleaned=true并把receipt移入completed array；rootedFailureCleaned同样要求cursor9/cleaned=true但0 receipt，下一revision只清current并继续infra scratch cleanup，不得重新分类error。preRootCleanupIntent复用同一cursor/roster；preRootCleaned同样要求cursor9/cleaned=true且preRoot evidence non-null。任何字段组合必须恰好match一个state branch，删除中途crash只能续该prefix。

Receipt move的最终时序对上段一句简称作收窄：`jobCleaned`仍保持preparedCommandReceipt且completed array/gateCursor未变；只有其后的下一revision才原子append array、推进gateCursor并清current。不得在进入jobCleaned的同一revision移动receipt。

`currentCommand`时间字段的最终定义同时替代上段“jobCreateIntent时times为null/rootedStopped才起表”的旧句：唯一clock contract为macOS `clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)`，checked `u64(sec)*1_000_000_000+u64(nsec)`且nsec=0...999999999；进入`jobCreateIntent`的同一revision即采样并持久化`startedMonotonicNs`与`deadlineMonotonicNs`，两者non-null、前者小于后者且差值exact=`gate deadlineMs*1_000_000`，直到command terminal清理完成逐byte不变，并由bootSessionIdentitySha256约束同一boot epoch。coordinator、worker与pool allocator只能使用该clock/epoch，不能混用mach absolute/continuous/wall clock。rootedStopped只继承，绝不重启预算。derived `preRootDeadlineMonotonicNs=min(startedMonotonicNs+60_000_000_000,deadlineMonotonicNs)`不入DTO，但Python/Swift schema golden与实现必须按同一整数公式重算。control root/leaf/plist、baseline/bootstrap、pool建立、handshake、loaded/rootRef oracle都必须在该pre-root deadline前完成；到期先在同一revision锁`outcome=infra/terminalError=GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED`并进入preRootCleanupIntent，0 resume/0 receipt。每次sysctl/launchctl/libproc probe的独立deadline为`min(10s,当前阶段剩余deadline)`（sysctl仍受更严5s），stdout/stderr各≤1MiB；timeout/cap/partial transcript先持久化closed failure，再按preRoot或rooted branch清理，禁止fixed sleep、无限poll或把cleanup失败改写成另一产品失败。进入cleanup后每次恢复调用仍各自≤10s，单次失败保留journal并exit7，不在一个invocation中无限重试。

`currentCommand` JSON Schema必须直接编码下列closed truth table；“保持”表示逐byte继承前态immutable fact，未列事实必须null/false/0/empty，不能靠实现端if-chain放宽：

| state | required exact delta / allowed prefix |
| --- | --- |
| jobCreateIntent | start/deadline按上文whole-command预算non-null；root/io/plist instance+identity/transcript/handshake/rootRef/evidence全空；leaf array=[]，cleanup=0 |
| jobRootCreated | rootIdentity non-null；leaf array为fixed roster长度0...7；下一个leaf只能absent或唯一empty pre-journal create gap |
| jobIOCreated | leaf长度7、ioAuthority non-null；plist允许`absent+instance null`、`empty+instance null`或`same instance+expected prefix(0...full)+identity null`三branch |
| plistCreated | plistInstance/plistIdentity non-null且full expected；transcript empty |
| submitIntent | creation facts保持；transcript只允许empty或唯一sequence0 baseline-absent print |
| bootstrapped | transcript为baseline print+bootstrap+loaded-print prefix；runtime/root facts仍null |
| rootedStopped | handshake/rootRef/final transport non-null；start/deadline逐byte继承jobCreateIntent；worker exact SIGSTOP；transcript保持loaded prefix |
| resumeIntent | rooted facts保持；CONT item尚absent或side-effect gap由same rootRef running证明 |
| running | transcript恰含一项CONT并可跟running print；termination/evidence/receipt null；overflowObservedSha256/timeoutFenceSha256均null，或仅overflow marker SHA non-null且worker处于bounded overflow cleanup |
| terminationIntent | terminationIntent non-null；transportFailure要求closed reason且receipt null，overflow要求overflowObservedSha256与full-result SHA non-null且timeout fence null，timeout要求timeoutFenceSha256 non-null而overflow/result SHA null，workerResult branch要求两fence SHA null且full result non-null |
| termSent | 任一first-trigger branch可进入；pre-call为termSent=false+TERM item absent，post-call为termSent=true+TERM item present；killSent=false |
| killSent | TERM已present；pre-call为killSent=false+KILL item absent，post-call为killSent=true+KILL item present |
| bootoutIntent | first-trigger facts保持；允许bootout item absent（pre-call/side-effect gap），或唯一bootout success item后跟0...8项post-bootout loadedActive\|loadedNoPid poll，再跟0...8项terminalAbsent prefix；一旦terminalAbsent出现不得再loaded，bootedOut前terminalAbsent最终至少1，duplicate bootout恒非法 |
| bootedOut | normal/loaded branch为bootout+terminalAbsent完成；transportFailure already-absent branch为唯一recoveryAbsent且bootoutCompleted=false；teardownEvidence non-null |
| receiptPrepared | preparedCommandReceipt non-null且逐字段绑定worker/transport/teardown，cleanup=0 |
| jobCleanupIntent | receipt保持，cleanup cursor=0...9且present/absent prefix与fixed roster一致 |
| jobCleaned | cleanup=9、root absent、jobScratchCleaned=true，receipt保持 |
| rootedFailureCleanupIntent | terminationIntent=transportFailure、reason/teardown non-null、receipt null；cleanup cursor=0...9且prefix exact |
| rootedFailureCleaned | failure facts保持、cleanup=9、root absent、jobScratchCleaned=true、receipt null |
| preRootCleanupIntent | 只能从jobCreateIntent...bootstrapped失败边界进入；runtime receipt/teardown全null，preRootCleanupEvidence允许pre-oracle null或下文notNeeded\|intent\|completed。intent的transcript只允许bootout absent，或唯一bootout success后0...8 post-bootout loaded poll且terminalAbsent=0；completed要求同一唯一bootout success、0...8 post-loaded后1...8 terminalAbsent；notNeeded要求0 bootout/terminal且唯一recoveryAbsent。duplicate bootout/terminal后loaded非法。evidence null/intent、labelAbsent=false或workerLeavesClosed=false时cleanup必须0；只有notNeeded/completed且labelAbsent/workerLeavesClosed=true才允许cleanup=0...9 |
| preRootCleaned | cleanup=9、root absent、preRootCleanupEvidence non-null、jobScratchCleaned=true |

effect→journal gap恢复也closed：CONT intent后若same rootRef已running，按历史顺序补empty-channel CONT transcript，否则只向same stopped rootRef重发CONT；TERM/KILL intent后若same rootRef仍在，只允许向该identity重发对应signal，若已消失则以terminal census补其expected-empty transcript；bootoutIntent且尚无bootout success item时，loaded才可向same label重做，actual absent proof则先补expected-empty bootout item、再追加该proof的terminalAbsent item；已有bootout success item时，loaded只追加bounded post-bootout poll，绝不重做，absent只追加terminalAbsent。任何PID/start identity、boot session、label/plist或operation order不唯一都exit7；不得向pathname/复用PID/不同label重放。

rootRef曾绑定但fresh print已为recoveryAbsent的transportFailure例外不进入bootoutIntent，也不合成bootout item：先锁run infra/error与terminationIntent，再完成root/process/FD/socket census，写teardownEvidence.bootoutCompleted=false后直接进入bootedOut→rootedFailureCleanupIntent。只有此closed branch可绕过bootoutIntent；其他rooted branch必须bootoutCompleted=true。

上表所有transcript数量/形态均指下文定义的main successful projection；每态还必须接受且只接受该operation位置0...8个classification=failure block，以及下文closed alternates各自允许的recoveryAbsent。它们仍计入64项raw transcript与fingerprint，但不推进main projection。除此之外插入、重排或跨state failure/recovery item均match-count=0。

launchctl outer roster仅允许verified `/bin/launchctl bootstrap gui/<uid> <plist>`、`print gui/<uid>/<label>`、`kill CONT|TERM|KILL gui/<uid>/<label>`、`bootout gui/<uid>/<label>`；无legacy load/start/stop。每次调用遵守上文独立deadline/每流1MiB cap；timeout/cap同样记录actual failure item并进入对应cleanup。bootstrap/kill/bootout的success classification要求exit0、stdout/stderr empty。loaded print success要求exit0/stderr empty、stdout≤1MiB、valid UTF-8/no NUL/CR/C0除TAB/LF、exact one terminal LF、first line exact `gui/<uid>/<label> = {\n`、last line exact `}\n`；逐raw line不trim。active branch恰有一行exact `\tpid = <canonical positive decimal>\n`。loaded-no-pid branch恰为0条pid line，只允许三处closed cleanup/poll oracle：(1) bootstrap success后、rootRef仍null的preRoot cleanup，且label/plist digest与currentCommand一致，直接bootout、0 receipt；(2) rootRef已绑定而libproc证明recorded PID/start identity absent的unexpectedDeath cleanup，禁止再发TERM/KILL并直接bootout；(3) transcript已有唯一bootout successEmpty且state为bootoutIntent或preRoot bootout intent时的post-bootout poll，只证明same label/plist尚未terminal absent，不得绑定/改写rootRef、生成receipt或再次bootout。任何其他active阶段0 pid、去掉leading TAB后以`pid =`开头的unknown/duplicate行都拒绝；其他line只作opaque不落DTO。absent print沿用下段exit113 oracle。任一不满足success/absent grammar的实际调用仍须以failure classification和actual exit/channel SHA入transcript，但不得作为side-effect成功证据。

每次真实launchctl调用都先有对应currentCommand state intent，返回后把exact transcript item追加到最多64项launchctlTranscript；effect→journal gap只允许下文明确的recovery-synthesized success item。item required exact=`sequence,operation,signal,classification,argvFingerprint,exitCode,stdoutSha256,stderrSha256`，additionalProperties=false，operation=`bootstrap|print|kill|bootout`、signal只在kill为CONT|TERM|KILL，classification=`baselineAbsent|recoveryAbsent|loadedActive|loadedNoPid|terminalAbsent|successEmpty|failure`且必须与raw grammar或唯一recovery inference对应。baselineAbsent只允许准备继续bootstrap的首次absent oracle；recoveryAbsent只允许从未持久化bootout intent/effect的cleanup证明label自行不存在；terminalAbsent只允许已记录successEmpty bootout后。argvFingerprint=F(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-launchctl-argv.v1",operation,signal,domainDigest,labelDigest,plistSha256}`)，domain/label digest按上段bare定义；launchctlTranscriptFingerprint=F(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-launchctl-transcript.v1",items}`)。normal branch以submitIntent取得唯一baselineAbsent；删除classification=failure|recoveryAbsent后的normal successful projection为：baselineAbsent→bootstrap successEmpty→0...16 loaded print（rootedStopped前最终至少1）→CONT successEmpty→0...N running print→可选TERM successEmpty→可选KILL successEmpty→bootout successEmpty→0...8 post-bootout loadedActive|loadedNoPid poll→1...8 terminalAbsent。每个write-ahead operation位置只允许同operation最多8个连续failure item；failure不推进effect，baseline/loaded failure保留原态，CONT failure转transportFailure/launchctlFailure，TERM/KILL/bootout failure保留current+exit7直到same intent成功。closed alternate transcript exact为：(a) 任一pre-bootstrap source prefix（jobCreateIntent...submitIntent，但尚无baselineAbsent）进入preRoot后，cleanup print可有0...8 failure，随后唯一recoveryAbsent→preRoot cleanup；(b) 已有baselineAbsent后bootstrap返回0...8 failure且恢复仍absent：唯一recoveryAbsent→preRoot cleanup；(c) bootstrap success（含recovery补写）后、rooted前失败且same label为loadedActive或loadedNoPid：loaded prefix→bootout successEmpty→0...8 post-bootout loadedActive|loadedNoPid poll→1...8 terminalAbsent→preRoot cleanup；(d) bootstrap success后、bootout intent前label自行消失：唯一recoveryAbsent→preRoot cleanup；(e) rootRef曾绑定后label自行消失：唯一recoveryAbsent→transportFailure/unexpectedDeath rooted cleanup，0 TERM/KILL/bootout；(f) rootRef已绑定且label为loadedActive的CONT失败或其他rooted transport failure：只有reason=launchctlFailure时保留对应0...8 failure block，其他reason不得伪造failure item；随后TERM successEmpty→可选KILL successEmpty→bootout successEmpty→0...8 post-bootout loadedActive|loadedNoPid poll→1...8 terminalAbsent→rooted cleanup，若CONT从未成功不得伪造CONT；(g) rootRef已绑定但label为loadedNoPid且prior PID/start已由libproc证明absent：0 TERM/KILL，直接bootout successEmpty→0...8 post-bootout loadedActive|loadedNoPid poll→1...8 terminalAbsent→transportFailure/unexpectedDeath rooted cleanup。(a)(b)(d)(e)不得伪造bootout；(c)(g)不得伪造TERM/KILL。sequence从0连续，failure block只能位于其intent位置；kill-before-rootRef、duplicate successful bootstrap/CONT/bootout、bootout后kill均match-count=0。若bootstrap side effect后crash而item/state未推进，recovery loaded print先证明same label+plist，再按历史顺序补bootstrap successEmpty(empty channel hashes)并追加该actual loaded item。CONT/TERM/KILL/bootout effect gap同理：只有same rootRef running/absent与terminal census唯一证明effect后，才能在actual proof item之前补对应empty-channel success item；尤其preRoot或rooted `bootoutState/bootoutIntent=intent`恢复时若actual print已absent，必须先补bootout successEmpty，再把该actual exit113按terminalAbsent追加，绝不得标recoveryAbsent。一旦某branch已有bootout successEmpty，后续loadedActive|loadedNoPid只能作为最多8项post-bootout poll追加并等待terminalAbsent，绝不再次调用或补第二个bootout；poll超预算保留intent并exit7。poll slots含failure/proof item且最多64，预算不足即exit7。

worker先重验七leaf instance。rust-tests与transaction-tests各创建fixed 32对AF_INET numeric 127.0.0.1 connected channel：每对listener port0→connect→accept后验证四端互为同一loopback tuple，按pairIndex把server/client依次dup2到FD `198+2*i`/`199+2*i`，把same-instance empty `loopback-usage` leaf dup到FD197，关闭listener与原FD；其余command不得创建channel且usage leaf必须保持empty，启动时RLIMIT_NOFILE不足262直接pre-root transport failure。preconnectedLoopbackFingerprint=F(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-loopback-pool.v1",family:"AF_INET",baseFd:198,pairCount:32,ledgerFd:197,channels}`)，channels按pairIndex排序，每项exact=`pairIndex,serverFd,clientFd,serverLocalPort,serverPeerPort,clientLocalPort,clientPeerPort`，端口关系必须serverLocal=clientPeer且serverPeer=clientLocal，均1...65535。worker随后写/fsync handshake；handshake required exact=`schemaVersion,attemptId,labelDigest,pid,startSec,startUsec,pgid,executableIdentitySha256,ioAuthoritySha256,startedMonotonicNs,deadlineMonotonicNs,bootSessionIdentitySha256,preconnectedLoopbackFingerprint`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-command-handshake.v1`。attempt与三项time/boot facts来自strict worker argv并必须逐值等于planned transport/currentCommand，deadline-start exact；写入预绑定handshake leaf并自SIGSTOP。coordinator以loaded print pid、handshake与libproc三方same identity、attempt及三项authority确认；handshakeSha256与rootRefIdentitySha256都等于canonical handshake bytes的bare SHA256，不带`sha256:`。此时才以dynamic pool fingerprint生成final transportFingerprint并写rootedStopped；再次确认stopped后写resumeIntent，调用launchctl kill CONT，再证明same rootRef running才写running。

Phase3给Rust `spawn_webview_fixture`与Python synthetic adapter各加仅在exact distribution env存在时启用的pool allocator：base/count/ledger固定198/32/197。每个runtime process在首次allocate前创建唯一process-local mutex/actor；每次领取固定按`process-local lock → FD197 exclusive fcntl record lock → ledger verify → pwrite → fsync → fcntl unlock → process-local unlock`执行，因此同进程thread/task与跨进程caller都互斥。`fcntl` lock/unlock与ledger I/O的EINTR只在whole-command deadline内重试；mutex poison/cancellation、lock/unlock errno或deadline一律GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED，绝不绕过第二层锁。allocator记录initializing PID并注册atfork child reset；fork child首次allocate必须先重建为unlocked local mutex且确认没有继承owner state，再取得自己的fcntl lock，无法证明则拒绝，禁止使用复制自parent的locked mutex。持有双锁时重验ledger为0...96 bytes且exact等于`00\n01\n...<n-1两位十进制>\n`，再以EINTR-safe单次3-byte `pwrite`写exact offset=`3*n`并fsync；short write不得重试成第二record而是GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED，partial/duplicate/gap/size>96拒绝。unlock失败时已durable的record仍视为consumed、不得复用；后继caller只能按prefix继续。随后从对应两pool FD dup出own server/client handle，禁止socket/bind/listen/connect。未设置env时现有真实loopback行为逐byte不变。rust-tests与transaction-tests两command都使用preconnectedLoopback role及三个pool env，其他gate不得出现这些key。worker先waitpid direct child并证明除same rootRef worker自身外的same-PGID descendant census=0，再以same instance重读ledger、关闭自己的64个pool originals/FD197，令`loopbackChannelsConsumed=record count`；下段最终teardown才要求整个managed PGID=0。任一未关闭/重复/越界/ledger wrong-prefix为GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED。

inner spawn contract不创建第二process group：worker以`posix_spawn`启动唯一direct child，spawn attr明确不含`POSIX_SPAWN_SETPGROUP|POSIX_SPAWN_SETSID`等改变session/group的flag；spawn返回后、读取任一command byte前必须用libproc绑定direct child `(pid,startSec,startUsec,parentPid=workerPid,pgid=workerPgid)`，不满足即transportFailure。worker在每轮read/wait后都以bounded libproc refresh记录same-PGID成员与已观察descendant identity；inner及后代只能继承same worker PGID。direct child或任一已观察descendant的PGID/session/ancestry变化是`setpgid|setsid|detach`的可观察判定：立即停止生成result，worker退出非零，coordinator按rootRef执行transportFailure teardown；若detached target不能证明absent则RECOVERY_REQUIRED，不把它伪装成可自动清理。worker自身始终保留为该PGID的root/result writer，任何inner cleanup都必须显式排除worker `(pid,start identity)`，禁止对整个PGID调用killpg或等价group signal。

inner command完成后worker不自然exit：先close/fsync command output leaves，再按下文result arbitration把≤64KiB canonical ASCII JSON+LF写入same prebound result instance，最后自SIGSTOP等待coordinator teardown。commandWorkerResult required exact=`schemaVersion,attemptId,gateIndex,commandIndex,completedMonotonicNs,terminationClass,exitCode,signal,spawnErrno,stdoutBytes,stderrBytes,stdoutSha256,stderrSha256,stdoutTruncated,stderrTruncated,preconnectedLoopbackFingerprint,loopbackUsageSha256,loopbackChannelsConsumed,loopbackChannelsClosed`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-command-worker-result.v1`。termination oneOf：success→exitCode0/signal+errno null；exit→exitCode1...255/signal+errno null；signal→signal1...127/exitCode+errno null；spawnFailure→spawnErrno1...4095/exitCode+signal null；overflow→exitCode/signal/errno null且至少一项truncated=true。非overflow的completedMonotonicNs必须在worker已取得result lock后采样且`startedMonotonicNs<=completedMonotonicNs<deadlineMonotonicNs`；overflow则要求`observedMonotonicNs<=completedMonotonicNs<=overflowCleanupDeadlineMonotonicNs`。stdout/stderr bytes必须等于retained leaf size且不超各cap，SHA等于same-instance raw bytes；truncated只在已经观测并丢弃第cap+1 byte时true，exact cap后EOF仍为false。

result first-trigger使用same-instance leaf byte-range `[0,1)` 的process-shared `fcntl(F_SETLK)` exclusive record lock线性化；worker与coordinator各只持一个result FD，持锁期间禁止open/dup/close任何指向该inode的其他FD，所有验证用locked FD上的`fstat/pread`，unlock后才可从root FD重开验证identity。worker写任一normal result或overflow marker前必须先取得该锁；coordinator到普通deadline也竞争同一锁，最多等到`deadlineMonotonicNs+5_000_000_000`。无法取得、lock owner异常、EINTR预算耗尽或same-instance漂移都锁transportFailure/resultArbitrationFailed，0 receipt；root teardown后再按locked FD grammar取证。normal worker只有在锁内result仍empty且fresh monotonic `<deadline`时，才采样completedMonotonicNs并append full result、fsync、locked-FD pread逐byte重验；若已到deadline或看到timeout fence则0 append并自SIGSTOP等待teardown。

coordinator取得锁后只接受closed parse。若result empty且fresh monotonic `>=deadline`，它在仍持锁时先append canonical `timeoutFence` JSON+LF、fsync/pread重验，再发布同一currentCommand revision的`timeoutFenceSha256+terminationIntent=timeout`，journal/generation fsync成功后才unlock；crash在fence fsync→journal之间由recovery读取fence补同一timeout intent。timeoutFence required exact=`schemaVersion,attemptId,gateIndex,commandIndex,bootSessionIdentitySha256,deadlineMonotonicNs,classifiedMonotonicNs`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-timeout-fence.v1`，字段逐值绑定current command且classifiedMonotonicNs>=deadline；其SHA是canonical JSON bytes不含LF的bare SHA。worker之后即使曾raw-read cap+1，也必须在lock内看到fence并0 append，因此timeout intent永不与late marker/result并存。若锁内已是valid full normal result且completedMonotonicNs<deadline，则该result durable record先赢；若是valid overflow marker则marker先赢；partial/unknown/late-normal-result均为transportFailure。worker与coordinator谁先取得锁是唯一linearization point，不以事后wall-clock猜测改写winner。

cap+1的durable first-trigger复用既有result leaf而不增加leaf/cleanup cursor。worker读到首个cap+1 byte后先原子latch `overflowTeardown=true`、从此禁止spawn/接受新bytes；raw observation本身不具跨进程authority。worker立即竞争result lock，只有在锁内result仍empty、fresh monotonic `<deadline`且raw observed也`<deadline`时才fsync两output leaves并在result offset0 append canonical `overflowObserved` JSON+LF；`observed==deadline`或更晚必须0 marker并让coordinator timeout fence获胜。marker required exact=`schemaVersion,attemptId,gateIndex,commandIndex,bootSessionIdentitySha256,firstOverflowStream,observedMonotonicNs,stdoutBytes,stderrBytes,stdoutSha256,stderrSha256`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-overflow-observed.v1`，firstOverflowStream=`stdout|stderr`（同一poll同时ready时固定stdout先），observedMonotonicNs必须`started<=observed<deadline`，bootSession逐值等于current command，触发stream bytes exact等于其cap、另一stream为0...cap，SHA/bytes逐值等于same output leaves。marker canonical JSON≤64KiB；worker必须在同一lock内EINTR-safe full append、fsync并locked-FD pread逐byte重验后才unlock并允许任何inner signal。`overflowObservedSha256`是marker canonical JSON bytes（不含唯一transport LF）的bare SHA。若coordinator先写timeout fence，worker在lock内只读fence并0 append/自SIGSTOP；cap+1 raw-read→deschedule→late-lock因此稳定归timeout，而不是产生非法late marker。result leaf是append-only、不得truncate/overwrite，总cap128KiB；closed grammar仅为empty、单行normal commandWorkerResult strict prefix/full、单行timeout fence full，或overflow marker strict prefix/full后紧接第二行overflow commandWorkerResult strict prefix/full。marker/fence partial、extra/reorder/duplicate、marker后非overflow result均为transportFailure。full marker完成fsync+locked-FD验证并unlock这一点就是worker-side inner teardown的唯一durable write-ahead boundary；从该点起worker可在0 coordinator journal ack下执行下段identity-bound member signals，marker之前0 signal。marker durable→first SIGSTOP/TERM/KILL每个crash点都由same marker恢复为pending overflow cleanup或transportFailure，不能回退timeout；该例外不授权launchctl、bootout、unlink或其他coordinator side effect。

marker durable后，derived `overflowCleanupDeadlineMonotonicNs=observedMonotonicNs+30_000_000_000`，同boot session下可有界超过普通command deadline且不入DTO；worker按上段marker intent立即先向same direct-child identity发SIGSTOP，无需等待journal ack。coordinator在deadline arbitration锁内或恢复时看到valid full marker，必须先把overflowObservedSha256与run infra/error持久化，绝不生成timeout receipt，并在该journal fsync前0 coordinator teardown side effect；worker再用libproc在4096-member cap和该overflow cleanup deadline内反复枚举`same PGID + recorded/当前ancestry`成员，逐一重验`pid/start/ppid/pgid`并SIGSTOP，直到两次fresh census roster逐byte相同且所有inner成员stopped。随后保持direct child root stopped，按descendant leaves-first分wave逐identity发送TERM+CONT，任何fork-race新生descendant立即重新freeze并先处理，每wave共享250ms grace后仍存活者逐identity KILL，最后处理direct child并由worker唯一waitpid reap。每次signal前后都重验start identity；ESRCH只在fresh identity proof为absent时接受。最终必须两次独立fresh census都只剩显式排除的same-identity worker、direct child已reap、pipes达到EOF并完成bounded drain/closure、pool command的ledger closed且64 endpoint holder empty，才可重新取得result lock，确认首行仍为same marker且无第二行，采样completedMonotonicNs≤overflow cleanup deadline，append full overflow result、fsync/pread重验、unlock并自SIGSTOP；期间cap/cleanup deadline/identity/ancestry/PGID漂移、escaped target、worker异常或marker后缺full result都令coordinator锁terminationIntent=transportFailure/reason=overflowCleanupFailed（detached residual则RECOVERY_REQUIRED），0 overflow receipt。该流程从不group-signal仍需写result的worker；没有marker+full result不得构造overflow receipt。loopback fingerprint与usage raw bytes bare SHA仅rust/transaction non-null、consumed等于strict ledger record count 0...32、closed=true，其他四项分别null/null/0/null。worker-stdout/worker-stderr始终same instance,size0,SHA256(empty)；任何byte都是GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED。

result leaf active时只允许same instance的上述closed append grammar；full result必须与output leaves、wait status、pool closure交叉一致才写currentCommand.workerResultSha256，并且libproc仍证明worker以same rootRef处于SIGSTOP。partial/malformed/result full但worker非stopped、wrapper log nonempty、output size/SHA/truncation不符都不构造receipt：先按rootRef teardown，锁run infra/GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED。timeout要求result恰为single full timeout fence、timeoutFenceSha256 non-null而overflowObservedSha256/workerResultSha256均null；empty只允许deadline前或尚未完成arbitration，任何nonempty incomplete record是transportFailure，不能派生timeout receipt。timeout receipt由journal intent+bounded output leaves派生且truncated两项false；overflow必须有durable marker+full overflow result，success/exit/signal/spawnFailure必须有单行full worker result。preparedCommandReceipt必须逐字段等于该branch、final transport、managed-job evidence与output facts。

terminationIntent在首个trigger前exactly-once为`overflow|timeout|workerResult|transportFailure`；overflow必须已有durable marker+full verified overflow workerResult并同时持久化两个SHA，timeout必须已有durable timeout fence及其SHA，transportFailure必须有closed reason且永不产receipt，inner spawn失败属于full workerResult的spawnFailure，不是pre-root failure；bootstrap/handshake/rootRef前失败走preRootCleanupIntent。若恢复时尚无intent，deterministic precedence由result lock的first durable record而非unlocked read决定：invalid/partial grammar→transportFailure；valid overflow marker→持久化marker SHA并等待30秒cleanup（full后锁overflow，失败锁transportFailure/overflowCleanupFailed）；valid normal full result且completed<deadline→workerResult；deadline时empty→同lock写timeout fence并锁timeout。raw cap observation、empty pre-lock read或lock外wall clock都没有authority。已有terminationIntent永不改写；fence fsync后journal前crash只补同一intent。bootSessionIdentity变化使clock/lock tuple不可比较时RECOVERY_REQUIRED。worker按正常合同绝不自行exit：若prior rootRef已绑定而loaded print无current pid/libproc root absent，则写transportFailure/unexpectedDeath，按rooted 0-receipt teardown+cleanup闭环为GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED，不能伪装workerResult success。

超时/cap先journal term intent再`launchctl kill TERM`，5s后仍有same rootRef/process-group成员才journal+KILL，之后先bootoutIntent再bootout。teardownEvidence exact required=`schemaVersion,labelDigest,rootRefIdentitySha256,terminationIntent,termSent,killSent,bootoutCompleted,labelAbsent,rootPidAbsent,processGroupId,processGroupMembers,outputWriterCount,preconnectedLoopbackClosed`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-teardown-evidence.v1`；members按`pid,startSec,startUsec,executableIdentitySha256`排序，terminal evidence必须empty、outputWriterCount=0，loopback字段对rust-tests/transaction-tests为true、其他const null。当前目标macOS label-absent oracle锁为`launchctl print gui/<uid>/<label>` exit113、stdout empty、stderr exact两行grammar=`Bad request.\nCould not find service "<label>" in domain for user gui: <uid>\n`；其他exit/channel/text都不是absent证据。terminal FD oracle在label/root absent后执行两次fresh bounded libproc scan：`proc_listallpids`最多131072项，每PID先验证current uid/start identity，再以`PROC_PIDLISTFDS`最多4096项和vnode info匹配plist/七leaf recorded dev+ino；任一permission/cap/identity drift为RECOVERY_REQUIRED。两遍writer tuple `(pid,startSec,startUsec,fd,role)`须逐byte相等且empty，outputWriterCount由该tuple count派生；同一扫描还要求same PGID成员和所有已观察root descendant均empty。只有该oracle、七leaf closed且pool command的64 endpoint/dup无持有者，才能写receipt。自动清理合同只覆盖launchd service的same process group及未改PGID descendants；主动setsid/setpgid逃逸不在支持行为内，扫描发现detached residual必须RECOVERY_REQUIRED+人工清理。普通waitpid只负责worker direct child，不虚称reap grandchild。

pool socket closure不靠vnode oracle推断：两遍各自fresh枚举current-uid PID，并对其中每个socket FD调用`PROC_PIDFDSOCKETINFO`，单PID仍受4096 FD cap；只接受AF_INET/SOCK_STREAM/TCP事实，并把local/peer address+port canonical化后与preconnectedLoopbackFingerprint的64 endpoint tuple逐项匹配。全局PID roster不要求逐byte相同：两遍之间新增/消失的无关PID只要start identity校验成立且从未匹配受管inode/endpoint即可忽略；`ESRCH`只在重查PID start identity已absent时允许跳过。EPERM/EACCES、unsupported kind、cap，或受管writer/socket-holder/same-PGID/observed-descendant target tuple两遍漂移均RECOVERY_REQUIRED。terminal socket-holder tuple `(pid,startSec,startUsec,fd,pairIndex,side)`必须两遍相同且empty。full-result branch还必须同时满足commandWorkerResult.loopbackChannelsClosed=true；timeout或early rooted transportFailure没有full result时，则由label/root/PGID absent、64 endpoint holder与FD writer双遍empty直接派生closed。两种branch才可写teardownEvidence.preconnectedLoopbackClosed=true；timeout可继续生成其closed receipt，transportFailure仍为0 receipt。非pool command不得运行该匹配且字段null。

pre-root failure必须在同一revision先锁run outcome=infra/terminalError=GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED并写preRootCleanupIntent，之后才允许任何launchctl/FD/unlink cleanup side effect。preRootCleanupEvidence required exact=`schemaVersion,labelDigest,bootoutState,bootoutAttempted,labelAbsent,workerLeavesClosed,jobScratchCleaned`、additionalProperties=false，schema const=`harvis.distribution-pre-root-cleanup-evidence.v1`；刚进入preRoot、fresh print尚无结果时可为null。其closed oneOf为：`notNeeded`→bootoutAttempted=false,labelAbsent=true；`intent`→bootoutAttempted=true,labelAbsent=false,workerLeavesClosed=false,jobScratchCleaned=false；`completed`→bootoutAttempted=true,labelAbsent=true。workerLeavesClosed/jobScratchCleaned在notNeeded|completed内各为false→true单调前缀，jobScratchCleaned必须与currentCommand同名bool相等且只在cleanup cursor9/root absent时true。若尚无bootout intent的fresh print得到recoveryAbsent，原子写notNeeded；若得到loadedActive或允许的loadedNoPid，必须先写intent再调用bootout。intent恢复按transcript prefix唯一分支：尚无bootout successEmpty时，loaded则向same label重做bootout，absent则先暂存actual result、按历史顺序补successEmpty bootout item并把该actual exit113追加为terminalAbsent；已有bootout successEmpty时绝不重做bootout，只允许追加最多8项post-bootout loaded poll或最终terminalAbsent。bootout正常返回并持久化successEmpty后仍保持intent,labelAbsent=false；只有terminalAbsent proof与labelAbsent=true同一revision才转completed，任何路径都不得生成第二个bootout或把该proof标recoveryAbsent。workerLeavesClosed只在bounded process/FD/socket oracle通过后true；evidence为null/intent、labelAbsent=false或workerLeavesClosed=false时jobCleanupCursor必须0且jobScratchCleaned=false，禁止unlink/rmdir。只有notNeeded/completed且labelAbsent/workerLeavesClosed=true后才可按identity roster推进cursor0...9；cursor9同时要求root absent并把evidence/current的jobScratchCleaned原子置true，随后才能进入preRootCleaned。preRootCleaned只允许notNeeded|completed、labelAbsent/workerLeavesClosed/jobScratchCleaned全true。随后只清current并继续infra scratch cleanup，不生成managedJobFingerprint或commandReceipt；cleanup失败不改原error。不确定就保持run并返回exit7。

输出grammar统一为cap内任意opaque bytes，raw bytes在scratch cleanup前只用于SHA并不进artifact。commandReceipt exact required=commandIndex,argvFingerprint,transportFingerprint,managedJobFingerprint,terminationClass,exitCode,signal,stdoutSha256,stderrSha256,stdoutTruncated,stderrTruncated，additionalProperties=false；每项transportFingerprint必须对应同commandIndex的独立argv/env/sandbox parameter mapping。managedJobFingerprint=F(canonical exact `{schemaVersion:"harvis.distribution-managed-job.v1",plistSha256,launchctlTranscriptFingerprint,labelDigest,rootRefIdentitySha256,overflowObservedSha256,timeoutFenceSha256,workerResultSha256,teardownEvidenceSha256}`)：overflow为marker/result SHA non-null且timeout null，其他full-result branch仅result non-null，timeout仅fence non-null；所有commandReceipt的rootRef与teardown都non-null，teardownEvidenceSha256是exact DTO canonical bytes bare SHA。termination oneOf严格按journaled first-trigger区分success(exit0)、exit(nonzero)、timeout(exitCode/signal null且truncated两项false)、signal(signal non-null)、overflow(truncated至少一项true)、spawnFailure；spawnFailure仅对应rooted full `commandWorkerResult.terminationClass=spawnFailure`且terminationIntent=workerResult，pre-root失败0 receipt。gateReceipt exact required=gateId,commandReceipts，additionalProperties=false；普通gate恰1项，rust-release-build和bundle-package为表内commandIndex 0...1 ordered nonempty prefix：success gate必须恰2项全success，任一command non-success即停止且不得出现后项；@PROVENANCE_SHA只由bundle commandIndex=0 success产生。rust vendor commandIndex=0任何non-success仍是infra；bundle commandIndex=0 normal exit可作为只有1 commandReceipt的trusted package failure。禁止把真实exit124当timeout或exit130当signal。

**Verification artifact 与distribution result。** verification-result.json exact required=schemaVersion,generation,head,tree,outcome,failedGateId,reason,gateReceipts,gateRosterFingerprint,toolchainAuthorityFingerprint,candidateReceiptSha256,candidateAppTreeSha256,candidateRecordSha256,candidateIdentitySha256,signingMode,commandsFingerprint,evidenceFingerprint，schema const harvis.source-checkpoint-verification.v1、additionalProperties=false。passed branch：outcome=passed，failedGateId/reason=null，gateReceipts恰为9项ordered roster且每个nested commandReceipt=success；candidate四SHA non-null并与fixed promoted candidate exact，signingMode=adHocLocalCandidate。failed branch只允许首个产品gate命令以terminationClass=exit、exitCode=1...255、signal=null、truncated两项false结束；failedGateId与derived build|test|package non-null，receipts恰为gate1到该gate且末command为此normal exit；该末gate的commandReceipts是表中截至失败command的ordered prefix，candidate四SHA/signingMode全null。rust-release-build的vendor command任何非success、以及任意gate的timeout/signal/overflow/spawnFailure都属于infra result，0 verification、0 disposition；source/toolchain/sandbox/materialize/promotion/cleanup failure同样0 consumable artifact。commandsFingerprint严格使用上文exact payload；evidenceFingerprint=`F`作用于完整verification object excluding evidenceFingerprint。artifact write-once；已存在时canonical bytes必须exact才幂等，绝不替换同代结果；distributionRun非null时base/next均不可消费。

distribution stdout唯一canonical ASCII JSON+LF、stderr empty。result schema const=harvis.source-checkpoint-distribution-result.v1，required exact=schemaVersion,command,ok,retryable,generation,head,tree,outcome,disposition,verificationEvidenceFingerprint,candidateReceiptSha256,candidateAppTreeSha256,candidateRecordSha256,candidateIdentitySha256,error，additionalProperties=false，以下oneOf必须match-count=1：(a) passed exit0：command=verifyDistribution,ok=true,retryable=false，generation/head/tree non-null，outcome=passed,disposition=pending，evidence与candidate四SHA non-null，error=null；(b) trusted gate failure exit6：command=verifyDistribution,ok=false,retryable=false，generation/head/tree non-null，outcome=failed,disposition=rejectedPreDeployment，evidence non-null、candidate四SHA null，error exact {code:GATE_FAILED,stage:gate}；(c) usage exit2：command=unknown，其余facts全null，error=USAGE+usage；(d) validation/infra exit3：known command，其余facts全null，error code→stage只允许AUTHORITY_INVALID→authority、SOURCE_CHANGED→source、TOOLCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE→toolchain、SANDBOX_UNAVAILABLE→sandbox、MATERIALIZATION_FAILED→materialize、GATE_TRANSPORT_FAILED→gateTransport、CANDIDATE_PROMOTION_FAILED→promotion；(e) concurrent exit5：retryable=true、其余facts全null、CONCURRENT_TRANSACTION→lock；(f) recovery exit7：retryable=false、其余facts全null、RECOVERY_REQUIRED→artifact|journal|cleanup；(g) internal exit9：其余facts全null、INTERNAL_FAILURE→internal。除(e)外retryable=false。

`--retry-infra-fingerprint`若格式合法且当前确为terminal infra、但值不等于journal中的exact fingerprint，必须0 write返回(f)：command=verifyDistribution、error=`{code:RECOVERY_REQUIRED,stage:journal}`、exit7；格式错误或当前并非terminal infra则返回(c) usage exit2。两者均不得泄漏current fingerprint。exact fingerprint才允许以新run intent替换tombstone。

passed artifact发布、source journal仍pending且distributionRun已清null后才输出(a)；gate failed时同source lock先发布artifact、推进rejectedPreDeployment、清distributionRun，全部fsync后才输出(b)。artifact已rename但journal或disposition crash时无terminal stdout，下一次`verifyDistribution`只可按exact tuple幂等补transition；`status`仅报告RECOVERY_REQUIRED且0 write，public reject/plan/prepare在run非null时必须拒绝且0消费。planSnapshot.sourceCheckpoint与apply Approval的最终keys在本段再追加candidateRecordSha256,candidateIdentitySha256,signingMode；三者来自fixed candidate/verification，signingMode const=adHocLocalCandidate。plan只接受current generation、distributionRun=null、passed artifact且candidate receipt/app-tree/record/identity逐项相等；reject只接受distributionRun=null的failed artifact。所有failure不得回显raw path/output。
